# Implementação integral dos builders legados do baseline E0.
# NENHUMA FÓRMULA OU NOME DE COLUNA É ALTERADO.

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List

META_COLS: List[str] = [
    "datetime", "LogHost", "ProcessName", "UserName", "SourceIP",
    "UserID", "Time", "session_id", "AnomalyType", "EventID", "hour",
    "IpAddress", "SubStatus", "AuthenticationPackageName",
    "date", "new_session", "time_diff", "sub_session_id", "chunk_id",
    "day_of_week", "time_sin", "time_cos", "is_weekend",
    "Is_Anomaly", "_ord",
    # Intermediários de cálculo em E0 (11 helpers não numéricos no vetor final)
    "is_failed", "is_success", "bad_password", "bad_username",
    "account_locked", "rdp_event", "admin_activity",
    "network_logon", "is_kerberos_logon", "recent_4768",
    "rare_process",
]


def rolling_rate(df: pd.DataFrame, source_col: str, rate_window: int = 20) -> pd.Series:
    """
    Fração de eventos do tipo `source_col` na janela dos últimos
    `rate_window` eventos por usuário. Inclui o evento atual (estado no instante t).
    """
    if df.empty or source_col not in df.columns or "UserID" not in df.columns:
        return pd.Series(0.0, index=df.index, dtype=float)
    return df.groupby("UserID", sort=False)[source_col].transform(
        lambda v: v.rolling(rate_window, min_periods=1).mean()
    )


def time_window_count(df: pd.DataFrame, event_col: str, window_seconds: float, exclusive: bool = False) -> pd.Series:
    """
    Conta eventos com event_col == 1 nos `window_seconds` anteriores a cada
    evento, por usuário. Estritamente causal.
    `exclusive=True` exclui o próprio evento atual.
    """
    out = pd.Series(0.0, index=df.index, dtype=float)
    if df.empty or event_col not in df.columns or "Time" not in df.columns or "UserID" not in df.columns:
        return out

    for _, idx in df.groupby("UserID", sort=False).groups.items():
        sub = df.loc[idx, ["Time", event_col]].sort_values("Time", kind="stable")
        times = sub["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        vals = sub[event_col].to_numpy(dtype=float)

        cumsum = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(vals)])
        left = np.searchsorted(times, times - window_seconds, side="left")
        sums = cumsum[np.arange(len(times)) + 1] - cumsum[left]

        if exclusive:
            sums = sums - vals

        out.loc[sub.index] = sums

    return out


def fit_legacy_state(df_normal: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Ajusta os baselines históricos estatísticos e frequências relativas
    utilizando APENAS eventos normais (Is_Anomaly == 0).
    """
    state: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if df_normal.empty or "Time" not in df_normal.columns or "UserID" not in df_normal.columns:
        return state

    df_temp = df_normal.copy()
    df_temp["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(df_temp["Time"], unit="s")
    user_counts = df_temp.groupby("UserID", sort=False).size()

    # 1. Frequências relativas por usuário
    if "LogHost" in df_temp.columns:
        state["loghost_freq"] = df_temp.groupby(["UserID", "LogHost"]).size() / user_counts
        state["user_hosts_seen"] = {
            str(uid): set(hosts.dropna().astype(str))
            for uid, hosts in df_temp.groupby("UserID", sort=False)["LogHost"]
        }

    if "ProcessName" in df_temp.columns:
        state["process_freq"] = df_temp.groupby(["UserID", "ProcessName"]).size() / user_counts

    if "IpAddress" in df_temp.columns:
        state["ip_freq"] = df_temp.groupby(["UserID", "IpAddress"]).size() / user_counts

    if "SubStatus" in df_temp.columns:
        state["substatus_freq"] = df_temp.groupby(["UserID", "SubStatus"]).size() / user_counts

    if "AuthenticationPackageName" in df_temp.columns:
        state["authpkg_freq"] = df_temp.groupby(["UserID", "AuthenticationPackageName"]).size() / user_counts

    # 2. Baseline temporal por usuário
    df_temp["hour"] = df_temp["datetime"].dt.hour
    state["hour_mean"] = df_temp.groupby("UserID")["hour"].mean()
    state["hour_std"] = df_temp.groupby("UserID")["hour"].std()

    # 3. Baseline de volume diário por usuário
    df_temp["date"] = df_temp["datetime"].dt.date
    daily_counts = df_temp.groupby(["UserID", "date"]).size()
    state["daily_volume_mean"] = daily_counts.groupby("UserID").mean()
    state["daily_volume_std"] = daily_counts.groupby("UserID").std()

    return state


def apply_frequency_encoders(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Aplica frequency encoders aprendidos no fit."""
    encoders = [
        ("LogHost", "loghost_freq", "LogHost_enc"),
        ("ProcessName", "process_freq", "ProcessName_enc"),
        ("IpAddress", "ip_freq", "IpAddress_enc"),
        ("SubStatus", "substatus_freq", "SubStatus_enc"),
        ("AuthenticationPackageName", "authpkg_freq", "AuthPackage_enc"),
    ]
    for col, state_key, enc_col in encoders:
        if col in df.columns and state_key in historical_state:
            freq_map = historical_state[state_key].reset_index(name=enc_col)
            df["_ord"] = range(len(df))
            df = df.merge(freq_map, on=["UserID", col], how="left")
            df = df.sort_values("_ord", kind="stable").drop(columns=["_ord"]).reset_index(drop=True)
            df[enc_col] = df[enc_col].fillna(0.0)

    if "_ord" in df.columns:
        df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["_ord"]).reset_index(drop=True)
    return df


def build_temporal(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói desvios e z-score de horário."""
    df["hour"] = df["datetime"].dt.hour
    if "hour_mean" in historical_state:
        mean = df["UserID"].map(historical_state["hour_mean"])
        std = df["UserID"].map(historical_state["hour_std"]).fillna(3.0).clip(lower=0.5)

        raw = (df["hour"] - mean).abs()
        df["hour_dev"] = np.minimum(raw, 24 - raw).fillna(0)  # distância circular
        df["hour_z"] = (df["hour_dev"] / std).fillna(0)
        df["out_of_hours"] = (df["hour_z"] > 2.0).astype(int)
    else:
        df["out_of_hours"] = ((df["hour"] < 7) | (df["hour"] > 21)).astype(int)
        df["hour_dev"] = 0.0
        df["hour_z"] = 0.0
    return df


def build_auth_failure(df: pd.DataFrame, rate_window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói contadores causais e taxas de falha de autenticação."""
    df["is_failed"] = (df["EventID"] == 4625).astype(int)
    df["is_success"] = (df["EventID"] == 4624).astype(int)

    df["fail_rate"] = rolling_rate(df, "is_failed", rate_window)
    df["fail_count_5m"] = time_window_count(df, "is_failed", 300)
    df["fail_count_15m"] = time_window_count(df, "is_failed", 900)
    df["fail_count_60m"] = time_window_count(df, "is_failed", 3600)

    prev_fails = time_window_count(df, "is_failed", 300, exclusive=True)
    df["fail_then_success"] = ((df["is_success"] == 1) & (prev_fails > 2)).astype(int)

    if "SubStatus" in df.columns:
        sub = df["SubStatus"].astype(str).str.upper()
        df["bad_password"] = ((df["EventID"] == 4625) & sub.str.contains("0XC000006A")).astype(int)
        df["bad_username"] = ((df["EventID"] == 4625) & sub.str.contains("0XC0000064")).astype(int)
        df["account_locked"] = ((df["EventID"] == 4625) & sub.str.contains("0XC0000234")).astype(int)

        df["bad_pwd_rate"] = rolling_rate(df, "bad_password", rate_window)
        df["bad_pwd_count_15m"] = time_window_count(df, "bad_password", 900)

        df["lock_rate"] = rolling_rate(df, "account_locked", rate_window)
        df["lock_count_60m"] = time_window_count(df, "account_locked", 3600)

    return df


def build_lateral(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any], rate_window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói features de movimentação lateral e troca de host/IP."""
    if "LogHost" in df.columns:
        prev_host = df.groupby("UserID", sort=False)["LogHost"].shift()
        df["host_changed"] = (
            prev_host.notna() & (df["LogHost"].astype(str) != prev_host.astype(str))
        ).astype(int)

        def _cumnunique(x):
            seen, out = set(), []
            for v in x:
                seen.add(v)
                out.append(len(seen))
            return out

        df["unique_hosts"] = df.groupby("UserID", sort=False)["LogHost"].transform(_cumnunique)

        if "user_hosts_seen" in historical_state:
            seen_map = historical_state["user_hosts_seen"]
            df["user_host_new"] = [
                0 if str(h) in seen_map.get(str(u), set()) else 1
                for u, h in zip(df["UserID"], df["LogHost"])
            ]

    if "IpAddress" in df.columns:
        prev_ip = df.groupby("UserID", sort=False)["IpAddress"].shift()
        df["ip_changed"] = (
            prev_ip.notna() & (df["IpAddress"].astype(str) != prev_ip.astype(str))
        ).astype(int)

        def _cumnunique_ip(x):
            seen, out = set(), []
            for v in x:
                seen.add(v)
                out.append(len(seen))
            return out

        df["unique_ips"] = df.groupby("UserID", sort=False)["IpAddress"].transform(_cumnunique_ip)

    if "LogonType" in df.columns:
        df["network_logon"] = (
            ((df["EventID"] == 4624) & (df["LogonType"] == 3))
            | (df["EventID"] == 4648)
        ).astype(int)
        df["network_logon_rate"] = rolling_rate(df, "network_logon", rate_window)
        df["network_logon_count_60m"] = time_window_count(df, "network_logon", 3600)

    return df


def build_volume(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói features de volume diário e anomalia volumétrica."""
    if "date" in df.columns:
        df["event_count"] = df.groupby(["UserID", "date"], sort=False).cumcount() + 1
        if "daily_volume_mean" in historical_state:
            mean_map = historical_state["daily_volume_mean"]
            std_map = historical_state["daily_volume_std"]
            df["vol_mean"] = df["UserID"].map(mean_map).fillna(0)
            df["vol_std"] = df["UserID"].map(std_map).fillna(1)
            df["event_z"] = ((df["event_count"] - df["vol_mean"]) / (df["vol_std"] + 1e-6)).fillna(0)
            df.drop(columns=["vol_mean", "vol_std"], inplace=True, errors="ignore")
        else:
            df["event_z"] = 0.0
    return df


def build_rdp(df: pd.DataFrame, rate_window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói features de acesso RDP."""
    if "LogonType" in df.columns:
        df["rdp_event"] = ((df["EventID"] == 4624) & (df["LogonType"] == 10)).astype(int)
    else:
        df["rdp_event"] = (df["EventID"] == 4624).astype(int)

    df["rdp_rate"] = rolling_rate(df, "rdp_event", rate_window)
    df["rdp_count_60m"] = time_window_count(df, "rdp_event", 3600)
    return df


def build_golden_ticket(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói features de forja Kerberos e protocolo NTLM."""
    if "AuthenticationPackageName" in df.columns:
        pkg = df["AuthenticationPackageName"].astype(str).str.lower()
        df["is_kerberos_logon"] = (
            (df["EventID"] == 4624) & pkg.str.contains("kerberos")
        ).astype(int)
        df["is_ntlm"] = pkg.str.contains("ntlm").astype(int)

        if "EventID" in df.columns:
            df["recent_4768"] = time_window_count(
                df.assign(_evt4768=(df["EventID"] == 4768).astype(int)),
                "_evt4768", 600,
            )
            df.drop(columns=["_evt4768"], inplace=True, errors="ignore")
            df["kerberos_logon_without_tgt"] = (
                (df["is_kerberos_logon"] == 1) & (df["recent_4768"] == 0)
            ).astype(int)

    return df


def build_process(df: pd.DataFrame, rate_window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói features de raridade de processo."""
    if "ProcessName_enc" in df.columns:
        df["rare_process"] = (df["ProcessName_enc"] < 0.02).astype(int)
        df["rare_process_rate"] = rolling_rate(df, "rare_process", rate_window)
        df["rare_process_count_60m"] = time_window_count(df, "rare_process", 3600)
    return df


def build_privilege(df: pd.DataFrame, rate_window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói features de ferramentas administrativas privilegiadas."""
    if "ProcessName" in df.columns:
        proc_lower = df["ProcessName"].fillna("").astype(str).str.lower()
        admin_tools = [
            "powershell.exe", "cmd.exe", "wmic.exe", "ntdsutil.exe",
            "psexec.exe", "mmc.exe", "vssadmin.exe",
        ]
        df["admin_activity"] = proc_lower.isin(admin_tools).astype(int)
        df["admin_rate"] = rolling_rate(df, "admin_activity", rate_window)
        df["admin_count_60m"] = time_window_count(df, "admin_activity", 3600)
    return df


def add_interactions(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Constrói interações booleanas entre fora de horário e flags de eventos."""
    if "out_of_hours" not in df.columns:
        return df

    ooh = df["out_of_hours"].astype(int)
    for feat in ["admin_activity", "rdp_event", "is_success", "bad_password"]:
        if feat in df.columns:
            df[f"ooh_with_{feat}"] = ooh * df[feat].astype(int)

    return df


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\builders\pilar1_identity.py
################################################################################

# core/features/builders/pilar1_identity.py
#
# Pilar 1: Identidade, Sessão e Privilégios.

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List

SYSTEM_ACCOUNTS = {
    "SYSTEM", "LOCAL SERVICE", "NETWORK SERVICE", "ANONYMOUS LOGON",
    "NT AUTHORITY\\SYSTEM", "NT AUTHORITY\\LOCAL SERVICE", "NT AUTHORITY\\NETWORK SERVICE"
}


def build_pilar1_identity(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Constrói as features do Pilar 1:
    - entity_type (one-hot 4d: user, machine, service, system)
    - session_age_scaled + session_age_available
    - alternate_credentials_flag (EventID 4648)
    - privileged_token_flag (EventID 4672)
    - session_mismatch
    """
    if df.empty:
        return df

    # 1. entity_type (one-hot 4 dimensões)
    users = df["UserID"].astype(str)
    is_machine = users.str.endswith("$")
    is_system = users.str.upper().isin(SYSTEM_ACCOUNTS)
    is_service = users.str.lower().str.startswith(("svc_", "service_", "svc-", "svc.")) & (~is_machine)
    is_user = (~is_machine) & (~is_system) & (~is_service)

    df["entity_type_user"] = is_user.astype(float)
    df["entity_type_machine"] = is_machine.astype(float)
    df["entity_type_service"] = is_service.astype(float)
    df["entity_type_system"] = is_system.astype(float)

    # 2. session_age_scaled + session_age_available
    df["session_age_scaled"] = 0.0
    df["session_age_available"] = 0.0

    if "EventID" in df.columns and "Time" in df.columns:
        # Rastreia t_logon de 4624 por UserID
        for _, idx in df.groupby("UserID", sort=False).groups.items():
            sub = df.loc[idx, ["Time", "EventID"]]
            times = sub["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            evts = sub["EventID"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int64)

            last_logon = np.nan
            ages = np.zeros(len(times), dtype=float)
            avail = np.zeros(len(times), dtype=float)

            for i in range(len(times)):
                if evts[i] == 4624:
                    last_logon = times[i]
                if not np.isnan(last_logon) and times[i] >= last_logon:
                    age_sec = times[i] - last_logon
                    ages[i] = min(age_sec / 28800.0, 1.0)  # normalizado para 8 horas
                    avail[i] = 1.0

            df.loc[sub.index, "session_age_scaled"] = ages
            df.loc[sub.index, "session_age_available"] = avail

    # 3. alternate_credentials_flag
    if "EventID" in df.columns:
        df["alternate_credentials_flag"] = (df["EventID"] == 4648).astype(float)
        df["privileged_token_flag"] = (df["EventID"] == 4672).astype(float)
    else:
        df["alternate_credentials_flag"] = 0.0
        df["privileged_token_flag"] = 0.0

    # 4. session_mismatch
    if "LogonID" in df.columns and "UserID" in df.columns:
        # Se um LogonID for compartilhado por múltiplos UserIDs distintos
        user_by_logon = df.groupby("LogonID")["UserID"].transform("nunique")
        df["session_mismatch"] = (user_by_logon > 1).astype(float)
    else:
        df["session_mismatch"] = 0.0

    return df


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\builders\pilar2_process.py
################################################################################

# core/features/builders/pilar2_process.py
#
# Pilar 2: Processos e Linhagem.
# Correções aplicadas (Auditoria 01/11/2026):
# - Linhagem de processos com interpolação linear Jelinek-Mercer real (tripla, par, unigrama) via EM
# - lolbin_contextual estritamente causal: 1{lolbin} * process_surprisal (sem divisão por max global de batch)

import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Tuple

from core.features.estimators.jelinek_mercer import JelinekMercerEstimator

# Catálogo canônico versionado de Living-off-the-Land Binaries (LOLBins)
LOLBINS = {
    "certutil.exe", "bitsadmin.exe", "mshta.exe", "rundll32.exe", "regsvr32.exe",
    "cscript.exe", "wscript.exe", "wmic.exe", "powershell.exe", "pwsh.exe",
    "cmd.exe", "schtasks.exe", "at.exe", "vssadmin.exe", "nltest.exe",
    "dsquery.exe", "net.exe", "net1.exe", "psexec.exe", "esentutl.exe",
    "fodhelper.exe", "eventvwr.exe", "installutil.exe", "msbuild.exe"
}


def fit_pilar2_state(df_normal: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Ajusta tabelas de frequência de transição de processos e pesos JM no treino benigno."""
    state: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if df_normal.empty or "ProcessName" not in df_normal.columns:
        return state

    jm = JelinekMercerEstimator()
    procs = df_normal["ProcessName"].fillna("unknown").astype(str).str.lower()
    parents = (
        df_normal["ParentProcessName"].fillna("unknown").astype(str).str.lower()
        if "ParentProcessName" in df_normal.columns
        else None
    )
    grandparents = (
        df_normal["GrandparentProcessName"].fillna("unknown").astype(str).str.lower()
        if "GrandparentProcessName" in df_normal.columns
        else None
    )

    # Unigram counts
    proc_counts = procs.value_counts().to_dict()
    total_events = len(procs)
    vocab_size = max(len(proc_counts), 10)

    state["process_unigram_counts"] = proc_counts
    state["process_total_events"] = total_events
    state["process_vocab_size"] = vocab_size

    # Transição parent -> child e triplas (grandparent, parent, child)
    if parents is not None:
        if grandparents is None:
            # Reconstrói avô de forma sequencial causal
            gp_list: List[str] = []
            tracker: Dict[str, str] = {}
            for p, c in zip(parents, procs):
                gp = tracker.get(p, "unknown")
                gp_list.append(gp)
                tracker[c] = p
            gp_series = pd.Series(gp_list, index=df_normal.index)
        else:
            gp_series = grandparents

        pair_counts = df_normal.groupby([parents, procs]).size().to_dict()
        parent_totals = parents.value_counts().to_dict()

        triple_counts = df_normal.groupby([gp_series, parents, procs]).size().to_dict()
        pair_context_totals = df_normal.groupby([gp_series, parents]).size().to_dict()

        state["process_pair_counts"] = pair_counts
        state["process_parent_totals"] = parent_totals
        state["process_triple_counts"] = triple_counts
        state["process_pair_context_totals"] = pair_context_totals

        # Estimação dos pesos JM via EM
        sample_size = min(len(df_normal), 20000)
        p_triples = []
        p_pairs = []
        p_unigrams = []

        for idx in range(sample_size):
            g = gp_series.iloc[idx]
            p = parents.iloc[idx]
            c = procs.iloc[idx]

            t_cnt = triple_counts.get((g, p, c), 0)
            t_tot = pair_context_totals.get((g, p), 0)
            p_triples.append(jm.laplace_smooth_prob(t_cnt, t_tot, vocab_size))

            p_cnt = pair_counts.get((p, c), 0)
            p_tot = parent_totals.get(p, 0)
            p_pairs.append(jm.laplace_smooth_prob(p_cnt, p_tot, vocab_size))

            u_cnt = proc_counts.get(c, 0)
            p_unigrams.append(jm.laplace_smooth_prob(u_cnt, total_events, vocab_size))

        w_triple, w_pair, w_unigram = jm.estimate_weights_em(
            np.array(p_triples), np.array(p_pairs), np.array(p_unigrams)
        )
        state["process_jm_weights"] = (w_triple, w_pair, w_unigram)
    else:
        state["process_jm_weights"] = (0.0, 0.0, 1.0)

    return state


def build_pilar2_process(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    historical_state: Dict[str, Any] = None,
    alpha: float = 0.2
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Constrói as features do Pilar 2:
    - process_surprisal (Laplace em parent->child ou unigrama)
    - lineage_surprisal (Jelinek-Mercer multiescala com triplas e pesos EM)
    - lolbin_contextual (detecção de LOLBins com peso de surprisal sem leakage intra-batch)
    """
    if df.empty or "ProcessName" not in df.columns:
        return df

    state = historical_state or {}
    jm = JelinekMercerEstimator(laplace_alpha=alpha)

    procs = df["ProcessName"].fillna("unknown").astype(str).str.lower()
    parents = (
        df["ParentProcessName"].fillna("unknown").astype(str).str.lower()
        if "ParentProcessName" in df.columns
        else None
    )
    grandparents = (
        df["GrandparentProcessName"].fillna("unknown").astype(str).str.lower()
        if "GrandparentProcessName" in df.columns
        else None
    )

    pair_counts = state.get("process_pair_counts", {})
    parent_totals = state.get("process_parent_totals", {})
    triple_counts = state.get("process_triple_counts", {})
    pair_context_totals = state.get("process_pair_context_totals", {})
    unigram_counts = state.get("process_unigram_counts", {})
    total_events = state.get("process_total_events", len(df))
    vocab_size = max(state.get("process_vocab_size", 50), 10)
    w_triple, w_pair, w_unigram = state.get("process_jm_weights", (0.5, 0.3, 0.2))

    surp_vals = np.zeros(len(df), dtype=float)
    lineage_vals = np.zeros(len(df), dtype=float)

    parent_tracker: Dict[str, str] = {}

    for i in range(len(df)):
        proc = procs.iloc[i]
        u_cnt = unigram_counts.get(proc, 0)
        p_unigram = jm.laplace_smooth_prob(u_cnt, total_events, vocab_size)

        if parents is not None:
            parent = parents.iloc[i]
            p_cnt = pair_counts.get((parent, proc), 0)
            p_tot = parent_totals.get(parent, 0)
            p_pair = jm.laplace_smooth_prob(p_cnt, p_tot, vocab_size)

            if grandparents is not None:
                gp = grandparents.iloc[i]
            else:
                gp = parent_tracker.get(parent, "unknown")

            t_cnt = triple_counts.get((gp, parent, proc), 0)
            t_tot = pair_context_totals.get((gp, parent), 0)
            p_triple = jm.laplace_smooth_prob(t_cnt, t_tot, vocab_size)

            parent_tracker[proc] = parent

            p_laplace_pair = p_pair
            p_jm = (w_triple * p_triple) + (w_pair * p_pair) + (w_unigram * p_unigram)
        else:
            p_laplace_pair = p_unigram
            p_jm = p_unigram

        surp_vals[i] = jm.surprisal(p_laplace_pair)
        lineage_vals[i] = -math.log2(max(p_jm, 1e-6))

    df["process_surprisal"] = surp_vals
    df["lineage_surprisal"] = lineage_vals

    # 2. lolbin_contextual (Correção P1: remoção do max de batch, sem leakage)
    is_lolbin = procs.isin(LOLBINS).astype(float)
    df["lolbin_contextual"] = is_lolbin * surp_vals

    return df


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\builders\pilar3_auth.py
################################################################################

# core/features/builders/pilar3_auth.py
#
# Pilar 3: Autenticação e Dinâmica Temporal de Falhas.
# Correções aplicadas (Auditoria 01/11/2026):
# - Hawkes treinado e avaliado estritamente sobre eventos de falha (EventID == 4625 / is_failed == 1)
# - FSR como fração causal real N_fail / (N_succ + N_fail + 1e-6)
# - Spray com Entropia por IP com shrinkage por sub-rede contra o baseline

import math
from collections import defaultdict
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Tuple

from core.features.estimators.hawkes import HawkesEstimator
import core.features.builders.legacy as legacy


def fit_pilar3_state(df_normal: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Ajusta priors globais e parâmetros de Hawkes por usuário estritamente sobre falhas."""
    state: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if df_normal.empty or "Time" not in df_normal.columns or "UserID" not in df_normal.columns:
        return state

    hawkes = HawkesEstimator(tau=300.0)
    user_params: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]] = {}

    # Filtra estritamente eventos de falha no treino benigno
    if "is_failed" in df_normal.columns:
        df_fails = df_normal[df_normal["is_failed"] == 1]
    elif "EventID" in df_normal.columns:
        df_fails = df_normal[df_normal["EventID"] == 4625]
    else:
        df_fails = df_normal.iloc[0:0]

    # Hawkes global prior ajustado sobre falhas
    if not df_fails.empty:
        all_fail_times = df_fails["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        g_mu, g_alpha = hawkes.fit_user(all_fail_times)
    else:
        g_mu, g_alpha = hawkes.mu_min, (0.2 * hawkes.max_branching) / hawkes.tau
    state["hawkes_global_prior"] = (g_mu, g_alpha)

    # Parâmetros por usuário (sobre falhas do usuário)
    if not df_fails.empty:
        for uid, group in df_fails.groupby("UserID", sort=False):
            t_user = group["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            mu_u, alpha_u = hawkes.fit_user(t_user, global_prior=(g_mu, g_alpha))
            user_params[str(uid)] = (mu_u, alpha_u)

    state["hawkes_user_params"] = user_params

    # Metadados para Spraying por IP
    domain_users_count = df_normal["UserID"].nunique()
    state["spray_domain_users"] = max(domain_users_count, 2)
    state["spray_global_stats"] = (0.0, 1.0)
    state["spray_subnet_stats"] = {}

    return state


def _time_window_ratio(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    failed_col: str,
    success_col: str,
    window_seconds: float
) -> pd.Series:
    """
    Calcula a fração real de falhas N_fail / (N_succ + N_fail + 1e-6)
    estritamente no intervalo [t-Delta, t) excluindo o evento atual (exclusive=True).
    """
    n_fail = legacy.time_window_count(df, failed_col, window_seconds, exclusive=True)
    n_succ = legacy.time_window_count(df, success_col, window_seconds, exclusive=True)
    return n_fail / (n_succ + n_fail + 1e-6)


def build_pilar3_auth(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Constrói as features do Pilar 3:
    - hawkes_z (Processo autoexcitante estritamente sobre falhas)
    - fsr_1m, fsr_5m, fsr_1h (Fração Failure-to-Success Ratio causal)
    - spray_z (Entropia de usuários por IP na janela de 600s com shrinkage)
    - fail_then_success_flag (Spec F14: sucesso com >= 3 falhas nos 600s anteriores)
    """
    if df.empty:
        return df

    state = historical_state or {}
    hawkes = HawkesEstimator(tau=300.0)
    g_prior = state.get("hawkes_global_prior", (1e-4, 0.001))
    user_hawkes = state.get("hawkes_user_params", {})

    # Colunas binárias de falha e sucesso
    if "is_failed" not in df.columns:
        if "EventID" in df.columns:
            df["is_failed"] = (df["EventID"] == 4625).astype(int)
        else:
            df["is_failed"] = 0
    if "is_success" not in df.columns:
        if "EventID" in df.columns:
            df["is_success"] = (df["EventID"] == 4624).astype(int)
        else:
            df["is_success"] = 0

    # 1. Hawkes Z-score estritamente sobre eventos de falha
    hawkes_z_vals = np.zeros(len(df), dtype=float)
    if "Time" in df.columns and "UserID" in df.columns:
        for _, idx in df.groupby("UserID", sort=False).groups.items():
            sub = df.loc[idx, ["Time", "is_failed"]]
            times = sub["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            fails = sub["is_failed"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
            uid = str(df.loc[idx[0], "UserID"])
            mu_u, alpha_u = user_hawkes.get(uid, g_prior)

            fail_times_history: List[float] = []
            z_list: List[float] = []
            for cur_t, is_f in zip(times, fails):
                prev_fail_t = fail_times_history[-50:]
                intensity = hawkes.compute_intensity(cur_t, prev_fail_t, mu_u, alpha_u)
                z = hawkes.z_score(intensity, mu_u, alpha_u)
                z_list.append(z)
                if is_f == 1:
                    fail_times_history.append(cur_t)

            hawkes_z_vals[sub.index] = z_list

    df["hawkes_z"] = hawkes_z_vals

    # 2. FSR causal em janelas temporais estritas [t-Delta, t)
    df["fsr_1m"] = _time_window_ratio(df, "is_failed", "is_success", 60.0)
    df["fsr_5m"] = _time_window_ratio(df, "is_failed", "is_success", 300.0)
    df["fsr_1h"] = _time_window_ratio(df, "is_failed", "is_success", 3600.0)

    # 3. fail_then_success_flag (Spec F14: janela 600s, N_4625 >= 3)
    prev_fails_600s = legacy.time_window_count(df, "is_failed", 600.0, exclusive=True)
    df["fail_then_success_flag"] = ((df["is_success"] == 1) & (prev_fails_600s >= 3)).astype(float)

    # 4. spray_z (Entropia por IP com shrinkage)
    if "IpAddress" in df.columns and "Time" in df.columns and "UserID" in df.columns:
        domain_u = float(state.get("spray_domain_users", df["UserID"].nunique()))
        log2_u_dom = math.log2(max(domain_u, 2.0))
        g_mu, g_sigma = state.get("spray_global_stats", (0.0, 1.0))
        subnet_stats = state.get("spray_subnet_stats", {})

        spray_z_vals = np.zeros(len(df), dtype=float)

        # Agrupamento causal por IpAddress
        ip_series = df["IpAddress"].astype(str).str.strip()
        valid_ip_mask = ~ip_series.isin(["-", "", "none", "nan", "null"])

        if valid_ip_mask.any():
            for ip_val, group_idx in df[valid_ip_mask].groupby(ip_series[valid_ip_mask], sort=False).groups.items():
                sub = df.loc[group_idx, ["Time", "UserID", "is_success", "is_failed"]]
                t_arr = sub["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                u_arr = sub["UserID"].to_numpy()
                s_arr = sub["is_success"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
                f_arr = sub["is_failed"].to_numpy(dtype=int)

                n_events = len(t_arr)
                scores = []

                # Janela deslizante causal [t - 600.0, t)
                for i in range(n_events):
                    cur_t = t_arr[i]
                    left = np.searchsorted(t_arr, cur_t - 600.0, side="left")
                    # Eventos estritamente anteriores no intervalo [t - 600, t)
                    w_users = u_arr[left:i]
                    w_succ = s_arr[left:i]
                    w_fail = f_arr[left:i]

                    if len(w_users) == 0:
                        scores.append(0.0)
                        continue

                    # Contagem de usuários distintos
                    _, user_counts = np.unique(w_users, return_counts=True)
                    n_dist = len(user_counts)
                    if n_dist <= 1:
                        scores.append(0.0)
                        continue

                    total_ip_events = len(w_users)
                    probs = user_counts / float(total_ip_events)
                    h_entropy = -np.sum(probs * np.log2(probs))
                    h_norm = float(h_entropy / log2_u_dom)

                    n_4624 = np.sum(w_succ)
                    n_4625 = np.sum(w_fail)
                    sr = float(n_4624) / (float(n_4624 + n_4625) + 1e-6)

                    score = h_norm * (1.0 - sr) * math.log2(1.0 + float(n_dist))
                    scores.append(float(score) if score > 1e-6 else 0.0)

                # Shrinkage por sub-rede /24
                sub_key = ip_val.rsplit(".", 1)[0] if "." in ip_val else ip_val
                sub_mu, sub_sigma = subnet_stats.get(sub_key, (g_mu, g_sigma))

                # Normalização Z-score com shrinkage
                z_arr = ((np.array(scores) - sub_mu) / max(sub_sigma, 1e-4)).clip(-3.0, 10.0)
                spray_z_vals[group_idx] = z_arr

        df["spray_z"] = spray_z_vals
    elif "spray_z" not in df.columns:
        df["spray_z"] = 0.0

    return df


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\builders\pilar4_topology.py
################################################################################

# core/features/builders/pilar4_topology.py
#
# Pilar 4: Topologia de Rede e Rastreamento de Novidade com LazyDecaySet.
# Correções aplicadas (Auditoria 01/11/2026):
# - host_affinity: calculada estritamente em escala de bits: -log2(max(p_laplace, 1e-6))
# - host_entropy_z: z-score normalizado contra baseline histórico do treino por usuário (sem leakage do batch)

import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List

from core.features.decay import LazyDecaySet, TTL_SEEN_SETS


def fit_pilar4_state(df_normal: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Ajusta afinidades de host, priors de entropia e inicializa os conjuntos de novidade LazyDecaySet."""
    state: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if df_normal.empty:
        return state

    # 1. Host Affinity (Laplace)
    if "UserID" in df_normal.columns and "LogHost" in df_normal.columns:
        user_host_counts = df_normal.groupby(["UserID", "LogHost"]).size().to_dict()
        user_totals = df_normal.groupby("UserID").size().to_dict()
        total_unique_hosts = df_normal["LogHost"].nunique()

        state["affinity_user_host_counts"] = user_host_counts
        state["affinity_user_totals"] = user_totals
        state["affinity_vocab_size"] = max(total_unique_hosts, 5)

        # Inicializa conjuntos vistos no treino com TTL
        seen_user_host = LazyDecaySet(ttl_seconds=TTL_SEEN_SETS)
        for _, row in df_normal[["UserID", "LogHost", "Time"]].iterrows():
            seen_user_host.add(f"{row['UserID']}@{row['LogHost']}", float(row["Time"]))
        state["seen_user_host"] = seen_user_host

    if "ProcessName" in df_normal.columns and "LogHost" in df_normal.columns:
        seen_proc_host = LazyDecaySet(ttl_seconds=TTL_SEEN_SETS)
        for _, row in df_normal[["ProcessName", "LogHost", "Time"]].iterrows():
            seen_proc_host.add(f"{row['ProcessName']}@{row['LogHost']}", float(row["Time"]))
        state["seen_proc_host"] = seen_proc_host

    if "UserID" in df_normal.columns and "IpAddress" in df_normal.columns:
        seen_user_ip = LazyDecaySet(ttl_seconds=TTL_SEEN_SETS)
        for _, row in df_normal[["UserID", "IpAddress", "Time"]].iterrows():
            seen_user_ip.add(f"{row['UserID']}@{row['IpAddress']}", float(row["Time"]))
        state["seen_user_ip"] = seen_user_ip

    if "UserID" in df_normal.columns and "LogonType" in df_normal.columns:
        seen_user_logon = LazyDecaySet(ttl_seconds=TTL_SEEN_SETS)
        for _, row in df_normal[["UserID", "LogonType", "Time"]].iterrows():
            seen_user_logon.add(f"{row['UserID']}@{row['LogonType']}", float(row["Time"]))
        state["seen_user_logon"] = seen_user_logon

    # 2. Baseline de host_entropy_norm por usuário no treino benigno
    if "LogHost" in df_normal.columns and "Time" in df_normal.columns and "UserID" in df_normal.columns:
        user_entropy_stats = {}
        all_entropies = []
        for uid, idx in df_normal.groupby("UserID", sort=False).groups.items():
            sub = df_normal.loc[idx, ["Time", "LogHost"]].sort_values("Time", kind="stable")
            times = sub["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            hosts = sub["LogHost"].to_numpy()
            u_entropies = []
            for i, cur_t in enumerate(times):
                left = np.searchsorted(times, cur_t - 3600.0, side="left")
                window_hosts = hosts[left:i]
                if len(window_hosts) <= 1:
                    u_entropies.append(0.0)
                else:
                    _, counts = np.unique(window_hosts, return_counts=True)
                    if len(counts) <= 1:
                        u_entropies.append(0.0)
                    else:
                        probs = counts / np.sum(counts)
                        h_shannon = -np.sum(probs * np.log2(probs))
                        h_max = math.log2(len(counts))
                        u_entropies.append(float(h_shannon / h_max))

            if u_entropies:
                m_u = float(np.mean(u_entropies))
                s_u = float(np.std(u_entropies))
                user_entropy_stats[str(uid)] = (m_u, s_u)
                all_entropies.extend(u_entropies)

        g_mean = float(np.mean(all_entropies)) if all_entropies else 0.0
        g_std = float(np.std(all_entropies)) if all_entropies and np.std(all_entropies) > 0 else 1.0
        state["host_entropy_user_stats"] = user_entropy_stats
        state["host_entropy_global_stats"] = (g_mean, g_std)

    return state


def build_pilar4_topology(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    historical_state: Dict[str, Any] = None,
    alpha: float = 0.2
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Constrói as features do Pilar 4:
    - host_affinity (Laplace em escala de bits -log2 P)
    - is_new_user_on_host (LazyDecaySet)
    - is_new_process_on_host (LazyDecaySet)
    - is_new_ip_for_user (LazyDecaySet)
    - is_new_logontype_for_user (LazyDecaySet)
    - host_entropy_norm + host_entropy_z (janela 1 hora contra baseline de treino)
    """
    if df.empty:
        return df

    state = historical_state or {}

    # 1. Host Affinity (Laplace) em escala de bits
    if "UserID" in df.columns and "LogHost" in df.columns:
        u_h_counts = state.get("affinity_user_host_counts", {})
        u_totals = state.get("affinity_user_totals", {})
        v_size = state.get("affinity_vocab_size", 10)

        aff_list = []
        for u, h in zip(df["UserID"], df["LogHost"]):
            cnt = u_h_counts.get((u, h), 0)
            tot = u_totals.get(u, 0)
            p_laplace = (float(cnt) + alpha) / (float(tot) + (alpha * v_size))
            aff_list.append(-math.log2(max(p_laplace, 1e-6)))
        df["host_affinity"] = aff_list

        # is_new_user_on_host via LazyDecaySet
        seen_uh = state.get("seen_user_host")
        if seen_uh is not None:
            new_uh_flags = []
            for u, h, t in zip(df["UserID"], df["LogHost"], df["Time"]):
                key = f"{u}@{h}"
                is_known = seen_uh.contains_at(key, float(t))
                new_uh_flags.append(0.0 if is_known else 1.0)
            df["is_new_user_on_host"] = new_uh_flags
        else:
            df["is_new_user_on_host"] = 0.0

    # 2. is_new_process_on_host
    if "ProcessName" in df.columns and "LogHost" in df.columns:
        seen_ph = state.get("seen_proc_host")
        if seen_ph is not None:
            df["is_new_process_on_host"] = [
                0.0 if seen_ph.contains_at(f"{p}@{h}", float(t)) else 1.0
                for p, h, t in zip(df["ProcessName"], df["LogHost"], df["Time"])
            ]

    # 3. is_new_ip_for_user
    if "UserID" in df.columns and "IpAddress" in df.columns:
        seen_uip = state.get("seen_user_ip")
        if seen_uip is not None:
            df["is_new_ip_for_user"] = [
                0.0 if seen_uip.contains_at(f"{u}@{ip}", float(t)) else 1.0
                for u, ip, t in zip(df["UserID"], df["IpAddress"], df["Time"])
            ]

    # 4. is_new_logontype_for_user
    if "UserID" in df.columns and "LogonType" in df.columns:
        seen_ulg = state.get("seen_user_logon")
        if seen_ulg is not None:
            df["is_new_logontype_for_user"] = [
                0.0 if seen_ulg.contains_at(f"{u}@{lt}", float(t)) else 1.0
                for u, lt, t in zip(df["UserID"], df["LogonType"], df["Time"])
            ]

    # 5. host_entropy_norm + host_entropy_z na janela causal [t-3600s, t) contra baseline de treino
    if "LogHost" in df.columns and "Time" in df.columns and "UserID" in df.columns:
        entropy_vals = np.zeros(len(df), dtype=float)
        entropy_z_vals = np.zeros(len(df), dtype=float)
        user_entropy_stats = state.get("host_entropy_user_stats", {})
        g_mean_h, g_std_h = state.get("host_entropy_global_stats", (0.0, 1.0))

        for _, idx in df.groupby("UserID", sort=False).groups.items():
            sub = df.loc[idx, ["Time", "LogHost"]].sort_values("Time", kind="stable")
            times = sub["Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            hosts = sub["LogHost"].to_numpy()
            uid = str(df.loc[idx[0], "UserID"])
            u_mean, u_std = user_entropy_stats.get(uid, (g_mean_h, g_std_h))
            eff_std = u_std if u_std > 1e-4 else max(g_std_h, 0.1)

            u_entropies = []
            for i, cur_t in enumerate(times):
                left = np.searchsorted(times, cur_t - 3600.0, side="left")
                window_hosts = hosts[left:i]  # estritamente [t-3600, t)
                if len(window_hosts) <= 1:
                    u_entropies.append(0.0)
                else:
                    _, counts = np.unique(window_hosts, return_counts=True)
                    if len(counts) <= 1:
                        u_entropies.append(0.0)
                    else:
                        probs = counts / np.sum(counts)
                        h_shannon = -np.sum(probs * np.log2(probs))
                        h_max = math.log2(len(counts))
                        u_entropies.append(float(h_shannon / h_max))

            u_ent_arr = np.array(u_entropies, dtype=float)
            entropy_vals[sub.index] = u_ent_arr
            entropy_z_vals[sub.index] = ((u_ent_arr - u_mean) / eff_std).clip(-3.0, 5.0)

        df["host_entropy_norm"] = entropy_vals
        df["host_entropy_z"] = entropy_z_vals

    return df


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\builders\pilar5_temporal.py
################################################################################

# core/features/builders/pilar5_temporal.py
#
# Pilar 5: Ciclos Temporais, Harmônicos e Densidade Circular de Von Mises.
# Correções aplicadas (Auditoria 01/11/2026):
# - first_event_after_dormant: primeiro evento histórico do usuário é 0.0 (sem eventos anteriores)

import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Tuple

from core.features.estimators.von_mises import VonMisesEstimator


def fit_pilar5_state(df_normal: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Ajusta parâmetros circulares de Von Mises por usuário."""
    state: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if df_normal.empty or "Time" not in df_normal.columns or "UserID" not in df_normal.columns:
        return state

    dt_series = pd.to_datetime(df_normal["Time"], unit="s")
    hours = dt_series.dt.hour + (dt_series.dt.minute / 60.0)

    vm = VonMisesEstimator()
    user_vm: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float, int]] = {}

    for uid, idx in df_normal.groupby("UserID", sort=False).groups.items():
        u_hours = hours.loc[idx].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        mu, kappa, n = vm.fit_sample(u_hours)
        user_vm[str(uid)] = (mu, kappa, n)

    state["von_mises_user_params"] = user_vm
    return state


def build_pilar5_temporal(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Constrói as features do Pilar 5:
    - time_harmonics (6 dimensões: k=1,2,3)
    - weekday_harmonics (2 dimensões)
    - temporal_surprisal (Von Mises univariado por usuário)
    - first_event_after_dormant (inatividade > 12h, primeiro evento sempre 0.0)
    - dormant_surprisal
    """
    if df.empty or "Time" not in df.columns:
        return df

    state = historical_state or {}
    vm = VonMisesEstimator()
    user_vm = state.get("von_mises_user_params", {})

    dt_series = pd.to_datetime(df["Time"], unit="s")
    hour_float = dt_series.dt.hour + (dt_series.dt.minute / 60.0)
    wday = dt_series.dt.dayofweek.astype(float)

    # 1. time_harmonics (6 dimensões)
    theta_day = 2.0 * math.pi * (hour_float / 24.0)
    for k in [1, 2, 3]:
        df[f"time_sin_k{k}"] = np.sin(k * theta_day)
        df[f"time_cos_k{k}"] = np.cos(k * theta_day)

    # 2. weekday_harmonics (2 dimensões)
    theta_week = 2.0 * math.pi * (wday / 7.0)
    df["weekday_sin"] = np.sin(theta_week)
    df["weekday_cos"] = np.cos(theta_week)

    # 3. temporal_surprisal (Von Mises)
    surp_vals = np.zeros(len(df), dtype=float)
    if "UserID" in df.columns:
        for i in range(len(df)):
            uid = str(df.iloc[i]["UserID"])
            h = hour_float.iloc[i]
            if uid in user_vm:
                mu, kappa, _ = user_vm[uid]
                surp_vals[i] = vm.surprisal(h, mu, kappa)
            else:
                surp_vals[i] = math.log(2.0 * math.pi)

    df["temporal_surprisal"] = surp_vals

    # 4. first_event_after_dormant e dormant_surprisal
    df["first_event_after_dormant"] = 0.0
    df["dormant_surprisal"] = 0.0

    if "UserID" in df.columns:
        for _, idx in df.groupby("UserID", sort=False).groups.items():
            sub_times = df.loc[idx, "Time"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            n_events = len(sub_times)
            is_dormant = np.zeros(n_events, dtype=float)
            dormant_surp = np.zeros(n_events, dtype=float)
            if n_events > 1:
                diffs = np.diff(sub_times)
                dormant_mask = diffs > 43200.0  # gap > 12 horas
                is_dormant[1:] = dormant_mask.astype(float)
                dormant_surp[1:] = np.log1p(np.maximum(diffs / 3600.0, 0.0)) * is_dormant[1:]
            df.loc[idx, "first_event_after_dormant"] = is_dormant
            df.loc[idx, "dormant_surprisal"] = dormant_surp

    return df


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\builders\pilar6_forensic.py
################################################################################

# core/features/builders/pilar6_forensic.py
#
# Pilar 6: Forense Digital, Decodificação de Registro e Detecção de Evasão em Linha de Comando.

import re
import math
import codecs
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List

from core.features.decay import LazyDecaySet, TTL_SEEN_SETS

# Padrões regulares para as 10 flags de evasão (Spec §3.6)
EVASION_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"-(?:enc|encodedcommand)\s+[A-Za-z0-9+/=]{4,}", re.IGNORECASE),          # 1. Base64
    re.compile(r"""(?:["']\w+["']){2,}"""),                                              # 2. Aspas intercaladas
    re.compile(r"\w+\^\w+"),                                                             # 3. Circumflexo (cmd)
    re.compile(r"""['"]\s*\+\s*['"]"""),                                                # 4. Concatenação de string
    re.compile(r"%[A-Za-z0-9_]+%|\$\{env:[A-Za-z0-9_]+\}", re.IGNORECASE),              # 5. Env variables
    re.compile(r"\b(?:iex|invoke-expression)\b", re.IGNORECASE),                         # 6. IEX
    re.compile(r"\b(?:downloadstring|downloadfile|webclient)\b", re.IGNORECASE),         # 7. Download cradle
    re.compile(r"-(?:executionpolicy|ep)\s+bypass", re.IGNORECASE),                      # 8. ExecPolicy bypass
    re.compile(r"-(?:w|windowstyle)\s+hidden|-nop\b", re.IGNORECASE),                    # 9. Janela oculta / NoProfile
]

SENSITIVE_REGISTRY_KEYS = [
    r"software\\microsoft\\windows\\currentversion\\run",
    r"software\\microsoft\\windows\\currentversion\\runonce",
    r"system\\currentcontrolset\\services",
    r"system\\currentcontrolset\\control\\lsa",
    r"sam\\sam\\domains\\account\\users",
]


def shannon_entropy(s: str) -> float:
    """Calcula entropia de Shannon de uma string."""
    if not s:
        return 0.0
    counts = {}
    for c in s:
        counts[c] = counts.get(c, 0) + 1
    total = len(s)
    ent = 0.0
    for cnt in counts.values():
        p = cnt / total
        ent -= p * math.log2(p)
    return float(ent)


def decode_userassist(val_name: str, val_bytes: bytes) -> int:
    """
    Decodifica ROT13 na chave UserAssist e extrai a contagem de execuções
    (inteiro de 4 bytes na posição 4 do array de bytes).
    """
    try:
        decoded_name = codecs.decode(val_name, "rot_13")
        if val_bytes and len(val_bytes) >= 8:
            # Bytes 4-7 contêm o contador de execuções (little-endian)
            count = int.from_bytes(val_bytes[4:8], byteorder="little")
            return count
    except Exception:
        pass
    return 0


def build_pilar6_forensic(df: pd.DataFrame, historical_state: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Constrói as features do Pilar 6:
    - userassist_decoded_exec
    - cmd_obfuscation_entropy
    - cmd_regex_evasion (10 dimensões)
    - registry_* (rarity, new, sensitive, value_entropy, len)
    - module_* (load_rarity, user_writable, first_seen, unsigned)
    """
    if df.empty:
        return df

    N = len(df)

    # 1. Linha de comando: entropia e 10 flags de evasão
    if "CommandLine" in df.columns:
        cmds = df["CommandLine"].fillna("").astype(str)
        df["cmd_obfuscation_entropy"] = [shannon_entropy(c) for c in cmds]

        # 9 flags via regex + 1 flag de comprimento > 500
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(EVASION_PATTERNS, start=1):
            df[f"cmd_evasion_flag_{idx}"] = [1.0 if pattern.search(c) else 0.0 for c in cmds]
        df["cmd_evasion_flag_10"] = (cmds.str.len() > 500).astype(float)
    else:
        df["cmd_obfuscation_entropy"] = 0.0
        for idx in range(1, 11):
            df[f"cmd_evasion_flag_{idx}"] = 0.0

    # 2. Registro (Sysmon Event 12/13/14)
    if "RegistryPath" in df.columns:
        paths = df["RegistryPath"].fillna("").astype(str).str.lower()
        is_sens = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
        for sens in SENSITIVE_REGISTRY_KEYS:
            is_sens = np.maximum(is_sens, paths.str.contains(sens, regex=True).astype(float))
        df["is_sensitive_key"] = is_sens
        df["registry_key_rarity"] = 0.0
        df["new_registry_key_on_host"] = 0.0
        df["registry_value_entropy"] = 0.0
        df["registry_value_len"] = 0.0
        df["userassist_decoded_exec"] = 0.0
    else:
        df["is_sensitive_key"] = 0.0
        df["registry_key_rarity"] = 0.0
        df["new_registry_key_on_host"] = 0.0
        df["registry_value_entropy"] = 0.0
        df["registry_value_len"] = 0.0
        df["userassist_decoded_exec"] = 0.0

    # 3. Módulos / DLLs (Sysmon Event 7)
    if "ModulePath" in df.columns:
        mod_paths = df["ModulePath"].fillna("").astype(str).str.lower()
        writable = mod_paths.str.contains(r"\\temp\\|\\appdata\\|\\users\\public\\", regex=True)
        df["module_from_user_writable"] = writable.astype(float)
        df["module_load_rarity"] = 0.0
        df["module_first_seen_on_host"] = 0.0
        if "SignatureStatus" in df.columns:
            df["module_unsigned_or_unknown"] = (~df["SignatureStatus"].astype(str).str.lower().isin(["valid", "signed"])).astype(float)
        else:
            df["module_unsigned_or_unknown"] = 0.0
    else:
        df["module_from_user_writable"] = 0.0
        df["module_load_rarity"] = 0.0
        df["module_first_seen_on_host"] = 0.0
        df["module_unsigned_or_unknown"] = 0.0

    return df


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\decay.py
################################################################################

# core/features/decay.py
#
# Estruturas de dados causais com decaimento exponencial preguiçoso (lazy decay)
# e conjuntos com TTL (Time-To-Live).
#
# Princípios:
# - Causalidade estrita: transform() não muta o estado.
# - Atualização sob demanda: decaimento avaliado no instante da leitura t.
# - Serializável para compor o FeatureState.

import math
from typing import Tuple, Optional, Any, Dict

# Constantes de configuração recomendadas (em segundos)
SECS_PER_HOUR = 3600.0
SECS_PER_DAY = 86400.0

HALF_LIFE_RARITY_PROCESS_REGISTRY = 7.0 * SECS_PER_DAY   # 7 dias
HALF_LIFE_HOST_AFFINITY           = 7.0 * SECS_PER_DAY   # 7 dias
HALF_LIFE_AUTH_BURSTS             = 1.0 * SECS_PER_HOUR  # 1 hora
TTL_SEEN_SETS                     = 30.0 * SECS_PER_DAY  # 30 dias


class LazyDecayCounter:
    """
    Contador contínuo com decaimento exponencial sob demanda (lazy evaluation).
    
    A intensidade decai segundo e^(-gamma * (t - t_last)), onde:
    gamma = ln(2) / half_life_seconds.
    """

    def __init__(self, half_life_seconds: float):
        if half_life_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"half_life_seconds deve ser positivo, recebido: {half_life_seconds}")
        self.half_life_seconds: float = float(half_life_seconds)
        self.gamma: float = math.log(2.0) / self.half_life_seconds
        self.value: float = 0.0
        self.t_last: Optional[float] = None

    def read(self, t: float) -> float:
        """
        Retorna o valor do contador projetado para o instante t aplicando e^(-gamma * dt).
        Não altera o estado interno (leitura pura).
        """
        if self.t_last is None:
            return 0.0
        t_val = float(t)
        if t_val <= self.t_last:
            # Não decai no passado ou instante simultâneo
            return float(self.value)
        dt = t_val - self.t_last
        return float(self.value * math.exp(-self.gamma * dt))

    def incr(self, t: float, amount: float = 1.0) -> None:
        """
        Avança o contador para o instante t com decaimento preguiçoso e adiciona amount.
        Muta o estado interno. Deve ser invocado apenas na fase de atualização (update/fit).
        """
        t_val = float(t)
        current = self.read(t_val)
        self.value = current + float(amount)
        self.t_last = t_val if self.t_last is None else max(self.t_last, t_val)

    def snapshot(self) -> Tuple[float, Optional[float]]:
        """Exporta tupla (value, t_last) para persistência."""
        return (float(self.value), float(self.t_last) if self.t_last is not None else None)

    @classmethod
    def from_snapshot(cls, snap: Tuple[float, Optional[float]], half_life_seconds: float) -> "LazyDecayCounter":
        """Reconstrói um LazyDecayCounter a partir do snapshot salvo."""
        counter = cls(half_life_seconds=half_life_seconds)
        counter.value = float(snap[0])
        counter.t_last = float(snap[1]) if snap[1] is not None else None
        return counter

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"LazyDecayCounter(value={self.value:.4f}, t_last={self.t_last}, half_life={self.half_life_seconds}s)"


class LazyDecaySet:
    """
    Conjunto com Time-To-Live (TTL) preguiçoso para rastreamento de novidade
    (ex.: is_new_user_on_host, is_new_ip_for_user).
    
    Um item expira se (t_current - t_seen) > ttl_seconds.
    """

    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: float):
        if ttl_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"ttl_seconds deve ser positivo, recebido: {ttl_seconds}")
        self.ttl_seconds: float = float(ttl_seconds)
        self._items: Dict[Any, float] = {}  # key -> last_seen_t
        self._current_t: float = 0.0

    def __contains__(self, key: Any) -> bool:
        """
        Verifica se a chave está presente e não expirou em relação a self._current_t.
        Remove chaves expiradas sob demanda.
        """
        if key not in self._items:
            return False
        last_seen = self._items[key]
        if (self._current_t - last_seen) > self.ttl_seconds:
            del self._items[key]
            return False
        return True

    def contains_at(self, key: Any, t: float) -> bool:
        """
        Avaliação estritamente causal no instante t.
        Retorna True se key foi vista em t' <= t e (t - t') <= ttl_seconds.
        Não altera o estado interno.
        """
        if key not in self._items:
            return False
        t_val = float(t)
        last_seen = self._items[key]
        if t_val < last_seen:
            # O item só foi visto no futuro em relação a t
            return False
        if (t_val - last_seen) > self.ttl_seconds:
            return False
        return True

    def add(self, key: Any, t: float) -> None:
        """
        Adiciona ou atualiza a chave com o timestamp t.
        Muta o estado interno e executa compactação preguiçosa periódica.
        """
        t_val = float(t)
        self._items[key] = t_val
        if t_val > self._current_t:
            self._current_t = t_val

        # Compactação periódica para evitar vazamento de memória em streams longos
        if len(self._items) > 5000 and len(self._items) % 1000 == 0:
            self.prune(self._current_t)

    def prune(self, current_t: float) -> int:
        """Remove explicitamente todas as chaves expiradas em current_t."""
        expired = [k for k, last_t in self._items.items() if (current_t - last_t) > self.ttl_seconds]
        for k in expired:
            del self._items[k]
        return len(expired)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._items)

    def snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Exporta dicionário para persistência."""
        return {
            "ttl_seconds": self.ttl_seconds,
            "items": dict(self._items),
            "current_t": self._current_t,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_snapshot(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "LazyDecaySet":
        """Reconstrói o LazyDecaySet a partir de um snapshot salvo."""
        decay_set = cls(ttl_seconds=data["ttl_seconds"])
        decay_set._items = dict(data.get("items", {}))
        decay_set._current_t = float(data.get("current_t", 0.0))
        return decay_set

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"LazyDecaySet(items={len(self._items)}, ttl={self.ttl_seconds}s, current_t={self._current_t})"


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\estimators\hawkes.py
################################################################################

# core/features/estimators/hawkes.py
#
# Processo pontual autoexcitante de Hawkes para modelagem de rajadas de autenticação.
# Garantias metodológicas:
# - Estabilidade matemática: branching ratio alpha * tau < 1.0 garantido por truncamento.
# - Piso de taxa basal: mu >= mu_min (1e-4 ev/s) para estabilidade em usuários de baixo volume.
# - Estimador MAP-EB com prior Gamma quando N_u < 5 eventos; MLE quando N_u >= 5.
# - Z-score da intensidade estacionária teórica E[lambda] = mu / (1 - alpha * tau).

import math
import numpy as np
from typing import Tuple, Dict, Any, Optional, List
from scipy.optimize import minimize


class HawkesEstimator:
    """
    Modelo univariado de Hawkes com núcleo de decaimento exponencial:
    lambda(t) = mu + sum_{t_i < t} alpha * exp(-(t - t_i) / tau)
    """

    def __init__(self, tau: float = 300.0, mu_min: float = 1e-4, max_branching: float = 0.95):
        self.tau = float(tau)                  # constante temporal (default: 5 minutos = 300s)
        self.mu_min = float(mu_min)            # piso de taxa de fundo
        self.max_branching = float(max_branching)  # teto estrito para alpha * tau

    def fit_user(self, event_times: np.ndarray, global_prior: Optional[Tuple[float, float]] = None) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """
        Ajusta os parâmetros (mu, alpha) para a sequência temporal de um usuário.
        Garante alpha * tau <= max_branching < 1.0.
        """
        times = np.sort(np.asarray(event_times, dtype=float))
        times = times[~np.isnan(times)]
        N = len(times)

        # Se N < 5 ou intervalo nulo, usa prior global empírico ou fallback padrão
        if N < 5:
            if global_prior is not None:
                return float(global_prior[0]), float(global_prior[1])
            mu_fb = max(self.mu_min, float(N) / max(times[-1] - times[0], 3600.0) if N > 1 else self.mu_min)
            alpha_fb = (0.5 * self.max_branching) / self.tau
            return mu_fb, alpha_fb

        T = times[-1] - times[0]
        if T <= 0:
            return self.mu_min, (0.5 * self.max_branching) / self.tau

        # Recursive computation of log-likelihood for exponential kernel
        # R_i = sum_{j < i} exp(-(t_i - t_j) / tau)
        dt = np.diff(times)
        decay = np.exp(-dt / self.tau)

        R = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
        for i in range(1, N):
            R[i] = decay[i - 1] * (R[i - 1] + 1.0)

        max_alpha = self.max_branching / self.tau

        def neg_log_likelihood(params: np.ndarray) -> float:
            mu, alpha = params[0], params[1]
            if mu <= 0 or alpha < 0 or (alpha * self.tau) >= 1.0:
                return 1e12

            # ln L = sum_{i=1}^N ln(mu + alpha * R_i) - mu * T - alpha * tau * sum_{i=1}^N (1 - exp(-(T_end - t_i)/tau))
            lambdas = mu + alpha * R
            if np.any(lambdas <= 0):
                return 1e12

            log_sum = np.sum(np.log(lambdas))
            integral_mu = mu * T
            integral_alpha = alpha * self.tau * np.sum(1.0 - np.exp(-(times[-1] - times) / self.tau))

            # Penalização MAP regularizada (Gamma prior)
            prior_penalty = -0.1 * math.log(mu) - 0.1 * math.log(max(alpha, 1e-9))
            return -(log_sum - integral_mu - integral_alpha) + prior_penalty

        init_mu = max(self.mu_min, float(N) / T)
        init_alpha = 0.2 * max_alpha

        res = minimize(
            neg_log_likelihood,
            x0=np.array([init_mu, init_alpha]),
            bounds=[(self.mu_min, 10.0), (0.0, max_alpha)],
            method="L-BFGS-B"
        )

        if res.success and not np.isnan(res.x[0]):
            mu_opt = float(np.clip(res.x[0], self.mu_min, 10.0))
            alpha_opt = float(np.clip(res.x[1], 0.0, max_alpha))
            return mu_opt, alpha_opt

        # Fallback seguro
        return init_mu, 0.2 * max_alpha

    def compute_intensity(self, current_t: float, prev_times: List[float], mu: float, alpha: float) -> float:
        """Calcula a intensidade pontual instantânea lambda(t)."""
        decay_sum = 0.0
        for pt in prev_times:
            if pt < current_t:
                dt = current_t - pt
                # Corta contribuição residual além de 5 meias-vidas
                if dt < 5.0 * self.tau:
                    decay_sum += math.exp(-dt / self.tau)
        return float(mu + alpha * decay_sum)

    def z_score(self, current_intensity: float, mu: float, alpha: float) -> float:
        """
        Calcula o Z-score em relação ao regime estacionário:
        E[lambda] = mu / (1 - alpha * tau)
        Var[lambda] approx (mu * alpha) / (2 * (1 - alpha * tau))
        """
        branching = min(alpha * self.tau, self.max_branching)
        denom = max(1.0 - branching, 1e-6)
        mean_intensity = mu / denom
        var_intensity = (mu * max(alpha, 1e-6)) / (2.0 * denom)
        std_intensity = math.sqrt(max(var_intensity, 1e-8))

        return float((current_intensity - mean_intensity) / std_intensity)


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\estimators\jelinek_mercer.py
################################################################################

# core/features/estimators/jelinek_mercer.py
#
# Estimador de interpolação linear Jelinek-Mercer com algoritmo EM
# e suavização de Laplace para raridade de processos, linhagens e registro.

import math
from typing import Tuple, Dict, Any, List, Optional
import numpy as np


class JelinekMercerEstimator:
    """
    Combina modelos de probabilidade em múltiplos níveis (usuário, host, global):
    P_JM = lambda_1 * P_user + lambda_2 * P_host + lambda_3 * P_global
    onde sum(lambda_i) == 1 e lambda_i >= 0.
    """

    DEFAULT_WEIGHTS = (0.6, 0.3, 0.1)

    def __init__(self, laplace_alpha: float = 0.2, max_iter: int = 50, tol: float = 1e-4):
        self.laplace_alpha = float(laplace_alpha)
        self.max_iter = int(max_iter)
        self.tol = float(tol)

    def estimate_weights_em(
        self,
        p_user: np.ndarray,
        p_host: np.ndarray,
        p_global: np.ndarray,
        initial_weights: Optional[Tuple[float, float, float]] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        """
        Ajusta os pesos lambda_1, lambda_2, lambda_3 via Expectation-Maximization (EM).
        Se a convergência falhar ou os vetores forem vazios, utiliza o fallback documentado (0.6, 0.3, 0.1).
        """
        N = len(p_user)
        if N == 0 or len(p_host) != N or len(p_global) != N:
            return self.DEFAULT_WEIGHTS

        # Piso para evitar divisão por zero
        p1 = np.maximum(np.asarray(p_user, dtype=float), 1e-9)
        p2 = np.maximum(np.asarray(p_host, dtype=float), 1e-9)
        p3 = np.maximum(np.asarray(p_global, dtype=float), 1e-9)

        w = list(initial_weights) if initial_weights else [0.6, 0.3, 0.1]
        w = np.array(w, dtype=float)
        w /= np.sum(w)

        for _ in range(self.max_iter):
            # E-step: calcula as responsabilidades posteriores
            denom = w[0] * p1 + w[1] * p2 + w[2] * p3
            denom = np.maximum(denom, 1e-12)

            gamma1 = (w[0] * p1) / denom
            gamma2 = (w[1] * p2) / denom
            gamma3 = (w[2] * p3) / denom

            # M-step: atualiza os pesos
            new_w = np.array([np.mean(gamma1), np.mean(gamma2), np.mean(gamma3)], dtype=float)
            new_w /= np.sum(new_w)

            if np.max(np.abs(new_w - w)) < self.tol:
                break
            w = new_w

        # Garante normalização estrita sum(lambda_i) == 1
        w_final = tuple(float(x) for x in (w / np.sum(w)))
        return w_final

    def laplace_smooth_prob(self, count: int, total: int, vocab_size: int) -> float:
        """Suavização aditiva de Laplace com parâmetro alpha."""
        alpha = self.laplace_alpha
        v = max(1, vocab_size)
        return (float(count) + alpha) / (float(total) + (alpha * v))

    def surprisal(self, prob: float) -> float:
        """Calcula surprisal -log2(P)."""
        p_safe = max(prob, 1e-12)
        return float(-math.log2(p_safe))


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\estimators\von_mises.py
################################################################################

# core/features/estimators/von_mises.py
#
# Estimador robusto da distribuição circular de Von Mises por usuário.
# Garantias:
# - Regularização para N pequeno: N=1 -> R_bar=0, kappa=0 (distribuição uniforme circular).
# - Uso de scipy.special.i0e para evitar overflow exponencial com kappa grande.
# - Surprisal analítica e estável para kappa in [1e-6, 1e3].

import math
import numpy as np
from typing import Tuple, Dict, Any, Optional
from scipy.special import i0e


class VonMisesEstimator:
    """
    Estima a densidade circular de Von Mises:
    f(theta; mu, kappa) = exp(kappa * cos(theta - mu)) / (2 * pi * I_0(kappa))
    """

    def __init__(self, kappa_min: float = 1e-6, kappa_max: float = 1000.0):
        self.kappa_min = float(kappa_min)
        self.kappa_max = float(kappa_max)

    def fit_sample(self, hours: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float, int]:
        """
        Ajusta (mu, kappa, N) a partir de uma série de horários [0, 24).
        Retorna (mu_rad, kappa, N).
        """
        valid_hours = np.asarray(hours, dtype=float)
        valid_hours = valid_hours[~np.isnan(valid_hours)]
        N = len(valid_hours)
        if N <= 1:
            # Caso limite: 0 ou 1 evento -> kappa=0 (máxima incerteza / uniforme)
            mu_default = (2.0 * math.pi * valid_hours[0] / 24.0) if N == 1 else 0.0
            return float(mu_default), 0.0, N

        # Conversão de horas [0, 24) para radianos [0, 2pi)
        angles = 2.0 * math.pi * (valid_hours % 24.0) / 24.0
        C = float(np.mean(np.cos(angles)))
        S = float(np.mean(np.sin(angles)))
        R_bar = math.sqrt(C * C + S * S)
        mu = math.atan2(S, C) % (2.0 * math.pi)

        # Regularização de amostra finita: (N-1)/N evita que R_bar atinja 1.0 artificialmente
        R_reg = min(R_bar * (float(N - 1) / float(N)), 0.999)

        # Aproximação empírica de Fisher/Banerjee para MLE de kappa
        if R_reg < 0.53:
            kappa = 2.0 * R_reg + (R_reg ** 3) + (5.0 / 6.0) * (R_reg ** 5)
        elif R_reg < 0.85:
            kappa = -0.4 + 1.39 * R_reg + 0.43 / max(1.0 - R_reg, 1e-6)
        else:
            denom = max((R_reg ** 3) - 4.0 * (R_reg ** 2) + 3.0 * R_reg, 1e-6)
            kappa = 1.0 / denom

        kappa = float(np.clip(kappa, self.kappa_min, self.kappa_max))
        return float(mu), kappa, N

    def surprisal(self, hour: float, mu: float, kappa: float) -> float:
        """
        Calcula a surprisal (auto-informação negativa) -ln(f(theta)) de forma numericamente estável:
        -ln(f(theta)) = ln(2*pi) + ln(i0e(kappa)) + kappa * (1 - cos(theta - mu)).
        """
        if kappa <= self.kappa_min:
            # Distribuição uniforme no círculo de raio 2*pi
            return float(math.log(2.0 * math.pi))

        theta = 2.0 * math.pi * (float(hour) % 24.0) / 24.0
        cos_diff = math.cos(theta - mu)
        # Usa i0e(kappa) = i0(kappa) * exp(-kappa)
        log_i0e = float(np.log(i0e(kappa)))
        surp = math.log(2.0 * math.pi) + log_i0e + kappa * (1.0 - cos_diff)
        return max(0.0, surp)
