# core/features/feature_processor.py
#
# Feature engineering orchestrator para detecção comportamental UEBA.
# Suporta os presets: E0-legacy, FE-Core, FE-Advanced, FE-Full, FE-Full-No-Deterministic.
# Correções aplicadas (Auditoria 01/11/2026):
# - Descarte rigoroso de features indisponíveis do vetor final (active_specs vs unavailable_features)
# - Suporte a specs multidimensionais (entity_type, time_harmonics, weekday_harmonics, cmd_regex_evasion)
# - Fit seletivo apenas para pilares ativos no preset (otimização de tempo FE-Core / ablações)

import os
import pickle
import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Set

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from core.features.state import FeatureState, compute_param_hash
from core.features.spec import FeatureSpec, FeatureMetadata
from core.features.sets import (
    PRESET_E0_LEGACY,
    PRESET_FE_CORE,
    PRESET_FE_ADVANCED,
    PRESET_FE_FULL,
    PRESET_FE_FULL_NO_DETERMINISTIC,
    normalize_preset_name,
)
from core.features.registry import get_specs_for_preset, FEATURE_REGISTRY
import core.features.builders.legacy as legacy
from core.features.builders.pilar1_identity import build_pilar1_identity
from core.features.builders.pilar2_process import build_pilar2_process, fit_pilar2_state
from core.features.builders.pilar3_auth import build_pilar3_auth, fit_pilar3_state
from core.features.builders.pilar4_topology import build_pilar4_topology, fit_pilar4_state
from core.features.builders.pilar5_temporal import build_pilar5_temporal, fit_pilar5_state
from core.features.builders.pilar6_forensic import build_pilar6_forensic


def _columns_for_spec(spec_name: str) -> List[str]:
    """Mapeia o nome da FeatureSpec para os nomes das colunas físicas geradas no DataFrame."""
    if spec_name == "entity_type":
        return ["entity_type_user", "entity_type_machine", "entity_type_service", "entity_type_system"]
    if spec_name == "time_harmonics":
        return ["time_sin_k1", "time_cos_k1", "time_sin_k2", "time_cos_k2", "time_sin_k3", "time_cos_k3"]
    if spec_name == "weekday_harmonics":
        return ["weekday_sin", "weekday_cos"]
    if spec_name == "cmd_regex_evasion":
        return [f"cmd_evasion_flag_{i}" for i in range(1, 11)]
    return [spec_name]


class FeatureProcessor:
    """
    Orquestrador causal do pipeline de features comportamentais UEBA.
    
    Princípios:
    - Anti-leakage: fit() utiliza apenas eventos normais (Is_Anomaly == 0).
    - Imutabilidade em inferência: transform() nunca altera o estado interno.
    - Versionamento determinístico: snapshot / restore via FeatureState.
    - Preservação estrita: preset 'E0-legacy' reproduz o baseline anterior bit-a-bit.
    """

    def __init__(self, config: Dict[str, Any], strict: bool = True, feature_set: str = PRESET_E0_LEGACY):
        self.config: Dict[str, Any] = config or {}
        self.strict: bool = strict

        feature_cfg = self.config.get("features", {})
        raw_set = feature_cfg.get("feature_set", feature_set)
        self.feature_set: str = normalize_preset_name(raw_set)
        
        self.rate_window: int = max(1, int(feature_cfg.get("rate_window", 20)))
        self.excluded_features: Set[str] = set(feature_cfg.get("exclude_features", []))
        self.output_dir: Optional[str] = self.config.get("output_dir")

        self.features_frozen: bool = False
        self.final_feature_names: List[str] = []
        self.active_builders: List[str] = []
        self.unavailable_features: List[str] = []
        self.is_fitted: bool = False

        self.state: FeatureState = FeatureState(
            feature_version=str(feature_cfg.get("feature_version", "1.0.0"))
        )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # Retrocompatibilidade com acessos diretos a self.historical_state
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def historical_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return self.state.params

    @historical_state.setter
    def historical_state(self, val: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.state.params = val if val is not None else {}
        self.state.param_hash = compute_param_hash(self.state.params)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # Persistência de Estado (Legada e Nova)
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    def save_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Salva dicionário legado de auditoria."""
        return {
            "historical_state": self.state.params,
            "active_builders": list(self.active_builders),
            "final_feature_names": list(self.final_feature_names),
            "features_frozen": bool(self.features_frozen),
            "rate_window": int(self.rate_window),
            "is_fitted": bool(self.is_fitted),
            "feature_set": self.feature_set,
            "param_hash": self.state.param_hash,
        }

    def load_state(self, state_dict: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Carrega dicionário legado de auditoria."""
        self.state.params = state_dict.get("historical_state", {})
        self.state.param_hash = state_dict.get("param_hash", compute_param_hash(self.state.params))
        self.active_builders = list(state_dict.get("active_builders", []))
        self.final_feature_names = list(state_dict.get("final_feature_names", []))
        self.features_frozen = bool(state_dict.get("features_frozen", False))
        self.rate_window = int(state_dict.get("rate_window", self.rate_window))
        self.is_fitted = bool(state_dict.get("is_fitted", True))
        self.feature_set = state_dict.get("feature_set", self.feature_set)

    def snapshot(self) -> bytes:
        """Serializa o FeatureState completo em bytes."""
        return self.state.serialize()

    @classmethod
    def restore(cls, config: Dict[str, Any], blob: bytes) -> "FeatureProcessor":
        """Restaura uma instância a partir de um snapshot binário."""
        inst = cls(config=config, strict=True)
        inst.state = FeatureState.deserialize(blob)
        inst.is_fitted = True
        return inst

    def _rolling_rate(self, df: pd.DataFrame, source_col: str) -> pd.Series:
        return legacy.rolling_rate(df, source_col, self.rate_window)

    def _time_window_count(self, df: pd.DataFrame, event_col: str, window_seconds: float, exclusive: bool = False) -> pd.Series:
        return legacy.time_window_count(df, event_col, window_seconds, exclusive=exclusive)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # FIT (Apenas Normais → Anti-Leakage Estrito)
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    def fit(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
        if df is None or df.empty:
            return

        target_col = self.config.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")
        if target_col in df.columns:
            df_temp = df[df[target_col] == 0].copy()
            if df_temp.empty:
                if self.strict:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        "FeatureProcessor.fit: nenhum evento 'Is_Anomaly==0' no treino. "
                        "Recusando ajustar baseline sobre eventos anômalos. "
                        "Verifique o split ou rode com strict=False explicitamente."
                    )
                logging.warning("FeatureProcessor.fit: nenhum evento normal. Usando base total.")
                df_temp = df.copy()
        else:
            df_temp = df.copy()

        logging.info("[FeatureProcessor] Ajustando baseline histórico (APENAS NORMAIS)...")

        # 1. Baseline legado de encoders e temporais
        params = legacy.fit_legacy_state(df_temp)

        # 2. Baselines dos novos pilares (apenas pilares ativos no preset)
        if self.feature_set != PRESET_E0_LEGACY:
            specs = get_specs_for_preset(self.feature_set)
            active_pillars = {s.pillar for s in specs}

            if 2 in active_pillars:
                p2 = fit_pilar2_state(df_temp)
                params.update(p2)

            if 3 in active_pillars:
                p3 = fit_pilar3_state(df_temp)
                params.update(p3)

            if 4 in active_pillars:
                p4 = fit_pilar4_state(df_temp)
                for k in ["seen_user_host", "seen_proc_host", "seen_user_ip", "seen_user_logon"]:
                    if k in p4:
                        self.state.seen_sets[k] = p4.pop(k)
                params.update(p4)

            if 5 in active_pillars:
                p5 = fit_pilar5_state(df_temp)
                params.update(p5)

        self.state.params = params
        self.state.param_hash = compute_param_hash(params)
        self.is_fitted = True
        logging.info("Baseline comportamental estabelecido com sucesso.")

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # TRANSFORM (Puro e Causal)
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    def transform(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if df is None or df.empty:
            return df

        if not self.is_fitted:
            if getattr(self, "strict", True):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "FeatureProcessor.transform() chamado sem .fit() prévio. "
                    "Em inferência, carregue o estado do treino via meta['feature_processor']. "
                    "Em treino, chame .fit(train_df) antes de .transform()."
                )
            logging.warning("FeatureProcessor.transform sem .fit prévio — modo permissivo.")
            self.fit(df)

        df = df.copy()
        df = df.sort_values(["UserID", "Time"], kind="stable").reset_index(drop=True)
        df["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(df["Time"], unit="s")

        available_cols = set(df.columns)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Caminho 1: Preset E0-legacy
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        if self.feature_set == PRESET_E0_LEGACY:
            df = legacy.apply_frequency_encoders(df, self.state.params)

            if not self.features_frozen:
                enabled_groups = set(self.config.get("features", {}).get("enabled_groups", [])) or set(FEATURE_REGISTRY.keys())
                self.active_builders = []
                for name, spec in FEATURE_REGISTRY.items():
                    if name in enabled_groups and set(spec["required"]).issubset(available_cols):
                        self.active_builders.append(spec["builder"])
                self.features_frozen = True

            builder_map = {
                "build_temporal": lambda d: legacy.build_temporal(d, self.state.params),
                "build_auth_failure": lambda d: legacy.build_auth_failure(d, self.rate_window),
                "build_lateral": lambda d: legacy.build_lateral(d, self.state.params, self.rate_window),
                "build_volume": lambda d: legacy.build_volume(d, self.state.params),
                "build_rdp": lambda d: legacy.build_rdp(d, self.rate_window),
                "build_golden_ticket": lambda d: legacy.build_golden_ticket(d),
                "build_process": lambda d: legacy.build_process(d, self.rate_window),
                "build_privilege": lambda d: legacy.build_privilege(d, self.rate_window),
            }
            for b in self.active_builders:
                if b in builder_map:
                    df = builder_map[b](df)

            df = legacy.add_interactions(df)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Caminho 2: Presets Modulares (FE-Core, FE-Advanced, FE-Full, etc.)
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        else:
            specs = get_specs_for_preset(self.feature_set)
            active_specs = [s for s in specs if s.is_active(available_cols)]
            self.unavailable_features = [s.name for s in specs if not s.is_active(available_cols)]

            # Prepara state com os sets
            state_params = dict(self.state.params)
            state_params.update(self.state.seen_sets)

            # Executa builders dos pilares selecionados
            active_pillars = {s.pillar for s in active_specs}

            if 1 in active_pillars:
                df = build_pilar1_identity(df, state_params)
            if 2 in active_pillars and "ProcessName" in df.columns:
                df = build_pilar2_process(df, state_params)
            if 3 in active_pillars:
                df = build_pilar3_auth(df, state_params)
            if 4 in active_pillars:
                df = build_pilar4_topology(df, state_params)
            if 5 in active_pillars:
                df = build_pilar5_temporal(df, state_params)
            if 6 in active_pillars:
                df = build_pilar6_forensic(df, state_params)

            # Higienização: descarta colunas pertencentes a specs indisponíveis
            for unavail_spec in self.unavailable_features:
                for col in _columns_for_spec(unavail_spec):
                    if col in df.columns:
                        df.drop(columns=[col], inplace=True, errors="ignore")

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Seleção Final do Vetor Numérico
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        if not self.final_feature_names:
            if self.feature_set == PRESET_E0_LEGACY:
                self.final_feature_names = [
                    c for c in df.columns
                    if c not in legacy.META_COLS
                    and c not in self.excluded_features
                    and pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(df[c])
                ]
            else:
                specs = get_specs_for_preset(self.feature_set)
                active_specs = [s for s in specs if s.is_active(available_cols)]
                candidate_cols: List[str] = []
                for s in active_specs:
                    candidate_cols.extend(_columns_for_spec(s.name))

                self.final_feature_names = [
                    c for c in candidate_cols
                    if c in df.columns
                    and c not in self.excluded_features
                    and pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(df[c])
                ]
                self.final_feature_names = list(dict.fromkeys(self.final_feature_names))

            self.features_frozen = True
            logging.info(f"Vetor de features CONGELADO: {len(self.final_feature_names)} dimensões.")
        else:
            for c in self.final_feature_names:
                if c not in df.columns:
                    df[c] = 0.0

        if self.output_dir:
            os.makedirs(self.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
            meta = FeatureMetadata(
                active_features=list(self.final_feature_names),
                unavailable_features=list(self.unavailable_features),
                feature_set=self.feature_set,
                feature_version=self.state.feature_version,
                param_hash=self.state.param_hash,
                fitted_at=self.state.fitted_at,
            )
            with open(os.path.join(self.output_dir, "feature_metadata.pkl"), "wb") as f:
                pickle.dump(meta.to_dict(), f)

        keep_cols = self.final_feature_names + ["UserID", "Time", "Is_Anomaly", "AnomalyType"]
        keep_cols = list(dict.fromkeys(keep_cols))
        return df[[c for c in keep_cols if c in df.columns]].fillna(0)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # UPDATE (Online)
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    def update(self, event: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Atualiza o estado causal S_{t-} no modo online."""
        if not event or "Time" not in event:
            return
        t_event = float(event["Time"])
        user = str(event.get("UserID", ""))
        host = str(event.get("LogHost", ""))
        if user and host and "seen_user_host" in self.state.seen_sets:
            self.state.seen_sets["seen_user_host"].add(f"{user}@{host}", t_event)

# =====================================================================
# 2. ESPECIFICAÇÕES DAS FEATURES DA NOVA ESPECIFICAÇÃO (PILARES 1 A 6)

    FeatureSpec("module_from_user_writable", 6, ("ModulePath",), (), "endpoint", 1, "pilar6_forensic", "P6-F10", None, "none", "static"),
    FeatureSpec("module_first_seen_on_host", 6, ("Time", "ModulePath", "LogHost"), (), "endpoint", 1, "pilar6_forensic", "P6-F11", 2592000.0, "online", "causal"),
    FeatureSpec("module_unsigned_or_unknown", 6, ("ModulePath", "SignatureStatus"), (), "endpoint", 1, "pilar6_forensic", "P6-F12", None, "none", "static", degraded_mode=None),
]


def get_specs_for_preset(preset_name: str) -> List[FeatureSpec]:
    """Retorna a lista de FeatureSpec associadas ao preset solicitado."""
    canon = normalize_preset_name(preset_name)
    if canon == PRESET_E0_LEGACY:
        return list(E0_SPECS)
    elif canon == PRESET_FE_CORE:
        # Pilares 1 a 5 sem estimadores complexos
        complex_names = {"hawkes_z", "temporal_surprisal", "lineage_surprisal"}
        return [s for s in SPEC_PILLAR_FEATURES if s.pillar in {1, 2, 3, 4, 5} and s.name not in complex_names]
    elif canon == PRESET_FE_ADVANCED:
        # Pilares 1 a 5 completos
        return [s for s in SPEC_PILLAR_FEATURES if s.pillar in {1, 2, 3, 4, 5}]
    elif canon == PRESET_FE_FULL:
        # Pilares 1 a 6 completos
        return list(SPEC_PILLAR_FEATURES)
    elif canon == PRESET_FE_FULL_NO_DETERMINISTIC:
        # Pilares 1 a 6 sem acumuladores estáticos
        return [s for s in SPEC_PILLAR_FEATURES if s.anti_leakage != "static" or s.formula_ref not in {"E0-LM2", "E0-LM5", "E0-VO1"}]
    else:
        # Default de segurança: E0-legacy
        return list(E0_SPECS)

from typing import Set, Dict, List, Any

PRESET_E0_LEGACY = "E0-legacy"

PRESET_DEFINITIONS: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {
    PRESET_E0_LEGACY: {
        "description": "Baseline histórico com 38 features legadas e encoders offline.",
        "mode": "legacy",
        "pillars": set(),
        "include_legacy": True,
    },
    PRESET_FE_CORE: {
        "description": "Núcleo comportamental dos Pilares 1 a 5 sem estimadores iterativos complexos.",
        "mode": "modular",
        "pillars": {1, 2, 3, 4, 5},
        "include_legacy": False,
        "exclude_complex_estimators": True,
    },
    PRESET_FE_ADVANCED: {
        "description": "Pilares 1 a 5 com estimadores probabilísticos avançados (Hawkes, Von Mises, JM).",
        "mode": "modular",
        "pillars": {1, 2, 3, 4, 5},
        "include_legacy": False,
        "exclude_complex_estimators": False,
    },
    PRESET_FE_FULL: {

        "mode": "modular",
        "pillars": {1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6},
        "include_legacy": False,

    },
    PRESET_FE_FULL_NO_DETERMINISTIC: {
        "description": "FE-Full estritamente causal, excluindo acumuladores determinísticos de partição.",
        "mode": "modular",
        "pillars": {1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6},
        "include_legacy": False,
        "exclude_deterministic": True,
    },
}


def normalize_preset_name(name: str) -> str:
    """Normaliza o nome do preset para chave canônica."""
    if not name:
        return PRESET_E0_LEGACY
    s = name.strip()
    lookup = {
        "e0": PRESET_E0_LEGACY,
        "e0-legacy": PRESET_E0_LEGACY,
        "legacy": PRESET_E0_LEGACY,
        "fe-core": PRESET_FE_CORE,
        "core": PRESET_FE_CORE,
        "fe-advanced": PRESET_FE_ADVANCED,
        "advanced": PRESET_FE_ADVANCED,
        "fe-full": PRESET_FE_FULL,
        "full": PRESET_FE_FULL,
        "fe-full-no-deterministic": PRESET_FE_FULL_NO_DETERMINISTIC,
        "no-deterministic": PRESET_FE_FULL_NO_DETERMINISTIC,
    }
    return lookup.get(s.lower(), s)

# core/features/spec.py
#
# Contrato formal FeatureSpec e estruturas de metadados para o pipeline UEBA.

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Tuple, Union, Callable, Optional, Literal, List, Set, Dict, Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FeatureSpec:
    """
    Especificação formal de uma feature comportamental UEBA.
    
    Campos:
    - name: Nome canônico da feature ou coluna base.
    - pillar: Pilar conceitual da especificação (1 a 6; ou 0 para auxiliares/legado).
    - required: Tupla com nomes de colunas estritamente obrigatórias para ativação.
    - optional: Tupla com colunas adicionais para enriquecimento.
    - source: Origem dos dados ("endpoint", "security_log", "both").
    - dims: Dimensão escalar gerada (int) ou função callable para cálculo dinâmico.
    - builder: Nome do método ou módulo construtor.
    - formula_ref: Referência formal da fórmula na especificação metodológica.
    - half_life: Meia-vida em segundos para contadores decaídos (se aplicável).
    - fit_strategy: Estratégia de ajuste ("offline", "online", "none").
    - anti_leakage: Garantia temporal ("static", "causal", "offline_params").
    - degraded_mode: Comportamento quando colunas opcionais faltam ou None se ausente.
    """
    name: str
    pillar: int
    required: Tuple[str, ...]
    optional: Tuple[str, ...] = ()
    source: Literal["endpoint", "security_log", "both"] = "security_log"
    dims: Union[int, Callable] = 1
    builder: str = ""
    formula_ref: str = ""
    half_life: Optional[float] = None
    fit_strategy: Literal["offline", "online", "none"] = "offline"
    anti_leakage: Literal["static", "causal", "offline_params"] = "causal"
    degraded_mode: Optional[str] = None

    def is_active(self, available_columns: Set[str]) -> bool:
        """Uma feature só é ativada se todas as suas colunas required existirem."""
        return set(self.required).issubset(available_columns)


@dataclass
class FeatureMetadata:
    """Metadados completos do pipeline de features congelados pós-fit/transform."""
    active_features: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    unavailable_features: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    feature_set: str = "FE-Full"
    feature_version: str = "1.0.0"
    param_hash: str = ""
    fitted_at: str = ""
    specs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "active_features": list(self.active_features),
            "unavailable_features": list(self.unavailable_features),
            "feature_set": self.feature_set,
            "feature_version": self.feature_version,
            "param_hash": self.param_hash,
            "fitted_at": self.fitted_at,
            "specs": list(self.specs),
        }


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\features\state.py
################################################################################

# core/features/state.py
#
# Representação serializável e versionada do estado causal S_{t-}.
# Contrato: transform(df) NUNCA muta FeatureState. Toda mutação ocorre em fit() ou update(event).

import json
import hashlib
import pickle
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from core.features.decay import LazyDecayCounter, LazyDecaySet


def compute_param_hash(params: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Calcula hash determinístico SHA-256 dos parâmetros aprendidos."""
    try:
        # Serialização determinística com sort_keys e tratamento padrão de tipos
        encoded = json.dumps(params, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode("utf-8")
    except Exception:
        # Fallback para pickle se houver objetos complexos não serializáveis em JSON
        encoded = pickle.dumps(params, protocol=4)
    return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


@dataclass
class FeatureState:
    """
    Estado causal do sistema de extração de features no instante anterior S_{t-}.
    
    Campos:
    - counters: Dicionário de contadores causais com decaimento exponencial.
    - seen_sets: Dicionário de conjuntos causais com TTL.
    - params: Parâmetros e baselines aprendidos offline no fit().
    - feature_version: Versão do pipeline de features.
    - param_hash: SHA-256 dos parâmetros aprendidos.
    - fitted_at: Timestamp ISO 8601 do momento do ajuste.
    """
    counters: Dict[str, LazyDecayCounter] = field(default_factory=dict)
    seen_sets: Dict[str, LazyDecaySet] = field(default_factory=dict)
    params: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    feature_version: str = "1.0.0"
    param_hash: str = ""
    fitted_at: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not self.fitted_at:
            self.fitted_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        if not self.param_hash and self.params:
            self.param_hash = compute_param_hash(self.params)

    def serialize(self) -> bytes:
        """
        Serializa o FeatureState em bytes para armazenamento persistente
        (compatível com pickle / trainer_meta.pkl).
        """
        payload = {
            "counters": {
                k: (v.snapshot(), v.half_life_seconds)
                for k, v in self.counters.items()
            },
            "seen_sets": {
                k: v.snapshot()
                for k, v in self.seen_sets.items()
            },
            "params": self.params,
            "feature_version": self.feature_version,
            "param_hash": self.param_hash,
            "fitted_at": self.fitted_at,
        }
        return pickle.dumps(payload, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)

    @classmethod
    def deserialize(cls, blob: bytes) -> "FeatureState":
        """Reconstrói uma instância de FeatureState a partir do blob de bytes."""
        payload = pickle.loads(blob)
        
        counters = {
            k: LazyDecayCounter.from_snapshot(snap, half_life)
            for k, (snap, half_life) in payload.get("counters", {}).items()
        }
        seen_sets = {
            k: LazyDecaySet.from_snapshot(data)
            for k, data in payload.get("seen_sets", {}).items()
        }
        
        return cls(
            counters=counters,
            seen_sets=seen_sets,
            params=payload.get("params", {}),
            feature_version=payload.get("feature_version", "1.0.0"),
            param_hash=payload.get("param_hash", ""),
            fitted_at=payload.get("fitted_at", ""),
        )

    def clone(self) -> "FeatureState":
        """Retorna uma cópia desacoplada do estado atual."""
        return self.deserialize(self.serialize())


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\models\autoencoder.py
################################################################################

##### ✅✅✅✅✅ core/models/autoencoder_model.py
#NOVO MODELO DO AUTOENCODER
import os
import random
import numpy as np
import tensorflow as tf
from tensorflow import keras
from tensorflow.keras import layers

from core.models.base_model import BaseModel


class AutoencoderModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Autoencoder padrão baseado em MLP (Keras/TensorFlow).
    Adaptado para aceitar tanto tensores 3D de sequências temporais (achatando-os)
    quanto tensores 2D convencionais.
    """

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)

        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("autoencoder", {})
        train_cfg = config.get("training", {})

        self.layers_config = model_cfg.get("layers", [64, 32, 16])
        self.activation = model_cfg.get("activation", "relu")
        self.dropout = model_cfg.get("dropout", 0.0)

        self.epochs = train_cfg.get("epochs", 20)
        self.batch_size = train_cfg.get("batch_size", 256)
        self.learning_rate = train_cfg.get("learning_rate", 1e-3)
        self.validation_split = train_cfg.get("validation_split", 0.1)
        self.patience = train_cfg.get("early_stopping_patience", 5)

        self.seed = train_cfg.get("seed", 42)
        self.feature_weights = train_cfg.get("feature_weights", None)

        self.model = None
        self.input_dim = None

    # =====================================================
    # REPRODUTIBILIDADE
    # =====================================================

    def _set_seed(self, seed):
        os.environ["PYTHONHASHSEED"] = str(seed)
        random.seed(seed)
        np.random.seed(seed)
        tf.keras.utils.set_random_seed(seed)
        try:
            tf.config.experimental.enable_op_determinism()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # =====================================================
    # CONSTRUÇÃO DO MODELO
    # =====================================================

    def _build_model(self, input_dim):
        if len(self.layers_config) < 2:
            raise ValueError(
                "O Autoencoder requer pelo menos duas camadas (encoder + bottleneck)"
            )

        inputs = keras.Input(shape=(input_dim,))
        x = inputs

        # Encoder
        for units in self.layers_config:
            x = layers.Dense(units, activation=self.activation)(x)
            if self.dropout > 0:
                x = layers.Dropout(self.dropout)(x)

        # Decoder
        for units in reversed(self.layers_config[:-1]):
            x = layers.Dense(units, activation=self.activation)(x)

        outputs = layers.Dense(input_dim, activation="linear")(x)

        model = keras.Model(inputs, outputs)
        optimizer = keras.optimizers.Adam(learning_rate=self.learning_rate)
        model.compile(optimizer=optimizer, loss="mse")

        return model

    # =====================================================
    # TREINAMENTO
    # =====================================================

    def fit(self, X):
        self._set_seed(self.seed)
        X = np.asarray(X).astype(np.float32)

        # Trata entrada 3D do TemporalBuilder (n_samples, sequence_length, n_features)
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)

        self.input_dim = X.shape[1]
        self.model = self._build_model(self.input_dim)

        callbacks = []
        if self.patience > 0:
            callbacks.append(
                keras.callbacks.EarlyStopping(
                    monitor="val_loss",
                    patience=self.patience,
                    restore_best_weights=True
                )
            )

        self.model.fit(
            X,
            X,
            epochs=self.epochs,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            shuffle=True,
            validation_split=self.validation_split,
            verbose=0,
            callbacks=callbacks
        )

    # =====================================================
    # CÁLCULO DE SCORE DE ANOMALIA
    # =====================================================

    def score(self, X):
        X = np.asarray(X).astype(np.float32)

        # Trata entrada 3D do TemporalBuilder
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)

        recon = self.model.predict(
            X,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            verbose=0
        )

        errors = (X - recon) ** 2

        if self.feature_weights is not None:
            weights = np.asarray(self.feature_weights)
            if weights.shape[0] != errors.shape[1]:
                raise ValueError(
                    "O tamanho de feature_weights deve bater com o número de features"
                )
            errors = errors * weights

        scores = np.mean(errors, axis=1)

        # Contrato: valores maiores significam maior anomalia
        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores, invert=False)

    def save(self, path): 
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        self.model.save(os.path.join(path, "model.keras"))

    def load(self, path):
        self.model = keras.models.load_model(os.path.join(path, "model.keras"), compile=False)

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\models\base_model.py
################################################################################


##### ✅✅✅✅✅ core/models/base_model.py
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
import numpy as np


class BaseModel(ABC):
    """
    Classe base para todos os modelos de detecção de anomalia.

    CONTRATO OBRIGATÓRIO:
    - fit(X)
    - score(X) → retorna anomaly score (quanto maior, mais anômalo)
    """

    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config

    # =====================================================
    # TRAIN
    # =====================================================

    @abstractmethod
    def fit(self, X: np.ndarray):
        """
        Treina o modelo.

        Parâmetros:
            X (np.ndarray): dados de treino (apenas normais idealmente)
        """
        pass

    # =====================================================
    # SCORE
    # =====================================================

    @abstractmethod
    def score(self, X: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Retorna anomaly scores.

        REGRA GLOBAL (CRÍTICA):
        - scores maiores ⇒ MAIS ANOMALIA

        Isso padroniza todos os modelos e permite:
        - threshold único
        - comparação justa
        - cálculo consistente de PR-AUC

        Retorna:
            np.ndarray shape (n_samples,)
        """
        pass

    # =====================================================
    # PREDICT (PADRONIZAÇÃO)
    # =====================================================

    def predict(self, X: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Garante que qualquer chamada legado usando .predict() 
        retorne exatamente o mesmo que .score(), mantendo a 
        consistência da interface em todo o pipeline.
        """
        return self.score(X)

    # =====================================================
    # OPTIONAL: FIT + SCORE (para modelos que precisam)
    # =====================================================

    def fit_score(self, X: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Alguns modelos podem querer treinar e já retornar score.
        (opcional, não obrigatório)
        """
        self.fit(X)
        return self.score(X)

    # =====================================================
    # UTIL: GARANTIR FORMATO DE SAÍDA
    # =====================================================

    def _ensure_1d(self, scores):
        """
        Garante que scores sejam vetor 1D
        """
        scores = np.asarray(scores)

        if scores.ndim > 1:
            scores = scores.reshape(-1)

        return scores

    # =====================================================
    # UTIL: NORMALIZAR DIREÇÃO DO SCORE
    # =====================================================

    def _ensure_higher_is_anomaly(self, scores, invert=False):
        """
        Alguns modelos (ex: sklearn) retornam:
        - valores maiores = mais normal

        Este método padroniza:
        - valores maiores = mais anômalo

        Parâmetros:
            invert (bool): se True, inverte o sinal
        """
        scores = self._ensure_1d(scores)

        if invert:
            scores = -scores

        return scores

# ARQUIVO: core\models\ensemble.py
################################################################################

import pickle
import os
import numpy as np
from sklearn.preprocessing import RobustScaler
import importlib
from core.models.base_model import BaseModel

class EnsembleModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Detetor de Anomalias Híbrido (Esquadrão).
    
    Esta versão opera na estrutura de pastas plana e utiliza o RobustScaler
    para garantir que nenhum modelo domine a votação devido a outliers.
    """
    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.config = config
        
        # IMPORT DINÂMICO PARA EVITAR RECURSÃO CIRCULAR
        # Como ModelFactory importa Ensemble, Ensemble deve importar Factory dentro do método.
        try:
            module = importlib.import_module("core.models.model_factory")
            ModelFactory = getattr(module, "ModelFactory")
        except (ImportError, AttributeError) as e:
            raise ImportError(f"❌ Erro crítico ao recrutar o Esquadrão: Não foi possível carregar a ModelFactory. {e}")

        ens_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("ensemble", {})
        model_names = ens_cfg.get("models", [])
        self.weights = ens_cfg.get("weights", [])

        if not model_names:
            raise ValueError("❌ O modelo 'ensemble' requer uma lista de 'models' no config.yaml.")

        # Garantia de pesos equilibrados, caso não definidos
        if not self.weights or len(self.weights) != len(model_names):
            self.weights = [1.0 / len(model_names)] * len(model_names)

        self.models = []
        self.scalers = [] 
        
        print("\n🤝 [ENSEMBLE] Recrutando Esquadrão (Estrutura Plana)...")
        for name in model_names:
            # Cria uma cópia da configuração com alteração apenas do tipo para o sub-modelo
            sub_config = config.copy()
            sub_config["model"] = config["model"].copy()
            sub_config["model"]["type"] = name
            
            try:
                m = ModelFactory.get_model(sub_config)
                self.models.append(m)
                # RobustScaler: Essencial para manter a democracia entre modelos heterogêneos
                self.scalers.append(RobustScaler())
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"⚠️ [ENSEMBLE] Falha ao recrutar sub-modelo '{name}': {e}")

        if not self.models:
            raise RuntimeError("❌ O Esquadrão não conseguiu recrutar nenhum modelo válido.")

    def fit(self, X):
        """
        Treina todos os membros do esquadrão sequencialmente.
        """
        X_arr = np.asarray(X)
        for i, model in enumerate(self.models):
            print(f"🏋️ [ENSEMBLE] Treinando sub-modelo {i+1}/{len(self.models)}...")
            model.fit(X_arr)
            
            # Calibra o scaler do esquadrão com os scores do treino (baseline de normalidade)
            raw_scores = model.score(X_arr)
            self.scalers[i].fit(raw_scores.reshape(-1, 1))

    def score(self, X):
        """
        Combina os votos de todos os modelos usando média ponderada normalizada.
        """
        X_arr = np.asarray(X)
        final_scores = np.zeros(len(X_arr))
        
        for i, model in enumerate(self.models):
            raw_scores = model.score(X_arr)
            
            # Normalização: impede que um erro grande de um modelo
            # anule a opinião dos outros membros do esquadrão.
            scaled_scores = self.scalers[i].transform(raw_scores.reshape(-1, 1)).flatten()
            
            # Aplicação do peso (votação ponderada)
            final_scores += scaled_scores * self.weights[i]
            
        # Garante que a saída siga o contrato: Maior = Mais Anômalo
        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(final_scores)
    
    def save(self, path):
        """
        Salva os sub-modelos e os scalers de normalização.
        """
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        for i, model in enumerate(self.models):
            if hasattr(model, "save"):
                sub_path = os.path.join(path, f"sub_model_{i}")
                model.save(sub_path)
                
        # Salva os scalers do esquadrão
        with open(os.path.join(path, "ensemble_scalers.pkl"), "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(self.scalers, f)

    def load(self, path):
        """
        Carrega os sub-modelos e os scalers.
        """
        for i, model in enumerate(self.models):
            if hasattr(model, "load"):
                sub_path = os.path.join(path, f"sub_model_{i}")
                model.load(sub_path)
                
        # Carrega os scalers
        scaler_file = os.path.join(path, "ensemble_scalers.pkl")
        if os.path.exists(scaler_file):
            with open(scaler_file, "rb") as f:
                self.scalers = pickle.load(f)




################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\models\isolation_forest.py
################################################################################

import os
from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest
import numpy as np
from core.models.base_model import BaseModel
import pickle

class IsolationForestModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Isolation Forest wrapper otimizado para detecção de anomalias.
    
    Tem a capacidade de processar janelas temporais (3D) do workflow atual.
    """
    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)
        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("isolation_forest", {})
        
        # Parâmetros originais para controle total
        self.n_estimators = model_cfg.get("n_estimators", 200)
        self.max_samples = model_cfg.get("max_samples", "auto")
        self.contamination = model_cfg.get("contamination", "auto")
        self.max_features = model_cfg.get("max_features", 1.0)
        self.bootstrap = model_cfg.get("bootstrap", False)
        self.n_jobs = model_cfg.get("n_jobs", -1)
        self.random_state = config.get("training", {}).get("seed", 42)

        self.model = IsolationForest(
            n_estimators=self.n_estimators,
            max_samples=self.max_samples,
            contamination=self.contamination,
            max_features=self.max_features,
            bootstrap=self.bootstrap,
            n_jobs=self.n_jobs,
            random_state=self.random_state
        )

    def fit(self, X):
        # Conversão defensiva para numpy
        X = np.asarray(X)
        
        # INTELIGÊNCIA DE PIPELINE: Isolation Forest espera (n_samples, n_features).
        # Se os dados forem 3D (janelas), faz o flattening automático.
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)
            
        self.model.fit(X)

    def score(self, X):
        X = np.asarray(X)
        
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)
            
        # sklearn: decision_function retorna valores onde quanto menor, mais anômalo.
        # Nosso contrato exige: scores maiores = MAIS ANOMALIA.
        scores = self.model.decision_function(X)
        
        # Padronização via BaseModel (invert=True para garantir a direção correta)
        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores, invert=True)

    # =====================================================
    # SALVAR E CARREGAR MODELO
    # =====================================================
    def save(self, path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(self.model, f)

    def load(self, path):
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "rb") as f:
            self.model = pickle.load(f)

# ARQUIVO: core\models\lof.py
################################################################################

# core/models/lof_model.py
#NOVO MODELO DO LOF
import os
import pickle
import numpy as np
from sklearn.neighbors import LocalOutlierFactor

from core.models.base_model import BaseModel


class LOFModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Wrapper do Local Outlier Factor (LOF) adaptado para o ecossistema do projeto.
    Dá suporte a inferência em dados novos (test set) através de novelty=True
    e suporta o workflow temporal 3D com realização de achatamento dimensional.
    """

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)

        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("lof", {})

        self.n_neighbors = model_cfg.get("n_neighbors", 20)
        self.algorithm = model_cfg.get("algorithm", "auto")
        self.leaf_size = model_cfg.get("leaf_size", 30)
        self.metric = model_cfg.get("metric", "minkowski")
        self.p = model_cfg.get("p", 2)
        self.n_jobs = model_cfg.get("n_jobs", -1)
        
        # Valor estático de contaminação para evitar avisos de deprecabilidade
        self.contamination = model_cfg.get("contamination", 0.1)

        self.model = None

    # =====================================================
    # TREINAMENTO
    # =====================================================

    def fit(self, X):
        X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64)

        # Suporte para o tensor 3D de janelas temporais
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)

        self.model = LocalOutlierFactor(
            n_neighbors=self.n_neighbors,
            algorithm=self.algorithm,
            leaf_size=self.leaf_size,
            metric=self.metric,
            p=self.p,
            n_jobs=self.n_jobs,
            contamination=self.contamination,
            novelty=True  # Habilita a predição de dados fora da amostra original de treino
        )

        self.model.fit(X)

    # =====================================================
    # CÁLCULO DE SCORE DE ANOMALIA
    # =====================================================

    def score(self, X):
        X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64)

        # Suporte para o tensor 3D de janelas temporais
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)

        if self.model is None:
            raise ValueError("O modelo LOF deve ser treinado antes do cálculo de scores.")

        # O decision_function do Scikit-Learn retorna valores negativos onde quanto menor, mais anômalo
        scores = self.model.decision_function(X)

        # Inverte o sinal e segue o contrato BaseModel (maior valor = mais anômalo)
        scores = self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores, invert=True)

        return scores

    # =====================================================
    # SALVAR E CARREGAR MODELO
    # =====================================================
    def save(self, path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(self.model, f)

    def load(self, path):
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "rb") as f:
            self.model = pickle.load(f)

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\models\lstm_autoencoder.py
################################################################################

import os
import numpy as np
import tensorflow as tf
from tensorflow import keras
from tensorflow.keras import layers
from core.models.base_model import BaseModel

class LSTMAutoencoder(BaseModel):
    """
    Autoencoder que usa redes recorrentes LSTM.
    HERANÇA E CONTRATO DEFINITIVOS (Compatível com GPUs genéricas/Apple Silicon).
    """
    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.config = config

        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("lstm_autoencoder", {})
        train_cfg = config.get("training", {})

        self.latent_dim = model_cfg.get("latent_dim", 32)
        self.lstm_units = model_cfg.get("lstm_units", [64, 32])
        self.dropout = model_cfg.get("dropout", 0.1)

        self.epochs = train_cfg.get("epochs", 20)
        self.batch_size = train_cfg.get("batch_size", 256)
        self.learning_rate = train_cfg.get("learning_rate", 1e-3)
        self.validation_split = train_cfg.get("validation_split", 0.1)
        self.patience = train_cfg.get("early_stopping_patience", 5)
        self.seed = train_cfg.get("seed", 42)

        self.model = None
        self.encoder = None
        self.timesteps = None
        self.n_features = None

    def _set_seed(self):
        os.environ["PYTHONHASHSEED"] = str(self.seed)
        np.random.seed(self.seed)
        tf.random.set_seed(self.seed)

        try:
            tf.config.experimental.enable_op_determinism()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _build(self):
        inputs = keras.Input(shape=(self.timesteps, self.n_features))
        x = inputs

        # ENCODER
        for units in self.lstm_units[:-1]:
            # Substituído layers.LSTM por layers.RNN(layers.LSTMCell) para compatibilidade universal de GPU
            x = layers.RNN(layers.LSTMCell(units), return_sequences=True)(x)
            if self.dropout > 0:
                x = layers.Dropout(self.dropout)(x)

        # Última camada do Encoder
        # Substituído layers.LSTM por layers.RNN(layers.LSTMCell) para compatibilidade universal de GPU
        x = layers.RNN(layers.LSTMCell(self.lstm_units[-1]), return_sequences=False)(x)
        latent = layers.Dense(self.latent_dim, activation="linear", name="latent")(x)

        # REPETIDOR DE VETOR
        x = layers.RepeatVector(self.timesteps)(latent)

        # DECODER
        for units in reversed(self.lstm_units):
            # Substituído layers.LSTM por layers.RNN(layers.LSTMCell) para compatibilidade universal de GPU
            x = layers.RNN(layers.LSTMCell(units), return_sequences=True)(x)
            if self.dropout > 0:
                x = layers.Dropout(self.dropout)(x)

        outputs = layers.TimeDistributed(
            layers.Dense(self.n_features, activation="linear")
        )(x)

        self.model = keras.Model(inputs, outputs)
        self.encoder = keras.Model(inputs, latent)

        optimizer = keras.optimizers.Adam(learning_rate=self.learning_rate)

        self.model.compile(
            optimizer=optimizer,
            loss="mse"
        )

    def fit(self, X):
        self._set_seed()
        X = np.asarray(X).astype(np.float32)

        if X.ndim != 3:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected 3D input, got {X.shape}")

        if len(X) == 0:
            raise ValueError("Empty training data")

        # Keras takes validation_split from the end before applying shuffle.
        # Shuffle deterministically so the internal holdout spans benign users and times.
        X = X[np.random.default_rng(self.seed).permutation(len(X))]

        self.timesteps = X.shape[1]
        self.n_features = X.shape[2]

        self._build()

        callbacks = []

        if self.patience > 0:
            callbacks.append(
                keras.callbacks.EarlyStopping(
                    monitor="val_loss",
                    patience=self.patience,
                    restore_best_weights=True
                )
            )

        self.model.fit(
            X, X,
            epochs=self.epochs,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            shuffle=True,
            validation_split=self.validation_split,
            verbose=0,
            callbacks=callbacks
        )

    def encode(self, X):
        X = np.asarray(X).astype(np.float32)
        return self.encoder.predict(
            X,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            verbose=0
        )

    def score(self, X):
        """
        Padronização Absoluta: Retorna vetor 1D com direção de anomalia normalizada.
        """
        X = np.asarray(X).astype(np.float32)

        recon = self.model.predict(
            X,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            verbose=0
        )

        errors = (X - recon) ** 2
        scores = np.mean(errors, axis=(1, 2))
        
        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores)

    def save(self, path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        self.model.save(os.path.join(path, "model.keras"))
        self.encoder.save(os.path.join(path, "encoder.keras"))

    def load(self, path):
        self.model = keras.models.load_model(
            os.path.join(path, "model.keras"),
            compile=False
        )
        self.encoder = keras.models.load_model(
            os.path.join(path, "encoder.keras"),
            compile=False
        )


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\models\mlp_autoencoder.py
################################################################################


##### ✅✅✅✅✅ core/models/base_model.py
import os
import numpy as np
import tensorflow as tf
from tensorflow import keras
from tensorflow.keras import layers

from core.models.base_model import BaseModel

class MLPAutoencoder(BaseModel):
    """
    Autoencoder MLP otimizado. 
    Inclui BatchNormalization e Gradient Clipping.
    Herda de BaseModel e utiliza a interface padronizada de score.
    """
    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.config = config
        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("mlp_autoencoder", {})
        train_cfg = config.get("training", {})

        self.latent_dim = model_cfg.get("latent_dim", 16)
        self.dense_units = model_cfg.get("dense_units", [128, 64])
        self.dropout = model_cfg.get("dropout", 0.1)

        self.epochs = train_cfg.get("epochs", 20)
        self.batch_size = train_cfg.get("batch_size", 256)
        self.learning_rate = train_cfg.get("learning_rate", 1e-3)
        self.validation_split = train_cfg.get("validation_split", 0.1)
        self.patience = train_cfg.get("early_stopping_patience", 5)
        self.seed = train_cfg.get("seed", 42)

        self.model = None
        self.encoder = None

    def _set_seed(self):
        os.environ["PYTHONHASHSEED"] = str(self.seed)
        np.random.seed(self.seed)
        tf.random.set_seed(self.seed)

    def _build(self):
        inputs = keras.Input(shape=(self.timesteps, self.n_features))
        
        # Achata a janela temporal para passar no MLP
        x = layers.Flatten()(inputs)

        # Encoder
        for units in self.dense_units:
            x = layers.Dense(units, activation="relu")(x)
            # INTELIGÊNCIA ADAPTADA: BatchNormalization estabiliza os pesos
            x = layers.BatchNormalization()(x) 
            if self.dropout > 0:
                x = layers.Dropout(self.dropout)(x)

        latent = layers.Dense(self.latent_dim, activation="linear", name="latent")(x)

        # Decoder
        x = latent
        for units in reversed(self.dense_units):
            x = layers.Dense(units, activation="relu")(x)
            # INTELIGÊNCIA ADAPTADA: BatchNormalization no decodificador
            x = layers.BatchNormalization()(x)
            if self.dropout > 0:
                x = layers.Dropout(self.dropout)(x)

        # Reconstrução
        flat_output_dim = self.timesteps * self.n_features
        x = layers.Dense(flat_output_dim, activation="linear")(x)
        outputs = layers.Reshape((self.timesteps, self.n_features))(x)

        self.model = keras.Model(inputs, outputs)
        self.encoder = keras.Model(inputs, latent)

        # INTELIGÊNCIA ADAPTADA: clipnorm=1.0 para evitar explosão de gradiente
        self.model.compile(
            optimizer=keras.optimizers.Adam(
                learning_rate=self.learning_rate, 
                clipnorm=1.0
            ),
            loss="mse"
        )

    def fit(self, X):
        self._set_seed()
        X = np.asarray(X).astype(np.float32)
        
        # Keras takes validation_split from the end before applying shuffle.
        # Shuffle deterministically so the internal holdout spans benign users and times.
        X = X[np.random.default_rng(self.seed).permutation(len(X))]

        self.timesteps = X.shape[1]
        self.n_features = X.shape[2]

        self._build()

        callbacks = []
        if self.patience > 0:
            callbacks.append(
                keras.callbacks.EarlyStopping(monitor="val_loss", patience=self.patience, restore_best_weights=True)
            )

        # Ajuste: verbose=1 para exibir a barra de progresso no terminal
        self.model.fit(
            X, X,
            epochs=self.epochs,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            shuffle=True,
            validation_split=self.validation_split,
            verbose=1,
            callbacks=callbacks
        )

    # =========================================================
    # SCORE (Interface Padronizada)
    # =========================================================
    def score(self, X):
        """
        Substitui o antigo predict() incorreto. Calcula a perda (MSE) 
        entre a entrada e a reconstrução e devolve como anomaly score 1D.
        """
        X = np.asarray(X).astype(np.float32)
        recon = self.model.predict(X, batch_size=self.batch_size, verbose=0)
        errors = (X - recon) ** 2
        scores = np.mean(errors, axis=(1, 2))
        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores)

    def save(self, path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        # Atualizado para formato nativo do Keras para evitar warnings
        self.model.save(os.path.join(path, "model.keras"))
        self.encoder.save(os.path.join(path, "encoder.keras"))

    def load(self, path):
        self.model = keras.models.load_model(os.path.join(path, "model.keras"), compile=False)
        self.encoder = keras.models.load_model(os.path.join(path, "encoder.keras"), compile=False)



################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\models\mlp_single.py
################################################################################

# core/models/mlp_single.py
# CORRIGIDO: Representação da janela agora usa 6 estatísticas (last, mean, std, min, max, sum)
# Baseado nos resultados do diagnóstico: Teste 3 (janela estatística) atingiu PR-AUC 0.96.
# Isso substitui a antiga representação (last + mean) que perdia sinal.
import os
import pickle
import numpy as np
from sklearn.neural_network import MLPRegressor
import warnings

from core.models.base_model import BaseModel


class MLPSingleModel(BaseModel):
    """
    MLP Single (Otimizado para CPU usando Scikit-Learn).
    Versão corrigida: expande a janela temporal para 6 estatísticas por feature:
      - último evento
      - média
      - desvio padrão
      - mínimo
      - máximo
      - soma
    Entrada: sequence_length (20) × 16 features → 96 features de entrada.
    """

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)

        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("mlp_single", {})

        hidden_layer_sizes = model_cfg.get("hidden_layer_sizes", (32,))
        if isinstance(hidden_layer_sizes, list):
            hidden_layer_sizes = tuple(hidden_layer_sizes)

        self.hidden_layer_sizes = hidden_layer_sizes
        self.activation = model_cfg.get("activation", "relu")
        self.solver = model_cfg.get("solver", "adam")
        self.alpha = model_cfg.get("alpha", 0.0001)
        self.batch_size = model_cfg.get("batch_size", "auto")
        self.learning_rate_init = model_cfg.get("learning_rate_init", 0.001)
        self.max_iter = model_cfg.get("max_iter", 200)
        self.early_stopping = model_cfg.get("early_stopping", True)
        self.validation_fraction = model_cfg.get("validation_fraction", 0.1)
        self.random_state = config.get("training", {}).get("seed", 42)

        self.model = None

    # =====================================================
    # PREPARAÇÃO DIMENSIONAL (CORRIGIDA)
    # =====================================================
    def _prepare_data(self, X):
        """
        Converte a matriz 3D do TemporalBuilder (janelas) em uma representação
        2D enriquecida com 6 estatísticas por feature.
        Antes: último evento + média (32 features)
        Agora:  último + média + std + min + max + soma (96 features)
        """
        X_arr = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float32)

        if X_arr.ndim == 3:
            # (n_samples, sequence_length, n_features)
            X_last = X_arr[:, -1, :]                  # último evento
            X_mean = np.mean(X_arr, axis=1)           # média
            X_std = np.std(X_arr, axis=1)             # desvio padrão
            X_min = np.min(X_arr, axis=1)             # mínimo
            X_max = np.max(X_arr, axis=1)             # máximo
            X_sum = np.sum(X_arr, axis=1)             # soma

            return np.hstack([X_last, X_mean, X_std, X_min, X_max, X_sum])

        return X_arr

    # =====================================================
    # TREINAMENTO
    # =====================================================
    #def fit(self, X): aqui seria para nunca aceitar treinar com dados de anomalia
    def fit(self, X, y=None, **kwargs): #aqui deixei passar
        X_processed = self._prepare_data(X)

        self.model = MLPRegressor(
            hidden_layer_sizes=self.hidden_layer_sizes,
            activation=self.activation,
            solver=self.solver,
            alpha=self.alpha,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            learning_rate_init=self.learning_rate_init,
            max_iter=self.max_iter,
            early_stopping=self.early_stopping,
            validation_fraction=self.validation_fraction,
            random_state=self.random_state
        )

        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
            self.model.fit(X_processed, X_processed)

    # =====================================================
    # CÁLCULO DE SCORE DE ANOMALIA
    # =====================================================
    def score(self, X):
        X_processed = self._prepare_data(X)

        if self.model is None:
            raise ValueError("O modelo MLP Single precisa ser treinado antes de calcular scores.")

        recon = self.model.predict(X_processed)
        errors = (X_processed - recon) ** 2
        scores = np.mean(errors, axis=1)

        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores, invert=False)
   # =====================================================
    # SALVAR E CARREGAR MODELO
    # =====================================================
    def save(self, path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(self.model, f)

    def load(self, path):
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "rb") as f:
            self.model = pickle.load(f)

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\models\mlp_supervised.py
################################################################################

# Arquivo: core/models/mlp_supervised.py
import os
import pickle
import numpy as np
from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier
import warnings

from core.models.base_model import BaseModel

class MLPSupervisedModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Especialista Supervisionado (IDS Clássico).
    Treinado com rótulos explícitos (0 = Normal, 1 = Ataque Alvo).
    Retorna a probabilidade da janela pertencer à classe de ataque.
    """

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)
        
        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("mlp_supervised", config.get("model", {}).get("mlp_single", {}))
        
        hidden_layer_sizes = model_cfg.get("hidden_layer_sizes", (32,))
        if isinstance(hidden_layer_sizes, list):
            hidden_layer_sizes = tuple(hidden_layer_sizes)
            
        self.hidden_layer_sizes = hidden_layer_sizes
        self.activation = model_cfg.get("activation", "relu")
        self.solver = model_cfg.get("solver", "adam")
        self.alpha = model_cfg.get("alpha", 0.0001)
        self.batch_size = model_cfg.get("batch_size", "auto")
        self.learning_rate_init = model_cfg.get("learning_rate_init", 0.001)
        self.max_iter = model_cfg.get("max_iter", 200)
        self.early_stopping = model_cfg.get("early_stopping", True)
        self.validation_fraction = model_cfg.get("validation_fraction", 0.1)
        self.random_state = config.get("training", {}).get("seed", 42)

        self.model = None

    def _prepare_data(self, X):
        X_arr = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float32)
        if X_arr.ndim == 3:
            X_last_event = X_arr[:, -1, :]
            X_mean_context = np.mean(X_arr, axis=1)
            return np.hstack([X_last_event, X_mean_context])
        return X_arr

    def fit(self, X, y=None):
        X_processed = self._prepare_data(X)
        
        if y is None or len(np.unique(y)) < 2:
            raise ValueError("O modelo supervisionado exige rótulos 'y' com pelo menos duas classes (0 e 1).")

        self.model = MLPClassifier(
            hidden_layer_sizes=self.hidden_layer_sizes,
            activation=self.activation,
            solver=self.solver,
            alpha=self.alpha,
            batch_size=self.batch_size,
            learning_rate_init=self.learning_rate_init,
            max_iter=self.max_iter,
            early_stopping=self.early_stopping,
            validation_fraction=self.validation_fraction,
            random_state=self.random_state
        )

        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
            self.model.fit(X_processed, y)

    def score(self, X):
        X_processed = self._prepare_data(X)

        if self.model is None:
            raise ValueError("O modelo supervisionado precisa ser treinado antes de calcular scores.")

        probs = self.model.predict_proba(X_processed)
        
        if probs.shape[1] == 1:
            return np.zeros(len(X_processed))
            
        scores = probs[:, 1]
        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores, invert=False)

    def save(self, path):
        """Exporta o modelo matematicamente treinado para o disco."""
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(self.model, f)

    def load(self, path):
        """Carrega o modelo do disco para inferência futura."""
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "rb") as f:
            self.model = pickle.load(f)

# ARQUIVO: core\models\model_factory.py

# core/models/model_factory.py
import re
import importlib
import inspect
import pkgutil
import logging
import core.models
from core.models.base_model import BaseModel

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MODEL_REGISTRY = {}
_DISCOVERED = False

def _to_snake(name: str) -> str:
    """
    Converte CamelCase para snake_case.
    Ex: MLPAutoencoder -> mlp_autoencoder
        IsolationForestModel -> isolation_forest
        LOFModel -> lof
    """
    s = re.sub(r'(.)([A-Z][a-z]+)', r'\1_\2', name)
    s = re.sub(r'([a-z0-9])([A-Z])', r'\1_\2', s).lower()
    return s.replace('_model', '')  # remove o sufixo '_model' se existir

def _discover_models():
    global _DISCOVERED
    if _DISCOVERED:
        return
        
    logger.info("🔍 [ModelFactory] Percorrendo core.models para descoberta automática...")
    
    package_path = getattr(core.models, "__path__", [])
    for _, module_name, _ in pkgutil.iter_modules(package_path):
        if module_name in {"base_model", "model_factory"}:
            continue
        
        full_module_name = f"core.models.{module_name}"
        try:
            module = importlib.import_module(full_module_name)
            for name, obj in inspect.getmembers(module, inspect.isclass):
                if issubclass(obj, BaseModel) and obj is not BaseModel:
                    key = _to_snake(name)
                    if key not in MODEL_REGISTRY:
                        MODEL_REGISTRY[key] = obj
                        logger.debug(f"  ✅ {key} -> {name}")
                    else:
                        logger.warning(f"  ⚠️ Chave '{key}' já registrada. Ignorando {name}.")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"  ❌ Erro ao carregar {full_module_name}: {e}")
            
    _DISCOVERED = True

# Executa a descoberta no momento da importação
_discover_models()

# 🔥 REGISTRO MANUAL DE MODELOS ADICIONAIS (para garantir que estão disponíveis)
try:
    from core.models.random_forest_supervised import RandomForestSupervisedModel
    MODEL_REGISTRY["random_forest_supervised"] = RandomForestSupervisedModel
    logger.info("✅ RandomForestSupervisedModel registrado manualmente.")
except ImportError as e:
    logger.warning(f"⚠️ Não foi possível importar RandomForestSupervisedModel: {e}")

# (Aqui você pode adicionar outros modelos manuais, se necessário)

class ModelFactory:
    @classmethod
    def get_model(cls, config: dict) -> BaseModel:
        model_cfg = config.get("model", {})
        model_name = model_cfg.get("type")
        
        if not model_name or model_name not in MODEL_REGISTRY:
            raise ValueError(
                f"❌ Modelo '{model_name}' não encontrado.\n"
                f"   Modelos disponíveis: {list(MODEL_REGISTRY.keys())}"
            )
        
        model_class = MODEL_REGISTRY[model_name]
        logger.info(f"🤖 Instanciando: {model_name} ({model_class.__name__})")
        model = model_class(config)
        
        # Validação do contrato obrigatório (fit e score)
        required = ("fit", "score")
        missing = [m for m in required if not callable(getattr(model, m, None))]
        if missing:
            raise NotImplementedError(
                f"🚨 O modelo '{model_name}' não implementa os métodos obrigatórios: {missing}"
            )
        return model

    @classmethod
    def register(cls, name: str, model_class):
        """Permite registro manual se necessário (útil para testes ou plugins externos)."""
        MODEL_REGISTRY[name] = model_class


def create(config):
    return ModelFactory.get_model(config)

# ARQUIVO: core\models\ocsvm.py

#NOVO MODELO DE OCSVM
import numpy as np
from sklearn.svm import OneClassSVM

from core.models.base_model import BaseModel


class OCSVMModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Wrapper para o algoritmo One-Class SVM.
    Adaptado para aceitar a estrutura de tensores 3D com achatamento em 2D.
    """

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)

        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("ocsvm", {})

        self.kernel = model_cfg.get("kernel", "rbf")
        self.nu = model_cfg.get("nu", 0.05)
        self.gamma = model_cfg.get("gamma", "scale")
        self.degree = model_cfg.get("degree", 3)
        self.coef0 = model_cfg.get("coef0", 0.0)
        self.tol = model_cfg.get("tol", 1e-3)
        self.cache_size = model_cfg.get("cache_size", 200)
        self.max_iter = model_cfg.get("max_iter", -1)

        self.model = None

    # =====================================================
    # TREINAMENTO
    # =====================================================

    def fit(self, X):
        X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64)

        # Adaptação para o pipeline temporal 3D do projeto
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)

        self.model = OneClassSVM(
            kernel=self.kernel,
            nu=self.nu,
            gamma=self.gamma,
            degree=self.degree,
            coef0=self.coef0,
            tol=self.tol,
            cache_size=self.cache_size,
            max_iter=self.max_iter
        )

        self.model.fit(X)

    # =====================================================
    # CÁLCULO DE SCORE DE ANOMALIA
    # =====================================================

    def score(self, X):
        X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64)

        # Adaptação para o pipeline temporal 3D do projeto
        if X.ndim == 3:
            X = X.reshape(X.shape[0], -1)

        if self.model is None:
            raise ValueError("O modelo OCSVM precisa ser treinado antes de computar os scores.")

        # decision_function original retorna positivo para normal e negativo para anômalo
        scores = self.model.decision_function(X)

        # Inverte os valores para seguir o contrato global (scores maiores indicam anomalias)
        scores = self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores, invert=True)

        return scores

    # =====================================================
    # SALVAR E CARREGAR MODELO
    # =====================================================
    def save(self, path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(self.model, f)

    def load(self, path):
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "rb") as f:
            self.model = pickle.load(f)

# ARQUIVO: core\models\random_forest_supervised.py

# core/models/random_forest_supervised.py
import os
import pickle
import numpy as np
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier
import warnings

from core.models.base_model import BaseModel


class RandomForestSupervisedModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Random Forest supervisionado para detecção de anomalias.
    Treinado com rótulos explícitos (0 = Normal, 1 = Anomalia).
    Retorna a probabilidade da classe 1 (anomalia).
    """

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)

        model_cfg = config.get("model", {}).get("random_forest_supervised", {})

        self.n_estimators = model_cfg.get("n_estimators", 150)
        self.max_depth = model_cfg.get("max_depth", 8)
        self.min_samples_split = model_cfg.get("min_samples_split", 10)
        self.min_samples_leaf = model_cfg.get("min_samples_leaf", 5)
        self.class_weight = model_cfg.get("class_weight", "balanced_subsample")
        self.random_state = config.get("training", {}).get("seed", 42)
        self.n_jobs = model_cfg.get("n_jobs", -1)

        self.model = None

    def _prepare_data(self, X):
        """Aceita dados 2D ou 3D. Se 3D, achata (mas usamos sequence_length=1, então é 2D)."""
        X_arr = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float32)
        if X_arr.ndim == 3:
            X_arr = X_arr.reshape(X_arr.shape[0], -1)
        return X_arr

    def fit(self, X, y=None):
        X_processed = self._prepare_data(X)

        if y is None:
            raise ValueError("O modelo supervisionado exige rótulos 'y'.")

        self.model = RandomForestClassifier(
            n_estimators=self.n_estimators,
            max_depth=self.max_depth,
            min_samples_split=self.min_samples_split,
            min_samples_leaf=self.min_samples_leaf,
            class_weight=self.class_weight,
            random_state=self.random_state,
            n_jobs=self.n_jobs
        )

        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
            self.model.fit(X_processed, y)

    def score(self, X):
        X_processed = self._prepare_data(X)

        if self.model is None:
            raise ValueError("O modelo precisa ser treinado antes de calcular scores.")

        # Retorna a probabilidade da classe 1 (anomalia)
        probs = self.model.predict_proba(X_processed)
        if probs.shape[1] == 1:
            return np.zeros(len(X_processed))
        
        scores = probs[:, 1]
        
        # 🔍 Diagnóstico (opcional, remove depois)
        # print(f"🔍 Score range: min={scores.min():.4f}, max={scores.max():.4f}, mean={scores.mean():.4f}")
        
        return self._ensure_higher_is_anomaly(scores, invert=False)

    def save(self, path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(self.model, f)

    def load(self, path):
        with open(os.path.join(path, "model.pkl"), "rb") as f:
            self.model = pickle.load(f)

# ARQUIVO: core\temporal_builder.py
