# core/temporal_builder.py

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np


class TemporalBuilder:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        temporal_cfg = config.get("temporal", {})
        self.session_timeout = temporal_cfg.get("session_timeout", 3600)
        # min_events_session: se > 1, sessões menores viram NaN (descartadas
        # quando window_grouping='session'). Default 1 = comportamento anterior.
        self.min_events_session = max(1, int(temporal_cfg.get("min_events_session", 1)))

    def transform(self, df):
        if df is None or df.empty:
            return pd.DataFrame()

        print(f"🕒 [TemporalBuilder] Processando dinâmica temporal (Timeout: {self.session_timeout}s, MinEvents: {self.min_events_session})...")

        df_temp = df.copy()

        if 'Time' not in df_temp.columns:
            print("⚠️ Coluna 'Time' ausente. Retornando dataset bruto.")
            return df_temp

        if not pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(df_temp['Time']):
            print("⚠️ Coluna 'Time' não é numérica. Retornando dataset bruto.")
            return df_temp

        # Aviso (sem dropar): valores não-finitos em Time podem contaminar sessões
        n_bad_time = int((~np.isfinite(df_temp['Time'].astype(float))).sum())
        if n_bad_time > 0:
            print(f"   ⚠️ {n_bad_time} eventos com Time inválido (NaN/inf). Considere filtrar antes.")

        # Ordenação estável (determinismo em empates)
        df_temp = df_temp.sort_values(by=['UserID', 'Time'], kind='stable').reset_index(drop=True)

        # Cálculo de delta e sessão
        df_temp['time_diff'] = df_temp.groupby('UserID', sort=False)['Time'].diff().fillna(0)
        df_temp['new_session'] = (df_temp['time_diff'] > self.session_timeout).astype(int)
        df_temp['session_id'] = df_temp.groupby('UserID', sort=False)['new_session'].cumsum()
        df_temp['session_id'] = df_temp['UserID'].astype(str) + "_" + df_temp['session_id'].astype(str)

        # Filtro opcional de sessões pequenas
        if self.min_events_session > 1:
            sizes = df_temp.groupby('session_id')['session_id'].transform('size')
            mask_tiny = sizes < self.min_events_session
            df_temp.loc[mask_tiny, 'session_id'] = None

        df_temp = df_temp.drop(columns=['time_diff', 'new_session'])

        n_sessions = df_temp['session_id'].nunique()
        print(f"   ✅ Sessões identificadas. Total de sessões válidas: {n_sessions}")

        return df_temp

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\trainer.py
################################################################################

# core/trainer.py
#
# Protocolo de treino causal UEBA com anti-leakage, escalonamento per-user com fallback global,
# calibração de limiares operacionais e suporte a cold-start e versionamento de FeatureState.

import os
import json
import pickle
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import sliding_window_view

try:
    from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest
    HAS_IFOREST = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_IFOREST = False


TRAINING_PROTOCOL_VERSION = "train_val_independent_v5_features_v3"


class Trainer:
    def _checkpoint_uses_current_protocol(self, meta_path):
        try:
            with open(meta_path, "rb") as f:
                meta = pickle.load(f)
            return meta.get("training_protocol") == TRAINING_PROTOCOL_VERSION
        except Exception:
            return False

    def __init__(self, config, model):
        self.config = config
        self.model = model
        self.sequence_length = config.get("model", {}).get("sequence_length", 10)
        self.scalers = {}
        self.feature_columns = None
        self.output_dir = config.get("output_dir", "./output")
        os.makedirs(self.output_dir, exist_ok=True)

        self.feature_processor = None
        self.temporal_builder = None

        self.user_thresholds = {}
        self.global_threshold = 0.0
        self.user_event_counts = {}

    @staticmethod
    def _select_training_events(train_df, target="Is_Anomaly"):
        if target not in train_df.columns:
            return train_df.copy()
        return train_df.loc[train_df[target] == 0].copy()

    def _infer_role(self, user_id, group=None) -> str:
        """
        Infere o papel comportamental (role) do usuário para priors de cold-start
        baseado em heurísticas do Active Directory.
        """
        uid = str(user_id)
        if uid.endswith("$"):
            return "Machine Account"
        uid_upper = uid.upper()
        if uid_upper in {"SYSTEM", "LOCAL SERVICE", "NETWORK SERVICE", "ANONYMOUS LOGON"}:
            return "System Account"
        uid_lower = uid.lower()
        if uid_lower.startswith(("adm", "admin", "root", "administrator")):
            return "Domain Admin"
        if uid_lower.startswith(("svc_", "service_", "svc-")):
            return "Service Account"
        return "Standard User"

    def _build_benign_training_sequences(self, train_df, feature_columns=None):
        target = self.config.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")
        benign_events = self._select_training_events(train_df, target=target)

        # Atualiza contadores de eventos históricos por usuário (para cold-start: N < 10)
        self.user_event_counts = {
            str(uid): len(grp) for uid, grp in benign_events.groupby("UserID", sort=False)
        }

        # 1. Scaler per-user
        for user_id, group in benign_events.groupby("UserID", sort=False):
            self._prepare_and_scale_X(
                group, user_id, fit=True, feature_columns=feature_columns
            )

        # 2. Scaler global fallback (cold-start) — fitado APENAS sobre benignos
        if self.feature_columns is not None and len(benign_events) > 1:
            try:
                # Adiciona is_cold_start se fizer parte das feature_columns
                b_df = benign_events.copy()
                if "is_cold_start" in self.feature_columns and "is_cold_start" not in b_df.columns:
                    b_df["is_cold_start"] = [
                        1.0 if self.user_event_counts.get(str(u), 0) < 10 else 0.0
                        for u in b_df["UserID"]
                    ]

                X_global = b_df.reindex(
                    columns=self.feature_columns, fill_value=0
                ).values.astype(np.float32)
                if X_global.shape[0] > 1:
                    global_scaler = StandardScaler()
                    global_scaler.fit(X_global)
                    if hasattr(global_scaler, "scale_"):
                        global_scaler.scale_ = np.maximum(global_scaler.scale_, 1e-3)
                    self.scalers["__GLOBAL__"] = global_scaler
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"⚠️ Global scaler fallback não criado: {e}")

        X_all, indices, users, labels = self._build_sequences(
            train_df, fit=False, return_labels=True
        )
        if labels is not None:
            benign_windows = labels == 0
            X_all = X_all[benign_windows]
            indices = indices[benign_windows]
            users = users[benign_windows]
        return X_all, indices, users, labels

    def _compute_f2_score(self, y_true, y_pred):
        if y_true is None or len(y_true) == 0:
            return 0.0
        tp = np.sum((y_pred == 1) & (y_true == 1))
        fp = np.sum((y_pred == 1) & (y_true == 0))
        fn = np.sum((y_pred == 0) & (y_true == 1))

        if tp == 0:
            return 0.0
        precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0
        recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0

        if precision == 0 or recall == 0:
            return 0.0
        beta = 2.0
        return (1 + beta**2) * (precision * recall) / ((beta**2 * precision) + recall)

    def _personalized_thresholds(self, scores, users, labels, percentile, min_windows=5):
        if labels is None:
            return {}
        scores = np.asarray(scores)
        users = np.asarray(users)
        labels = np.asarray(labels)
        thresholds = {}
        for uid in np.unique(users):
            user_mask = users == uid
            if np.sum(user_mask) >= min_windows and np.any(labels[user_mask] == 1):
                thresholds[str(uid)] = float(np.percentile(scores[user_mask], percentile))
        return thresholds

    def _prepare_and_scale_X(self, group, user_id, fit=False, feature_columns=None):
        grp = group.copy()
        
        # Cold-start flag: adicionada se esperada em feature_columns, habilitada via config ou já presente
        include_cold = (
            (self.feature_columns is not None and "is_cold_start" in self.feature_columns)
            or self.config.get("features", {}).get("include_cold_start", False)
            or "is_cold_start" in grp.columns
        )
        if include_cold:
            n_hist = self.user_event_counts.get(str(user_id), len(grp) if fit else 0)
            grp["is_cold_start"] = 1.0 if n_hist < 10 else 0.0

        if fit and feature_columns is not None:
            self.feature_columns = list(feature_columns)
            if include_cold and "is_cold_start" not in self.feature_columns:
                self.feature_columns.append("is_cold_start")

        if self.feature_columns is not None:
            if include_cold and "is_cold_start" not in self.feature_columns:
                self.feature_columns.append("is_cold_start")
            X_num = grp.reindex(columns=self.feature_columns, fill_value=0)
        else:
            target = self.config.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")
            drop_cols = ["UserID", "Time", target, "EventID", "datetime", "session_id", "AnomalyType"]
            X_num = grp.drop(columns=[c for c in drop_cols if c in grp.columns]).select_dtypes(include=[np.number])
            if fit:
                self.feature_columns = X_num.columns.tolist()
                if include_cold and "is_cold_start" not in self.feature_columns:
                    self.feature_columns.append("is_cold_start")

        X_arr = X_num.values.astype(np.float32)
        if X_arr.shape[0] == 0:
            return X_arr

        if fit:
            scaler = StandardScaler()
            scaler.fit(X_arr)
            if hasattr(scaler, "scale_"):
                scaler.scale_ = np.maximum(scaler.scale_, 1e-3)
            X_scaled = scaler.transform(X_arr)
            X_scaled = np.clip(X_scaled, -10.0, 10.0)
            self.scalers[str(user_id)] = scaler
        else:
            # Fallback: usuário → global → identidade
            scaler = self.scalers.get(str(user_id)) or self.scalers.get("__GLOBAL__")
            if scaler:
                if hasattr(scaler, "scale_"):
                    scaler.scale_ = np.maximum(scaler.scale_, 1e-3)
                X_scaled = scaler.transform(X_arr)
                X_scaled = np.clip(X_scaled, -10.0, 10.0)
            else:
                X_scaled = X_arr

        return X_scaled

    def _build_sequences(self, df, fit=False, return_labels=False, feature_columns=None):
        X_all, indices_all, users_all, labels_all = [], [], [], []
        target = self.config.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")

        grouping = self.config.get("model", {}).get("window_grouping", "user")
        if grouping == "session" and "session_id" in df.columns:
            iterator = df.groupby(["UserID", "session_id"], sort=False)
        else:
            iterator = df.groupby("UserID", sort=False)

        for key, group in iterator:
            user_id = key[0] if isinstance(key, tuple) else key

            X = self._prepare_and_scale_X(group, user_id, fit=fit, feature_columns=feature_columns)
            if len(X) < self.sequence_length:
                continue

            windows = sliding_window_view(X, window_shape=self.sequence_length, axis=0)
            windows = np.swapaxes(windows, 1, 2)
            X_all.append(windows)

            idx = group.index.values[self.sequence_length - 1:]
            indices_all.append(idx)
            users_all.append(np.full(len(idx), str(user_id)))

            if return_labels and target in df.columns:
                windows_labels = sliding_window_view(group[target].values, window_shape=self.sequence_length)
                labels_all.append(np.max(windows_labels, axis=1))

        if not X_all:
            return np.array([]), np.array([]), np.array([]), None

        return (
            np.concatenate(X_all),
            np.concatenate(indices_all),
            np.concatenate(users_all),
            np.concatenate(labels_all) if labels_all else None
        )

    def _calculate_scores(self, X):
        if X is None or len(X) == 0:
            return np.array([])

        is_if_model = HAS_IFOREST and isinstance(self.model, IsolationForest)
        model_type_name = type(self.model).__name__.lower()

        if is_if_model or "isolationforest" in model_type_name or "iforest" in model_type_name:
            if hasattr(self.model, "decision_function"):
                return -self.model.decision_function(X)
            elif hasattr(self.model, "score_samples"):
                return -self.model.score_samples(X)

        if hasattr(self.model, "score"):
            scores = self.model.score(X)
            if self.config.get("model", {}).get("auto_invert_scores", False) and np.mean(scores) < 0:
                return -scores
            return scores

        raise ValueError("❌ Erro: O Modelo não possui um método de escoragem válido!")

    def _persist_meta(self, meta_path):
        """Persiste scalers, feature_columns, FeatureState e metadados de auditoria."""
        meta_dict = {
            "scalers": self.scalers,
            "feature_columns": self.feature_columns,
            "training_protocol": TRAINING_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
        }
        if self.feature_processor is not None:
            meta_dict["feature_processor"] = self.feature_processor
            # Extensão de versionamento de estado (Fase 4)
            if hasattr(self.feature_processor, "state"):
                fp_state = self.feature_processor.state
                meta_dict["feature_state"] = self.feature_processor.snapshot()
                meta_dict["feature_set"] = getattr(self.feature_processor, "feature_set", "E0-legacy")
                meta_dict["feature_version"] = getattr(fp_state, "feature_version", "1.0.0")
                meta_dict["param_hash"] = getattr(fp_state, "param_hash", "")

        if self.temporal_builder is not None:
            meta_dict["temporal_builder"] = self.temporal_builder

        with open(meta_path, "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(meta_dict, f)

    def run(self, train_df, val_df, test_df, feature_columns=None):
        train_df = train_df.reset_index(drop=True)
        val_df = val_df.reset_index(drop=True)
        if test_df is not None:
            test_df = test_df.reset_index(drop=True)

        self.user_thresholds = {}
        self.global_threshold = 0.0

        train_cfg = self.config.get("training", {})
        do_resume = train_cfg.get("resume", False)
        save_model = train_cfg.get("save_model", True)
        thresh_mode = train_cfg.get("threshold_mode", "per_user")

        is_supervised = train_cfg.get("supervised", False)
        target_attack = train_cfg.get("target_attack_type", None)

        fixed_threshold = train_cfg.get("fixed_threshold", None)
        fixed_percentile = train_cfg.get("fixed_percentile", None)
        target = self.config.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")

        model_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, "saved_model")
        meta_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, "trainer_meta.pkl")

        # -------------------- TREINO --------------------
        if (do_resume and os.path.exists(model_path) and os.path.exists(meta_path)
                and self._checkpoint_uses_current_protocol(meta_path)):
            print("🔄 [Trainer] Checkpoint detectado. Retomando execução...")
            with open(meta_path, "rb") as f:
                meta = pickle.load(f)
                self.scalers = meta['scalers']
                self.feature_columns = meta['feature_columns']
            if hasattr(self.model, "load"):
                self.model.load(model_path)
        else:
            print(f"🚀 [Trainer] Iniciando treino do zero (Modo: {thresh_mode})...")

            if is_supervised:
                X_train, _, _, y_train = self._build_sequences(
                    train_df, fit=True, return_labels=True,
                    feature_columns=feature_columns
                )
            else:
                X_train, _, _, y_train = self._build_benign_training_sequences(
                    train_df, feature_columns=feature_columns
                )
                if y_train is not None:
                    y_train = y_train[y_train == 0]
            if len(X_train) == 0:
                raise ValueError("Nenhuma janela de treino elegível foi gerada.")

            if y_train is None:
                y_train = np.zeros(len(X_train))

            if is_supervised and len(np.unique(y_train)) < 2:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Treino supervisionado com uma única classe. "
                    "Forneça ataques na partição de treino ou remova o modelo supervisionado."
                )

            if hasattr(self.model, "fit"):
                if is_supervised:
                    self.model.fit(X_train, y=y_train)
                else:
                    self.model.fit(X_train)

            if save_model:
                os.makedirs(model_path, exist_ok=True)
                if hasattr(self.model, "save"):
                    self.model.save(model_path)
                self._persist_meta(meta_path)
                print(f"💾 [Trainer] Modelo e metadados salvos em: {model_path}")

        # -------------------- VALIDAÇÃO --------------------
        print("\n--- [Trainer] Gerando Janelas de VALIDAÇÃO ---")

        if target_attack and "AnomalyType" in val_df.columns:
            print(f"   🔍 [ESPECIALISTA] Filtrando validação para '{target_attack}'...")
            mask = (val_df[target] == 0) | (val_df["AnomalyType"] == target_attack)
            val_df = val_df[mask].copy()

        X_val, _, val_users, y_val = self._build_sequences(val_df, fit=False, return_labels=True)
        if X_val is None or len(X_val) == 0:
            raise ValueError("❌ Conjunto de VALIDAÇÃO gerou 0 janelas.")

        val_scores = self._calculate_scores(X_val)

        if y_val is not None and np.sum(y_val) > 0:
            print(f"   ✅ Validação contém {int(np.sum(y_val))} janelas anômalas.")
        else:
            print(f"   ❌ ATENÇÃO: Validação livre de anomalias!")

        best_p, best_f2 = 95.0, -1.0
        print("\n--- [Trainer] Definição de Limiares ---")

        if fixed_threshold is not None:
            self.global_threshold = float(fixed_threshold)
            self.user_thresholds = {"__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__": self.global_threshold}
            print(f"⚖️ [YAML] Limiar absoluto fixado: {fixed_threshold}")

        elif fixed_percentile is not None:
            best_p = float(fixed_percentile)
            self.global_threshold = float(np.percentile(val_scores, best_p)) if val_scores.size > 0 else 0.5
            self.user_thresholds = {"__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__": self.global_threshold}
            if thresh_mode == "per_user":
                self.user_thresholds.update(
                    self._personalized_thresholds(val_scores, val_users, y_val, best_p)
                )
            print(f"⚖️ [YAML] Percentil fixado: {best_p}%")

        else:
            if y_val is not None and np.sum(y_val) > 0:
                print(f"🧠 [AUTO] Otimizando com F2-Score...")
                calibration_cfg = train_cfg.get("threshold_search", {})
                percentile_min = float(calibration_cfg.get("percentile_min", 0.0))
                percentile_max = float(calibration_cfg.get("percentile_max", 99.5))
                percentile_step = float(calibration_cfg.get("percentile_step", 0.5))
                if not (0 <= percentile_min < percentile_max <= 100) or percentile_step <= 0:
                    raise ValueError("Configuração inválida em training.threshold_search.")
                for p in np.arange(percentile_min, percentile_max + percentile_step / 2, percentile_step):
                    t_glob = np.percentile(val_scores, p)
                    if thresh_mode == "per_user":
                        u_t_dict = self._personalized_thresholds(
                            val_scores, val_users, y_val, p
                        )
                        y_pred = (val_scores > np.array([u_t_dict.get(str(uid), t_glob) for uid in val_users])).astype(int)
                    else:
                        y_pred = (val_scores > t_glob).astype(int)

                    f2 = self._compute_f2_score(y_val, y_pred)
                    if f2 > best_f2:
                        best_f2, best_p = f2, p
                print(f"✅ [AUTO] Percentil ideal: {best_p:.1f}% (F2: {best_f2:.4f})")
            else:
                best_p = 99.0
                print(f"⚠️ [AUTO] Validação limpa. Percentil de segurança: {best_p}%")

            self.global_threshold = float(np.percentile(val_scores, best_p)) if val_scores.size > 0 else 0.5
            self.user_thresholds = {"__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__": self.global_threshold}
            if thresh_mode == "per_user":
                self.user_thresholds.update(
                    self._personalized_thresholds(val_scores, val_users, y_val, best_p)
                )

        with open(os.path.join(self.output_dir, "user_thresholds.json"), "w") as f:
            json.dump(self.user_thresholds, f, indent=4)

        print("\n--- [Trainer] Execução Finalizada ---")
        return {
            "val_f2": float(best_f2) if best_f2 != -1 else -1.0,
            "global_threshold": self.global_threshold
        }


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: core\utils\config_validator.py
################################################################################

class ConfigValidator:

    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config

    def validate(self):

        cfg = self.config

        # =========================================================
        # DATA
        # =========================================================
        if "data" not in cfg:
            raise ValueError("Missing 'data' section")

        data_cfg = cfg["data"]

        # aceita path único OU split
        if not (
            "path" in data_cfg or
            (
                "train_path" in data_cfg and
                "val_path" in data_cfg and
                "test_path" in data_cfg
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "data must have either 'path' OR "
                "'train_path', 'val_path', 'test_path'"
            )

        # =========================================================
        # MODEL
        # =========================================================
        if "model" not in cfg:
            raise ValueError("Missing 'model' section")

        if "type" not in cfg["model"]:
            raise ValueError("Missing 'model.type'")

        if "sequence_length" not in cfg["model"]:
            raise ValueError("Missing 'model.sequence_length'")

        # =========================================================
        # SAMPLING
        # =========================================================
        if "sampling" in cfg:

            mode = cfg["sampling"].get("mode", "full")

            if mode not in ["full", "debug"]:
                raise ValueError("sampling.mode must be 'full' or 'debug'")

        print("✅ Config validation passed")

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\auditar_detection.py
################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
auditar_detection.py — reproduz o bloco:
============================================================
📊 RESUMO DA AUDITORIA DE DETECÇÃO (DOUBLE CHECK)
============================================================
Lê os artefatos REAIS de um run_dir e reaplica Trainer._build_sequences +
_calculate_scores + user_thresholds.json.
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import pickle
import argparse
import glob
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import yaml
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score, roc_auc_score

root_path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
if root_path not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, root_path)

from core.data.dataset_adapter import DatasetAdapter
from core.features.feature_processor import FeatureProcessor
from core.temporal_builder import TemporalBuilder
from core.trainer import Trainer
from core.models.model_factory import create as create_model


# =====================================================================
# CARGA DOS ARTEFATOS
# =====================================================================
def _load_run(run_dir):
    cfg = os.path.join(run_dir, "config_used.yaml")
    if not os.path.exists(cfg):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"config_used.yaml ausente em {run_dir}")
    with open(cfg, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    meta = {}
    mp = os.path.join(run_dir, "trainer_meta.pkl")
    if os.path.exists(mp):
        with open(mp, "rb") as f:
            meta = pickle.load(f)

    thresholds = {}
    tp = os.path.join(run_dir, "user_thresholds.json")
    if os.path.exists(tp):
        with open(tp) as f:
            thresholds = json.load(f)

    model = create_model(config)
    model_dir = os.path.join(run_dir, "saved_model")
    if hasattr(model, "load") and os.path.isdir(model_dir):
        try:
            model.load(model_dir)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️  Falha ao carregar modelo ({e}).")
    return config, model, meta, thresholds


# =====================================================================
# DATASET
# =====================================================================
def _resolve_test_df(config, run_dir, data_override):
    if data_override:
        path = data_override
    else:
        cand = os.path.join(run_dir, "test_audit.csv")
        if os.path.exists(cand):
            path = cand
        else:
            path = (config.get("data", {}).get("dataset_path")
                    or config.get("data", {}).get("test_path"))
            if not path:
                raise FileNotFoundError("Nenhum dataset resolvível. Passe --data.")

    adapter = DatasetAdapter(config)
    raw = adapter._load_resource(path)
    if raw is None or raw.empty:
        raise ValueError(f"Dataset vazio: {path}")
    raw = adapter._normalize(raw)
    raw = adapter._apply_auto_labels(raw, path)

    if not path.endswith("test_audit.csv"):
        _, _, raw = adapter._perform_dynamic_split(raw)
    return raw, path


# =====================================================================
# PIPELINE + JANELAS
# =====================================================================
def _build_windows(t_df, config, meta, model):
    trainer = Trainer(config, model)
    trainer.scalers = meta.get("scalers", {}) or {}
    trainer.feature_columns = meta.get("feature_columns", None)

    X, indices, users, y_true = trainer._build_sequences(
        t_df, fit=False, return_labels=True
    )
    if X is None or len(X) == 0:
        raise ValueError("Nenhuma janela gerada.")
    scores = trainer._calculate_scores(X)
    seq_len = config.get("model", {}).get("sequence_length", 10)
    return trainer, X, indices, users, y_true, scores, seq_len


def _thresholds_per_user(users, scores, thresholds):
    fb = thresholds.get("__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__")
    if fb is None:
        fb = float(np.median(scores))
    thr = np.array([thresholds.get(str(u), fb) for u in users])
    return (scores > thr).astype(int)


# =====================================================================
# RELATÓRIO
# =====================================================================
def _print_report(total_events, total_anomalies, seq_len, y_true, y_pred, scores):
    tot_w = len(y_true)
    anom_w = int(np.sum(y_true))
    alerts = int(np.sum(y_pred))
    tp = int(np.sum((y_pred == 1) & (y_true == 1)))
    fp = int(np.sum((y_pred == 1) & (y_true == 0)))
    fn = int(np.sum((y_pred == 0) & (y_true == 1)))
    tn = int(np.sum((y_pred == 0) & (y_true == 0)))
    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) else 0.0
    rec = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) else 0.0
    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) else 0.0
    try:
        pr = average_precision_score(y_true, scores)
    except Exception:
        pr = 0.0
    try:
        roc = roc_auc_score(y_true, scores)
    except Exception:
        roc = 0.0
    raw_prev = total_anomalies / total_events if total_events else 0.0
    win_prev = anom_w / tot_w if tot_w else 0.0

    print("=" * 60)
    print("📊 RESUMO DA AUDITORIA DE DETECÇÃO (DOUBLE CHECK)")
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f" 🔹 Eventos Totais (Brutos)      : {total_events:,}")
    print(f" 🔹 Eventos Anômalos (Brutos)    : {total_anomalies:,} (Prevalência: {raw_prev*100:.2f}%)")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f" 🔹 Janelas Avaliadas (Seq={seq_len})   : {tot_w:,}")
    print(f" 🔹 Janelas Anômalas (Gabarito)  : {anom_w:,} (Prevalência: {win_prev*100:.2f}%)")
    print(f" 🔹 Alertas Disparados           : {alerts:,}")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f" ✅ Verdadeiros Positivos (TP) : {tp:,}")
    print(f" ⚠️  Falsos Positivos (FP)     : {fp:,}")
    print(f" ❌ Falsos Negativos (FN)     : {fn:,}")
    print(f" 🛡️  Verdadeiros Negativos (TN) : {tn:,}")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f" 🎯 Precision (Precisão)      : {prec:.4f}")
    print(f" 🎯 Recall (Sensibilidade)    : {rec:.4f}")
    print(f" 🎯 F1-Score                  : {f1:.4f}")
    print(f" 🎯 PR-AUC                    : {pr:.4f}")
    print(f" 🎯 ROC-AUC                   : {roc:.4f}")
    print("=" * 60)
    return dict(events=total_events, anomalies_events=total_anomalies,
                windows=tot_w, anomalous_windows=anom_w, alerts=alerts,
                tp=tp, fp=fp, fn=fn, tn=tn,
                precision=prec, recall=rec, f1=f1, pr_auc=pr, roc_auc=roc)


# =====================================================================
# API
# =====================================================================
def auditar(run_dir, data_path=None, verbose=True):
    config, model, meta, thresholds = _load_run(run_dir)
    df_raw, path_used = _resolve_test_df(config, run_dir, data_path)

    target = config.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")
    if target not in df_raw.columns:
        df_raw[target] = 0
    total_events = len(df_raw)
    total_anomalies = int(df_raw[target].sum())

    processor = meta.get("feature_processor")
    temporal = meta.get("temporal_builder")
    if processor is None:
        if verbose:
            print("⚠️  feature_processor ausente — refitando.")
        processor = FeatureProcessor(config)
        processor.fit(df_raw)
    if temporal is None:
        temporal = TemporalBuilder(config)

    t_df = temporal.transform(processor.transform(df_raw))
    _, _, _, users, y_true, scores, seq_len = _build_windows(t_df, config, meta, model)
    y_true = y_true.astype(int)
    y_pred = _thresholds_per_user(users, scores, thresholds)

    if verbose:
        print(f"📂 run_dir : {run_dir}")
        print(f"📂 dataset : {path_used}")
    return _print_report(total_events, total_anomalies, seq_len, y_true, y_pred, scores)


# =====================================================================
# BATCH
# =====================================================================
def _find_runs(root):
    runs = []
    for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(root):
        if "config_used.yaml" in filenames and "trainer_meta.pkl" in filenames:
            runs.append(dirpath)
    return sorted(runs)


def auditar_batch(results_root, data_path=None, output_csv=None):
    runs = _find_runs(results_root)
    print(f"🔎 Encontrei {len(runs)} run_dirs em {results_root}", flush=True)
    for r in runs:
        print(f"   • {os.path.relpath(r, results_root)}", flush=True)
    if not runs:
        print("⚠️  Nenhum run_dir encontrado.", flush=True)
        return []

    rows = []
    for i, run_dir in enumerate(runs, 1):
        print(f"\n### [{i}/{len(runs)}] {os.path.relpath(run_dir, results_root)} ###", flush=True)
        try:
            r = auditar(run_dir, data_path=data_path, verbose=False)
            r["run_dir"] = os.path.relpath(run_dir, results_root)
            rows.append(r)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"   ❌ {type(e).__name__}: {e}", flush=True)

    if output_csv and rows:
        pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(output_csv, index=False)
        print(f"\n💾 {len(rows)} linhas salvas em: {output_csv}", flush=True)
    return rows


# =====================================================================
# CLI
# =====================================================================
if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--run_dir")
    p.add_argument("--results_root")
    p.add_argument("--data", default=None)
    p.add_argument("--output_csv", default=None)
    a = p.parse_args()

    if a.results_root:
        auditar_batch(a.results_root, a.data, a.output_csv)

    elif a.run_dir:
        res = auditar(a.run_dir, a.data)
        out_path = a.output_csv or os.path.join(a.run_dir, "auditoria_detecao.csv")
        pd.DataFrame([{**res, "run_dir": a.run_dir}]).to_csv(out_path, index=False)
        print(f"\n💾 CSV salvo em: {out_path}")

    else:
        p.error("Use --run_dir + --data OU --results_root.")

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\batch_inferir.py
################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Inferência em lote com dois modos bem separados:

--mode official     (default)
    Usa os thresholds salvos (user_thresholds.json) do treino.
    Produz 1 linha por modelo por dataset. Esta é a métrica oficial.

--mode sensitivity
    Varre percentis de threshold sobre o próprio dataset alvo.
    É uma ANÁLISE DE SENSIBILIDADE, NÃO uma avaliação confirmatória.
    Produz N linhas por modelo (uma por percentil). Rotuladas como
    evaluation_mode='sensitivity'.

Falha alto em qualquer erro de carregamento de modelo.
"""
import os
os.environ['TF_CPP_MIN_LOG_LEVEL'] = '3'
os.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES'] = '-1'

import sys
import glob
import gc
import pickle
import yaml
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import argparse
import traceback

current_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
project_root = current_dir
for _ in range(4):
    project_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(project_root, ".."))
    if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(project_root, "core")):
        break
if project_root not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root)

try:
    import torch
except ImportError:
    torch = None
from sklearn.metrics import precision_recall_curve, auc, roc_auc_score, confusion_matrix

from core.data.dataset_adapter import DatasetAdapter
from core.temporal_builder import TemporalBuilder
from core.models.model_factory import create as create_model
from core.trainer import Trainer


def clear_memory():
    gc.collect()
    try:
        import tensorflow as tf
        tf.keras.backend.clear_session()
    except ImportError:
        pass


def _load_model_from_dir(base_model, model_path):
    """Falha alto em erro — nunca retorna base_model sem pesos."""
    candidates = [
        os.path.join(model_path, "model.pkl"),
        os.path.join(model_path, "model.pt"),
        os.path.join(model_path, "model.keras"),
    ]
    target_file = next((c for c in candidates if os.path.exists(c)), None)

    if target_file is None:
        if hasattr(base_model, "load"):
            base_model.load(model_path)
            return base_model
        raise RuntimeError(f"Nenhum peso encontrado em: {model_path}")

    if target_file.endswith('.pkl'):
        with open(target_file, "rb") as pf:
            loaded_obj = pickle.load(pf)
        obj_module = type(loaded_obj).__module__
        if obj_module.startswith(('sklearn', 'xgboost', 'lightgbm', 'catboost')):
            base_model.model = loaded_obj
            return base_model
        elif hasattr(loaded_obj, "score") or hasattr(loaded_obj, "predict"):
            return loaded_obj
        elif isinstance(loaded_obj, dict) and hasattr(base_model, "load_state_dict"):
            base_model.load_state_dict(loaded_obj)
            return base_model
        else:
            base_model.model = loaded_obj
            return base_model

    elif target_file.endswith('.keras'):
        try:
            base_model.load(model_path)
            return base_model
        except Exception as e:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Falha ao carregar Keras em {target_file}: {e}")

    else:  # torch
        try:
            loaded_obj = torch.load(target_file, map_location=torch.device("cpu"), weights_only=False)
        except Exception as e:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Falha ao carregar torch model em {target_file}: {e}")
        if isinstance(loaded_obj, torch.nn.Module):
            return loaded_obj
        elif isinstance(loaded_obj, dict) and hasattr(base_model, "load_state_dict"):
            base_model.load_state_dict(loaded_obj)
            return base_model
        else:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Formato torch não reconhecido em {target_file}")

def _compute_metrics(y_test, y_pred, scores, eval_mode):
    if y_test is None or len(y_test) == 0:
        return {
            "evaluation_mode": eval_mode, "has_labels": 0,
            "pr_auc": float("nan"), "roc_auc": float("nan"),
            "f1_score": float("nan"), "precision": float("nan"), "recall": float("nan"),
            "tp": 0, "fp": 0, "tn": 0, "fn": 0,
        }

    if len(np.unique(y_test)) > 1:
        precision_curve, recall_curve, _ = precision_recall_curve(y_test, scores)
        pr_auc = auc(recall_curve, precision_curve)
        try:
            roc_auc = roc_auc_score(y_test, scores)
        except ValueError:
            roc_auc = float("nan")
    else:
        pr_auc = float("nan")
        roc_auc = float("nan")

    tn, fp, fn, tp = confusion_matrix(y_test, y_pred, labels=[0, 1]).ravel() if len(y_test) > 0 else (0, 0, 0, 0)
    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else float("nan")
    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else float("nan")
    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else float("nan")

    return {
        "evaluation_mode": eval_mode, "has_labels": 1,
        "pr_auc": pr_auc, "roc_auc": roc_auc,
        "f1_score": f1, "precision": precision, "recall": recall,
        "tp": int(tp), "fp": int(fp), "tn": int(tn), "fn": int(fn),
    }
    
'''def _compute_metrics(y_test, y_pred, scores, eval_mode):
    if len(np.unique(y_test)) > 1:
        precision_curve, recall_curve, _ = precision_recall_curve(y_test, scores)
        pr_auc = auc(recall_curve, precision_curve)
        try:
            roc_auc = roc_auc_score(y_test, scores)
        except ValueError:
            roc_auc = float("nan")
    else:
        pr_auc = float("nan")
        roc_auc = float("nan")

    tn, fp, fn, tp = confusion_matrix(y_test, y_pred, labels=[0, 1]).ravel() if len(y_test) > 0 else (0, 0, 0, 0)
    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0
    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0
    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0.0

    return {
        "evaluation_mode": eval_mode,
        "pr_auc": pr_auc,
        "roc_auc": roc_auc,
        "f1_score": f1,
        "precision": precision,
        "recall": recall,
        "tp": int(tp), "fp": int(fp), "tn": int(tn), "fn": int(fn),
    }
'''

def processar_modelo(model_dir, dataset_path, percentis_teste, mode):
    print(f"\n[{model_dir}] (mode={mode})")

    meta_path = os.path.join(model_dir, "trainer_meta.pkl")
    config_path = os.path.join(model_dir, "config_used.yaml")
    thresholds_path = os.path.join(model_dir, "user_thresholds.json")

    if not os.path.exists(config_path):
        print("  ⚠️ config_used.yaml ausente. Pulando.")
        return []

    with open(config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    with open(meta_path, "rb") as f:
        meta = pickle.load(f)
    scalers = meta['scalers']
    feature_columns = meta['feature_columns']

    if 'feature_processor' not in meta and 'feature_state' not in meta:
        print("  ❌ Meta sem 'feature_processor' nem 'feature_state'. Pulando.")
        return []
    if 'feature_processor' in meta:
        processor = meta['feature_processor']
    else:
        from core.features.feature_processor import FeatureProcessor
        processor = FeatureProcessor.restore(config, meta['feature_state'])
    if not getattr(processor, "is_fitted", False):
        print("  ❌ FeatureProcessor não fitted. Pulando.")
        return []
    processor.strict = True

    # Configura para usar o dataset alvo
    config.setdefault("data", {})
    config["data"]["test_path"] = dataset_path
    config["data"]["train_path"] = ""
    config["data"]["val_path"] = ""
    config["data"]["dataset_path"] = ""
    config.setdefault("sampling", {})["mode"] = "full"

    # Modelo (falha alto em erro)
    base_model = create_model(config)
    model_path = os.path.join(model_dir, "saved_model")
    final_model = _load_model_from_dir(base_model, model_path)

    if hasattr(final_model, "eval"):
        final_model.eval()

    trainer = Trainer(config, final_model)
    trainer.scalers = scalers
    trainer.feature_columns = feature_columns

    # Dataset
    adapter = DatasetAdapter(config)
    _, _, test_raw = adapter.load()

    if test_raw is None or test_raw.empty:
        print("  ❌ Dataset vazio. Pulando.")
        return []

    test_scaled = processor.transform(test_raw)

    if 'temporal_builder' in meta:
        temporal = meta['temporal_builder']
    else:
        temporal = TemporalBuilder(config)

    test_df = temporal.transform(test_scaled)

    X_test, _, users_test, y_test = trainer._build_sequences(
        test_df, fit=False, return_labels=True, feature_columns=feature_columns
    )

    if X_test is None or len(X_test) == 0:
        print("  ⚠️ Sem janelas. Pulando.")
        return []

    scores = trainer._calculate_scores(X_test)
    #if y_test is None:
    #    y_test = np.zeros(len(scores))

    model_type = config.get("model", {}).get("type", "")
    folder_name = os.path.basename(model_dir)
    if "randomforest" in folder_name.lower() or "random_forest" in model_type.lower():
        model_preset = "RandomForest"
    elif "mlp" in folder_name.lower() or "mlp" in model_type.lower():
        model_preset = "MLP_Single"
    else:
        model_preset = config.get("model_presets", [{}])[0].get("name", "Unknown")

    split_strategy = config.get("sampling", {}).get("split_strategy", "Unknown")
    if split_strategy == "Unknown":
        if "chronological" in folder_name.lower():
            split_strategy = "chronological"
        elif "random" in folder_name.lower():
            split_strategy = "random"

    thresh_mode = "per_user" if "per_user" in folder_name.lower() else ("global" if "global" in folder_name.lower() else config.get("training", {}).get("threshold_mode", "Unknown"))
    seed = config.get("training", {}).get("seed", "Unknown")
    dataset_tag = os.path.basename(dataset_path)

    base_info = {
        "source_dir": model_dir,
        "model_preset": model_preset,
        "threshold_mode": thresh_mode,
        "split_strategy": split_strategy,
        "seed": seed,
        "dataset_tag": dataset_tag,
    }

    resultados = []

    if mode == "official":
        # Usa thresholds salvos (oficial, calibrado na validação)
        if not os.path.exists(thresholds_path):
            print("  ❌ user_thresholds.json ausente. Pulando (modo official exige thresholds salvos).")
            return []
        with open(thresholds_path, "r") as f:
            user_thresholds = json.load(f)

        thresh_mode = config.get("training", {}).get("threshold_mode", "per_user")
        global_fb = user_thresholds.get("__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__", 0.5)
        thresholds_aplicados = np.array([
            user_thresholds.get(str(u), global_fb)
            if thresh_mode == "per_user" and str(u) != "__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__"
            else global_fb
            for u in users_test
        ])
        y_pred = (scores > thresholds_aplicados).astype(int)

        metrics = _compute_metrics(y_test, y_pred, scores, "official")
        resultados.append({**base_info, "percentile": None, "threshold_value": None, **metrics})
        print(f"  ✅ official: n_alerts={int(np.sum(y_pred))}")

    elif mode == "sensitivity":
        # Varredura de percentis (exploratória)
        for p in percentis_teste:
            threshold = np.percentile(scores, p)
            y_pred = (scores > threshold).astype(int)
            metrics = _compute_metrics(y_test, y_pred, scores, "sensitivity")
            resultados.append({**base_info, "percentile": p, "threshold_value": threshold, **metrics})
        print(f"  ✅ sensitivity: {len(percentis_teste)} percentis avaliados.")

    else:
        raise ValueError(f"mode inválido: {mode}")

    return resultados


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=str, default="results")
    parser.add_argument("--dataset", type=str, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=str, default="batch_inference_results.csv")
    parser.add_argument("--mode", type=str, default="official",
                        choices=["official", "sensitivity"],
                        help="official: thresholds salvos (métricas confirmatórias). "
                             "sensitivity: varredura de percentis (exploratória).")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    print("="*70)
    print(f"🚀 INFERÊNCIA EM LOTE | mode={args.mode.upper()}")
    print("="*70)
    print(f"📁 Raiz: {args.root}")
    print(f"🎯 Dataset: {args.dataset}")

    todos_resultados = []
    pastas_processadas = set()
    dataset_tag = os.path.basename(args.dataset)

    if os.path.exists(args.output):
        try:
            df_existente = pd.read_csv(args.output)
            if 'source_dir' in df_existente.columns and 'dataset_tag' in df_existente.columns:
                # Resume: só pula modelos JÁ processados NESTE dataset NESTE modo
                existing = df_existente[
                    (df_existente['dataset_tag'] == dataset_tag)
                    & (df_existente['evaluation_mode'] == args.mode)
                ]
                pastas_processadas = set(existing['source_dir'].unique())
                todos_resultados = df_existente.to_dict('records')
                print(f"🔄 Retomando: {len(pastas_processadas)} modelos já feitos neste dataset/modo.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Erro na retomada: {e}")

    pattern = os.path.join(glob.escape(args.root), "**", "trainer_meta.pkl")
    meta_files = glob.glob(pattern, recursive=True)

    if not meta_files:
        print(f"❌ Nenhum modelo em {args.root}.")
        return

    diretorios_modelos = sorted(set(os.path.dirname(f) for f in meta_files))
    print(f"🔍 {len(diretorios_modelos)} modelos encontrados.")

    percentis_teste = list(range(99, 89, -1))

    for i, model_dir in enumerate(diretorios_modelos, 1):
        print(f"\nProgresso: {i}/{len(diretorios_modelos)}")
        if model_dir in pastas_processadas:
            print("  ⏭️ Já processado (mesmo dataset e modo).")
            continue

        try:
            resultados = processar_modelo(model_dir, args.dataset, percentis_teste, args.mode)
            if resultados:
                todos_resultados.extend(resultados)
                pd.DataFrame(todos_resultados).to_csv(args.output, index=False)
                print(f"  💾 CSV atualizado ({len(todos_resultados)} linhas).")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  ❌ Erro crítico em {model_dir}: {e}")
            traceback.print_exc()
            print("  ⏭️ Continuando com o próximo modelo.")

        clear_memory()

    if todos_resultados:
        df_final = pd.DataFrame(todos_resultados)
        df_final.to_csv(args.output, index=False)
        print(f"\n✅ CONCLUÍDO — {len(df_final)} linhas em {args.output}")
        print(f"   Filtre por evaluation_mode='official' para métricas confirmatórias.")
    else:
        print("\n⚠️ Nada foi gerado.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\bootstrap_inferencia.py
################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Bootstrap pareado sobre os scores salvos por inferir_lote.py.

Para cada (model_dir, dataset):
  - Recarrega o threshold salvo do treino em user_thresholds.json
  - Usa os scores.npy já calculados (forward pass único)
  - Bootstrap pareado: MESMO resample aplicado às 3 políticas
  - Thresholds (global@p e per_user@p) recalculados DENTRO de cada resample
  - CI95% para F1 de cada política e para os Δ pareados

Uso:
    python scripts/bootstrap_inferencia.py
    # Saída: results/12_inferencia_campeoes/bootstrap_results.csv
"""

import os
import json
import yaml
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from numpy.random import default_rng
from sklearn.metrics import f1_score

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configurações
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
ROOT_PATH = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
BASE = os.path.join(ROOT_PATH, "results", "12_inferencia_campeoes")

DATASETS = ["target_prev01", "target_prev05", "target_prev10", "target_prev30"]
N_ITER = 1000
SEED = 42
MIN_USER_WINDOWS = 5


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Resolução de caminho (meta.json salva relativo)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
def resolve_model_dir(model_dir: str) -> str:
    if os.path.isabs(model_dir):
        return model_dir
    return os.path.join(ROOT_PATH, model_dir)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Carregar thresholds salvos do treino original
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
def load_saved_thresholds(model_dir: str):
    model_dir = resolve_model_dir(model_dir)
    thr_path = os.path.join(model_dir, "user_thresholds.json")
    cfg_path = os.path.join(model_dir, "config_used.yaml")

    if not os.path.exists(thr_path):
        return None, None

    with open(thr_path) as f:
        user_thresholds = json.load(f)

    thr_mode = "global"
    if os.path.exists(cfg_path):
        with open(cfg_path) as f:
            cfg = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
        thr_mode = cfg.get("training", {}).get("threshold_mode", "global")

    return user_thresholds, thr_mode


def compute_saved_pred(scores, users, user_thresholds, thr_mode):
    """Reproduz o y_pred das linhas saved_* do CSV."""
    gf = user_thresholds.get("__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__", 0.5)
    if thr_mode == "per_user":
        thr = np.array([user_thresholds.get(str(u), gf) for u in users])
    else:
        thr = np.full(len(scores), gf)
    return (scores > thr).astype(int)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Bootstrap pareado
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
def bootstrap_paired(scores, y_true, users, saved_pred, p_honesto,
                     n_iter=N_ITER, seed=SEED, min_user_windows=MIN_USER_WINDOWS):
    rng = default_rng(seed)
    n = len(scores)

    out = {
        "f1_saved":    np.empty(n_iter),
        "f1_global":   np.empty(n_iter),
        "f1_per_user": np.empty(n_iter),
    }

    s_all = np.asarray(scores, dtype=np.float64)
    y_all = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=np.int8)
    u_all = np.asarray([str(u) for u in users])

    for i in range(n_iter):
        idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=n)
        s, y, u = s_all[idx], y_all[idx], u_all[idx]

        # (a) saved — usa y_pred original reindexado
        out["f1_saved"][i] = f1_score(y, saved_pred[idx], zero_division=0)

        # (b) recalibragem global dentro do resample
        thr_g = np.percentile(s, p_honesto)
        out["f1_global"][i] = f1_score(
            y, (s > thr_g).astype(int), zero_division=0
        )

        # (c) recalibragem per_user dentro do resample
        thr_pu = np.full(n, thr_g)
        for uid in np.unique(u):
            m = (u == uid)
            if m.sum() >= min_user_windows:
                thr_pu[m] = np.percentile(s[m], p_honesto)
        out["f1_per_user"][i] = f1_score(
            y, (s > thr_pu).astype(int), zero_division=0
        )

    return out


def summarize(boot: dict) -> dict:
    res = {}
    for k, arr in boot.items():
        lo, hi = np.percentile(arr, [2.5, 97.5])
        res[f"{k}_mean"] = float(arr.mean())
        res[f"{k}_lo"]   = float(lo)
        res[f"{k}_hi"]   = float(hi)

    pairs = [
        ("d_recalib_global",     "f1_global",   "f1_saved"),
        ("d_switch_per_user",    "f1_per_user", "f1_saved"),
        ("d_per_user_vs_global", "f1_per_user", "f1_global"),
    ]
    for name, a_key, b_key in pairs:
        d = boot[a_key] - boot[b_key]
        lo, hi = np.percentile(d, [2.5, 97.5])
        res[f"{name}_mean"] = float(d.mean())
        res[f"{name}_lo"]   = float(lo)
        res[f"{name}_hi"]   = float(hi)
        res[f"{name}_sig"]  = int(lo > 0 or hi < 0)

    return res


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Parse metadados do caminho do modelo
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
def parse_run_name(run: str):
    split = "chronological" if "Chronological" in run else \
            ("random" if "Random" in run else "?")
    preset = next(
        (t for t in ["MLP_Autoencoder", "RandomForest", "MLP_Single"] if t in run),
        run,
    )
    return preset, split


def parse_seed_from_model_dir(model_dir: str):
    parts = model_dir.split("/")
    for p in parts:
        if p.startswith("run_seed"):
            try:
                return int(p.replace("run_seed", ""))
            except ValueError:
                pass
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Loop principal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
def main():
    rows = []
    skipped = []

    for dataset in DATASETS:
        ds_dir = os.path.join(BASE, dataset)
        if not os.path.isdir(ds_dir):
            print(f"⚠️  Dataset ausente: {ds_dir}")
            continue

        print(f"\n📂 {dataset}")

        for h in sorted(os.listdir(ds_dir)):
            d = os.path.join(ds_dir, h)
            if not os.path.isdir(d):
                continue

            needed = {"meta.json", "scores.npy", "y_true.npy", "users.npy"}
            if not needed.issubset(set(os.listdir(d))):
                skipped.append((d, "arquivos ausentes"))
                continue

            with open(os.path.join(d, "meta.json")) as f:
                meta = json.load(f)

            model_dir = meta["model_dir"]
            resolved = resolve_model_dir(model_dir)

            user_thresholds, thr_mode = load_saved_thresholds(model_dir)
            if user_thresholds is None:
                skipped.append((resolved, "user_thresholds.json ausente"))
                continue

            try:
                scores = np.load(os.path.join(d, "scores.npy")).astype(np.float64)
                y_true = np.load(os.path.join(d, "y_true.npy")).astype(np.int8)
                users  = np.load(os.path.join(d, "users.npy"), allow_pickle=True)
            except Exception as e:
                skipped.append((d, f"erro carregando .npy: {e}"))
                continue

            saved_pred = compute_saved_pred(scores, users, user_thresholds, thr_mode)

            prev = float(y_true.mean())
            p_honesto = 100.0 * (1.0 - prev)

            boot = bootstrap_paired(
                scores, y_true, users, saved_pred, p_honesto
            )
            stats = summarize(boot)

            run = os.path.basename(resolved)
            preset, split = parse_run_name(run)
            seed = parse_seed_from_model_dir(model_dir)

            row = {
                "dataset": dataset,
                "preset": preset,
                "split": split,
                "seed": seed,
                "run_name": run,
                "saved_thr_mode": thr_mode,
                "n_windows": int(len(y_true)),
                "n_anomalies": int(y_true.sum()),
                "prev_janela": round(prev, 4),
                "p_honesto": round(p_honesto, 1),
                **stats,
            }
            rows.append(row)

            print(
                f"  ✅ {preset:16s} {split:13s} seed={str(seed):4s} | "
                f"prev={prev:.3f} | "
                f"Δrecalib={row['d_recalib_global_mean']:+.4f} "
                f"[{row['d_recalib_global_lo']:+.4f},{row['d_recalib_global_hi']:+.4f}] "
                f"sig={row['d_recalib_global_sig']}"
            )

    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out_path = os.path.join(BASE, "bootstrap_results.csv")
    df.to_csv(out_path, index=False)
    print(f"\n📄 Resultados: {out_path}  ({len(df)} linhas)")

    if skipped:
        print(f"\n⚠️  Pulados ({len(skipped)}):")
        for path, why in skipped:
            print(f"   - {path}: {why}")

    return df


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\consolidate_results.py
################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Consolida todos os arquivos master_results_table.csv encontrados recursivamente
em uma pasta raiz, gerando um único CSV com todos os resultados.

Uso:
    python scripts/consolidate_results.py --root results --output resultados_consolidados.csv

Argumentos:
    --root    : Pasta raiz onde procurar (padrão: results)
    --output  : Caminho do arquivo de saída (padrão: resultados_consolidados.csv)
    --add_path: Adiciona coluna com o caminho de origem (padrão: True)
"""

import os
import sys
import glob
import pandas as pd
import argparse
from datetime import datetime

def consolidate_results(root_dir, output_file, add_path=True):
    """
    Procura recursivamente por master_results_table.csv e consolida em um único CSV.
    """
    if not os.path.exists(root_dir):
        print(f"❌ Erro: Pasta '{root_dir}' não encontrada.")
        return

    # Padrão de busca recursiva
    pattern = os.path.join(root_dir, "**", "master_results_table.csv")
    files = glob.glob(pattern, recursive=True)

    if not files:
        print(f"⚠️ Nenhum arquivo 'master_results_table.csv' encontrado em '{root_dir}'.")
        return

    print(f"🔍 Encontrados {len(files)} arquivos.")

    # Lista para armazenar os DataFrames
    dfs = []
    for f in files:
        try:
            df = pd.read_csv(f)
            if add_path:
                # Adiciona coluna com o caminho relativo (para rastreabilidade)
                rel_path = os.path.relpath(f, root_dir)
                df["source_file"] = rel_path
            dfs.append(df)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Erro ao ler {f}: {e}")

    if not dfs:
        print("❌ Nenhum dado válido foi lido.")
        return

    # Concatena todos os DataFrames
    df_all = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)

    # Remove duplicatas (caso haja) baseado em todas as colunas
    df_all = df_all.drop_duplicates()

    # Ordena por dataset, modelo, seed (se existirem)
    sort_cols = []
    for col in ["dataset", "model_preset", "seed"]:
        if col in df_all.columns:
            sort_cols.append(col)
    if sort_cols:
        df_all = df_all.sort_values(by=sort_cols).reset_index(drop=True)

    # Salva
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_file) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    df_all.to_csv(output_file, index=False)

    print(f"\n✅ Consolidação concluída!")
    print(f"📊 Total de linhas: {len(df_all)}")
    print(f"📁 Arquivo salvo em: {output_file}")
    print(f"📌 Colunas disponíveis: {list(df_all.columns)}")

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Consolida todos os master_results_table.csv de uma pasta.")
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=str, default="results",
                        help="Pasta raiz para busca recursiva (padrão: results)")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=str, default="resultados_consolidados.csv",
                        help="Caminho do arquivo de saída (padrão: resultados_consolidados.csv)")
    parser.add_argument("--no-path", action="store_true",
                        help="Não adiciona a coluna 'source_file' com o caminho de origem")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    consolidate_results(
        root_dir=args.root,
        output_file=args.output,
        add_path=not args.no_path
    )

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
os.environ['TF_CPP_MIN_LOG_LEVEL'] = '3'
os.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES'] = '0'

import sys
if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
import yaml
import copy
import json
import argparse
import time
import psutil
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from itertools import product
from datetime import datetime
import traceback
import gc
import shutil
import pickle

root_path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
if root_path not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, root_path)

try:
    from core.data.dataset_adapter import DatasetAdapter
    from core.features.feature_processor import FeatureProcessor
    from core.temporal_builder import TemporalBuilder
    from core.models.model_factory import create as create_model
    from core.trainer import Trainer
