except ImportError as e:
    print(f"❌ Erro de Importação: {e}")
    sys.exit(1)


class ExperimentRunner:
    def __init__(self, experiment_config_path):
        actual_exp_path = self._resolve_path(experiment_config_path)
        print(f"📖 Lendo configuração unificada: {actual_exp_path}")
        with open(actual_exp_path, "r", encoding='utf-8') as f:
            self.config = yaml.safe_load(f)

        self.seeds = self.config.get("settings", {}).get("seeds", [42])

        self.isolated_root = self.config.get("data", {}).get("isolated_tests_dir", None)
        if self.isolated_root:
            self.isolated_root = self._resolve_path(self.isolated_root)

        self.sampling_mode = self.config.get("sampling", {}).get("mode", "full")
        base_root = self.config.get("settings", {}).get("results_root", "results")
        self.results_root = os.path.join(base_root, self.sampling_mode)

        cache_root = self.config.get("settings", {}).get("cache_root", "data/GLOBAL_CACHE_DETECTOR")
        self.cache_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(root_path, cache_root))
        os.makedirs(self.cache_root, exist_ok=True)

    def _resolve_path(self, path):
        if not path: return path
        search_paths = [
            path,
            os.path.join(root_path, path),
            os.path.join("/content/drive/MyDrive/Project_Detector", path)
        ]
        for p in search_paths:
            if p and os.path.exists(p): return os.path.abspath(p)
        return path

    def _apply_overrides(self, config, overrides):
        if not overrides: return config
        for key, val in overrides.items():
            parts = key.split('.')
            d = config
            for part in parts[:-1]:
                d = d.setdefault(part, {})
            d[parts[-1]] = val
        return config

    def _validate_dataframe(self, df, name):
        if df is None or df.empty:
            print(f"⚠️ [WARNING] {name} está VAZIO!")
            return False
        if 'Time' not in df.columns:
            print(f"⚠️ [WARNING] {name} não tem coluna 'Time'")
            return False
        return True
    
    def _evaluate_subset(self, trainer, temporal, test_df, output_dir, config, subset_name, verbose=False, precomputed_test=None):
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        start_time = time.perf_counter()
        
        if precomputed_test is not None:
            X_test, indices, users, y_test = precomputed_test
        else:
            X_test, indices, users, y_test = trainer._build_sequences(test_df, fit=False, return_labels=True)

        if len(X_test) == 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Subset '{subset_name}' gerou 0 janelas. Não é métrica zero — é execução inválida."
            )

        scores = trainer._calculate_scores(X_test)
        
        global_fallback = trainer.global_threshold
        thresholds_aplicados = np.array([trainer.user_thresholds.get(str(u), global_fallback) for u in users])
        y_pred = (scores > thresholds_aplicados).astype(int)

        np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, "scores.npy"), scores)
        np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, "y_pred.npy"), y_pred)
        np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, "indices.npy"), indices)
        if y_test is not None:
            np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, "y_true.npy"), y_test)

        source_thresholds = os.path.join(trainer.output_dir, "user_thresholds.json")
        if os.path.exists(source_thresholds):
            shutil.copy(source_thresholds, os.path.join(output_dir, "user_thresholds.json"))

        eval_config = copy.deepcopy(config)
        eval_config["output_dir"] = output_dir
        
        try:
            metrics = run_external_evaluation(
                eval_config, test_df=test_df, scores=scores, indices=indices, verbose=verbose,
                y_true=y_test, users=users, y_pred=y_pred
            )
        except Exception as e:
            # Falha de avaliação NÃO é "desempenho ruim". É execução inválida.
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Falha ao avaliar subset '{subset_name}' em {output_dir}: {e}"
            ) from e
        end_time = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            mem_peak_mb = psutil.Process().memory_info().peak_wset / (1024*1024) if sys.platform == 'win32' else psutil.Process().memory_info().rss / (1024*1024)
        except:
            mem_peak_mb = 0.0

        return {**metrics, "elapsed_sec": end_time - start_time, "mem_peak_mb": mem_peak_mb}



    def run(self):
        datasets = self.config.get("datasets", [])
        model_presets = self.config.get("model_presets", [])
        hparam_grid = self.config.get("hyperparameter_grid", {})

        grid_keys = list(hparam_grid.keys())
        grid_values = list(hparam_grid.values())
        grid_combinations = list(product(*grid_values)) if grid_values else [()]

        total_runs = len(datasets) * len(model_presets) * max(1, len(grid_combinations)) * len(self.seeds)

        print("\n" + "="*70)
        print("🚀 RESUMO DA MARATONA DE EXPERIMENTOS")
        print("="*70)
        print(f" 🔹 Modo de Execução : {self.sampling_mode.upper()}")
        print(f" 🔹 Diretório Base   : {self.results_root}")
        print(f" 🔹 Cache Global     : {self.cache_root}")
        print(f" 🔹 Datasets         : {len(datasets)}")
        print(f" 🔹 Modelos (Presets): {len(model_presets)}")
        print(f" 🔹 Combinações Grid : {max(1, len(grid_combinations))}")
        print(f" 🔹 Sementes (Seeds) : {len(self.seeds)} {self.seeds}")
        print(f" 🎯 TOTAL DE RUNS    : {total_runs}")
        print("="*70 + "\n")

        for seed in self.seeds:
            print(f"\n{'#'*70}\n🌱 INICIANDO TREINO (SEED {seed})\n{'#'*70}\n")
            
            config_run_base = copy.deepcopy(self.config)
            config_run_base.setdefault("training", {})["seed"] = seed

            self.base_output_dir = os.path.join(self.results_root, f"run_seed{seed}")
            os.makedirs(self.base_output_dir, exist_ok=True)
            self.results_log = []
            master_csv_path = os.path.join(self.base_output_dir, "master_results_table.csv")

            # =========================================================
            # 🔥 PLANEJAMENTO: Gerar lista de tarefas para esta seed
            # =========================================================
            tarefas = []
            for ds in datasets:
                ds_name = ds.get("name", "unknown_ds")
                for mp in model_presets:
                    mp_name = mp.get("name", "unknown_model")
                    for combo in grid_combinations:
                        current_hparams = dict(zip(grid_keys, combo))
                        tarefas.append((ds_name, mp_name, current_hparams, combo))

            tarefas_pendentes = []
            if os.path.exists(master_csv_path):
                try:
                    existing_df = pd.read_csv(master_csv_path)
                    existing_df = existing_df[existing_df["seed"] == seed]
                    subset_cols = ["dataset", "model_preset", "training.threshold_mode", "model.sequence_length"]
                    available_subset = [col for col in subset_cols if col in existing_df.columns]
                    
                    if available_subset:
                        tarefas_df = pd.DataFrame([
                            {
                                "dataset": t[0],
                                "model_preset": t[1],
                                "training.threshold_mode": t[2].get("training.threshold_mode"),
                                "model.sequence_length": t[2].get("model.sequence_length")
                            }
                            for t in tarefas
                        ])
                        
                        merged = tarefas_df.merge(
                            existing_df[available_subset],
                            on=available_subset,
                            how="left",
                            indicator=True
                        )
                        tarefas_para_rodar = merged[merged["_merge"] == "left_only"].drop(columns=["_merge"])
                        
                        for _, row in tarefas_para_rodar.iterrows():
                            ds_name = row["dataset"]
                            mp_name = row["model_preset"]
                            current_hparams = {}
                            for k in grid_keys:
                                if k == "training.threshold_mode":
                                    current_hparams[k] = row["training.threshold_mode"]
                                elif k == "model.sequence_length":
                                    current_hparams[k] = row["model.sequence_length"]
                                else:
                                    for t_orig in tarefas:
                                        if t_orig[0] == ds_name and t_orig[1] == mp_name and t_orig[2].get("training.threshold_mode") == row["training.threshold_mode"] and t_orig[2].get("model.sequence_length") == row["model.sequence_length"]:
                                            current_hparams = t_orig[2]
                                            break
                            combo_orig = None
                            for t_orig in tarefas:
                                if t_orig[0] == ds_name and t_orig[1] == mp_name and t_orig[2] == current_hparams:
                                    combo_orig = t_orig[3]
                                    break
                            tarefas_pendentes.append((ds_name, mp_name, current_hparams, combo_orig))
                        
                        print(f"📊 Planejamento: {len(tarefas_pendentes)} tarefas pendentes de {len(tarefas)} totais.")
                    else:
                        tarefas_pendentes = tarefas
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"⚠️ Erro ao ler CSV para planejamento: {e}. Executando todas as tarefas.")
                    tarefas_pendentes = tarefas
            else:
                tarefas_pendentes = tarefas

            if not tarefas_pendentes:
                print(f"✅ Seed {seed}: todas as tarefas já foram executadas. Pulando.")
                continue

            # =========================================================
            # PROCESSAMENTO: Executar apenas as tarefas pendentes
            # =========================================================
            for ds in datasets:
                ds_name = ds.get("name", "unknown_ds")
                config_ds = self._apply_overrides(copy.deepcopy(config_run_base), ds.get("overrides", {}))

                tarefas_ds = [t for t in tarefas_pendentes if t[0] == ds_name]
                if not tarefas_ds:
                    continue

                # =========================================================
                # ETAPA 1: CACHE GLOBAL DE FEATURES (N1)
                # =========================================================
                split_strategy = config_ds.get("sampling", {}).get("split_strategy", "chronological")
                feature_cfg = config_ds.get("features", {})
                feature_set = feature_cfg.get("feature_set", "E0-legacy")
                feature_version = feature_cfg.get("feature_version", "1.0.0")
                cache_feature_tag = f"{feature_set}__{feature_version}"
                cache_dir = os.path.join(self.cache_root, ds_name, f"seed_{seed}", split_strategy, cache_feature_tag)
                os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
                
                cache_t_train = os.path.join(cache_dir, "t_train.parquet")
                cache_t_val = os.path.join(cache_dir, "t_val.parquet")
                cache_t_test = os.path.join(cache_dir, "t_test.parquet")
                cache_meta = os.path.join(cache_dir, "processor_meta.pkl")
                cache_iso = os.path.join(cache_dir, "isolated_datasets.pkl")

                run_isolated = config_ds.get("settings", {}).get("run_isolated_eval", True)

                if all(os.path.exists(p) for p in [cache_t_train, cache_t_val, cache_t_test, cache_meta]):
                    print(f"\n📦 [CACHE GLOBAL] Carregando features pré-computadas para '{ds_name}' (Seed:{seed}, Split:{split_strategy.upper()})...")
                    t_train = pd.read_parquet(cache_t_train)
                    t_val = pd.read_parquet(cache_t_val)
                    t_test = pd.read_parquet(cache_t_test)
                    
                    with open(cache_meta, "rb") as f:
                        meta_cache = pickle.load(f)
                        valid_features = meta_cache["valid_features"]
                        processor = meta_cache["processor"]
                        temporal = meta_cache["temporal"]

                    isolated_datasets = {}
                    if run_isolated and os.path.exists(cache_iso):
                        with open(cache_iso, "rb") as f:
                            isolated_datasets = pickle.load(f)
                else:
                    print(f"\n⚙️ [PROCESSAMENTO] Extraindo features do zero para '{ds_name}' e gravando no CACHE GLOBAL...")
                    adapter = DatasetAdapter(config_ds)
                    train_raw, val_raw, test_raw = adapter.load()

                    if not self._validate_dataframe(train_raw, "train") or not self._validate_dataframe(test_raw, "test"):
                        print(f"⚠️ Pulando dataset {ds_name}: Dataframes vazios.")
                        continue

                    processor = FeatureProcessor(config_ds)
                    if hasattr(processor, 'fit'): processor.fit(train_raw)
                    
                    train_scaled = processor.transform(train_raw)
                    val_scaled = processor.transform(val_raw)
                    test_scaled = processor.transform(test_raw)
                    
                    valid_features = [c for c in processor.final_feature_names if c in train_scaled.columns and c in val_scaled.columns]

                    isolated_datasets = {}
                    test_size = config_ds.get("sampling", {}).get("test_size", 0)
                    isolated_targets = config_ds.get("settings", {}).get("isolated_targets", [])
                    
                    if run_isolated and self.isolated_root and os.path.isdir(self.isolated_root):
                        for item in os.listdir(self.isolated_root):
                            if isolated_targets and item not in isolated_targets:
                                continue
                                
                            item_path = os.path.join(self.isolated_root, item)
                            if os.path.isdir(item_path) and item.startswith("test_"):
                                test_file = os.path.join(item_path, f"{item}.jsonl")
                                if os.path.exists(test_file):
                                    iso_raw = adapter._load_resource(test_file, test_size)
                                    iso_raw = adapter._normalize(iso_raw)
                                    iso_raw = adapter._apply_auto_labels(iso_raw, test_file)
                                    if iso_raw is not None and not iso_raw.empty:
                                        isolated_datasets[item] = processor.transform(iso_raw)

                    temporal = TemporalBuilder(config_ds)
                    t_train = temporal.transform(train_scaled)
                    t_val = temporal.transform(val_scaled)
                    t_test = temporal.transform(test_scaled)
                    
                    t_train.to_parquet(cache_t_train)
                    t_val.to_parquet(cache_t_val)
                    t_test.to_parquet(cache_t_test)
                    
                    with open(cache_meta, "wb") as f:
                        pickle.dump({
                            "valid_features": valid_features,
                            "processor": processor,
                            "temporal": temporal
                        }, f)
                        
                    if isolated_datasets:
                        with open(cache_iso, "wb") as f:
                            pickle.dump(isolated_datasets, f)
                            
                    del train_raw, val_raw, test_raw, train_scaled, val_scaled, test_scaled
                    gc.collect()

                # =========================================================
                # 🔥 OTIMIZAÇÃO: N2 Hot Cache em memória
                # =========================================================
                n2_bundles = {}  # chave: (seq_len, is_sup) -> bundle

                def get_n2_bundle(seq_len, is_sup):
                    """Carrega ou cria o N2 uma única vez por (seq_len, is_sup)."""
                    key = (seq_len, is_sup)
                    if key in n2_bundles:
                        return n2_bundles[key]

                    cache_seq_dir = os.path.join(cache_dir, f"seq_{seq_len}_sup_{is_sup}")
                    
                    if os.path.exists(cache_seq_dir) and os.path.exists(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "meta.pkl")):
                        print(f"\n   ⚡ [CACHE N2] Carregando matrizes de janelas (Seq={seq_len}, Sup={is_sup})...")
                        X_train = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "X_train.npy"))
                        y_train = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "y_train.npy"))
                        X_val = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "X_val.npy"))
                        val_users = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "val_users.npy"), allow_pickle=True)
                        y_val = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "y_val.npy"))
                        
                        X_test = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "X_test.npy"))
                        test_indices = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "test_indices.npy"))
                        test_users = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "test_users.npy"), allow_pickle=True)
                        y_test = np.load(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "y_test.npy"))
                        
                        with open(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "meta.pkl"), "rb") as f:
                            seq_meta = pickle.load(f)
                            scalers_dict = seq_meta["scalers"]
                            feat_cols = seq_meta["feature_columns"]
                    else:
                        print(f"\n   ⚙️ [PROCESSAMENTO] Fatiando matrizes de janelas (Seq={seq_len}, Sup={is_sup}) e salvando no cache...")
                        os.makedirs(cache_seq_dir, exist_ok=True)
                        
                        dummy_trainer = Trainer(config_final, None)  # será sobrescrito pelo config do modelo, mas só usamos _build_sequences
                        target = config_ds.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")
                        if not is_sup:
                            X_train, _, _, y_train = dummy_trainer._build_benign_training_sequences(
                                t_train, feature_columns=valid_features
                            )
                            if y_train is not None:
                                y_train = y_train[y_train == 0]
                        else:
                            X_train, _, _, y_train = dummy_trainer._build_sequences(
                                t_train, fit=True, return_labels=True,
                                feature_columns=valid_features
                            )
                        if y_train is None: y_train = np.zeros(len(X_train))
                        
                        X_val, _, val_users, y_val = dummy_trainer._build_sequences(t_val, fit=False, return_labels=True)
                        if y_val is None: y_val = np.zeros(len(X_val))
                        
                        X_test, test_indices, test_users, y_test = dummy_trainer._build_sequences(t_test, fit=False, return_labels=True)
                        if y_test is None: y_test = np.zeros(len(X_test))
                        
                        scalers_dict = dummy_trainer.scalers
                        feat_cols = dummy_trainer.feature_columns
                        
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "X_train.npy"), X_train)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "y_train.npy"), y_train)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "X_val.npy"), X_val)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "val_users.npy"), val_users)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "y_val.npy"), y_val)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "X_test.npy"), X_test)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "test_indices.npy"), test_indices)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "test_users.npy"), test_users)
                        np.save(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "y_test.npy"), y_test)
                        
                        with open(os.path.join(cache_seq_dir, "meta.pkl"), "wb") as f:
                            pickle.dump({"scalers": scalers_dict, "feature_columns": feat_cols}, f)

                    bundle = {
                        "X_train": X_train, "y_train": y_train,
                        "X_val": X_val, "val_users": val_users, "y_val": y_val,
                        "X_test": X_test, "test_indices": test_indices, "test_users": test_users, "y_test": y_test,
                        "scalers": scalers_dict, "feat_cols": feat_cols
                    }
                    n2_bundles[key] = bundle
                    return bundle

                # =========================================================
                # LOOP DE MODELOS (APENAS OS PENDENTES)
                # =========================================================
                for mp in model_presets:
                    mp_name = mp.get("name", "unknown_model")
                    tarefas_mp = [t for t in tarefas_ds if t[1] == mp_name]
                    if not tarefas_mp:
                        continue
                                              
                    for combo in grid_combinations:
                        # 🔥 CORREÇÃO: usa any() em vez de set()
                        # pois combos podem conter listas (ex: hidden_layer_sizes=[32, 16])
                        # e tuplas com listas não são hashable.
                        if not any(t[3] == combo for t in tarefas_mp):
                            continue           

                        current_hparams = dict(zip(grid_keys, combo))
                        
                        config_final = self._apply_overrides(copy.deepcopy(config_ds), mp.get("overrides", {}))
                        for k, v in current_hparams.items():
                            config_final = self._apply_overrides(config_final, {k: v})

                        seq_len = config_final.get("model", {}).get("sequence_length", 10)
                        is_sup = config_final.get("training", {}).get("supervised", False)
                        
                        hparam_str = "_".join([str(v) for v in combo])
                        modelo_str = f"{ds_name}_{mp_name}_{hparam_str}" if hparam_str else f"{ds_name}_{mp_name}"
                        modelo_str = modelo_str.replace("/", "-")
                        
                        run_dir = os.path.join(self.base_output_dir, modelo_str)
                        os.makedirs(run_dir, exist_ok=True)
                        config_final["output_dir"] = run_dir

                        print(f"--- 🏋️ Treinando: {modelo_str} ---")

                        # 🔥 Obtém o bundle N2 do cache em memória
                        bundle = get_n2_bundle(seq_len, is_sup)
                        
                        X_train = bundle["X_train"]
                        y_train = bundle["y_train"]
                        X_val = bundle["X_val"]
                        val_users = bundle["val_users"]
                        y_val = bundle["y_val"]
                        X_test = bundle["X_test"]
                        test_indices = bundle["test_indices"]
                        test_users = bundle["test_users"]
                        y_test = bundle["y_test"]
                        scalers_dict = bundle["scalers"]
                        feat_cols = bundle["feat_cols"]

                        # =========================================================
                        # TREINO E AVALIAÇÃO
                        # =========================================================
                        try:
                            model = create_model(config_final)
                            trainer = Trainer(config_final, model)
                            
                            trainer.scalers = scalers_dict
                            trainer.feature_columns = feat_cols
                            trainer.feature_processor = processor
                            trainer.temporal_builder = temporal
                            
                            # 🔥 Lê a métrica de otimização do YAML (com fallback para f2)
                            optim_metric = config_final.get("training", {}).get("optimization_metric", "f2")
                            if optim_metric.lower() != "f2":
                                raise ValueError(
                                    f"optimization_metric='{optim_metric}' não suportada. "
                                    "O Trainer atual implementa apenas F2. Ajuste o YAML."
                                )
                            
                            results = trainer.run(t_train, t_val, t_test, feature_columns=valid_features)                        

                            config_save_path = os.path.join(run_dir, "config_used.yaml")
                            with open(config_save_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                                yaml.dump(config_final, f)
                                
                            meta_path = os.path.join(run_dir, "trainer_meta.pkl")
                            if os.path.exists(meta_path):
                                with open(meta_path, "rb") as f:
                                    meta_trainer = pickle.load(f)
                                meta_trainer['feature_processor'] = processor
                                meta_trainer['temporal_builder'] = temporal
                                with open(meta_path, "wb") as f:
                                    pickle.dump(meta_trainer, f)

                            run_mixed = config_final.get("settings", {}).get("run_mixed_eval", True)
                            if run_mixed:
                                mixed_output = os.path.join(run_dir, "eval_mixed")
                                print(f"   🧪 Avaliando conjunto de teste misto...")
                                
                                precomp_test_bundle = (X_test, test_indices, test_users, y_test)
                                mixed_metrics = self._evaluate_subset(
                                    trainer, temporal, t_test, mixed_output, config_final, 
                                    subset_name=ds_name, verbose=True, precomputed_test=precomp_test_bundle
                                )
                                
                                log_entry = {"seed": seed, "dataset": ds_name, "model_preset": mp_name, **current_hparams, **results, **mixed_metrics}
                                self.results_log.append(log_entry)
                            else:
                                print(f"   ⏭️ Avaliação mista ignorada.")

                            if isolated_datasets and run_isolated:
                                print(f"   🧪 Avaliando {len(isolated_datasets)} ataques isolados...")
                                for iso_name, iso_scaled in isolated_datasets.items():
                                    iso_df = temporal.transform(iso_scaled.copy())
                                    iso_out = os.path.join(run_dir, f"eval_{iso_name}")
                                    iso_metrics = self._evaluate_subset(trainer, temporal, iso_df, iso_out, config_final, subset_name=iso_name, verbose=True)
                                    self.results_log.append({"seed": seed, "dataset": iso_name, "model_preset": mp_name, **current_hparams, **results, **iso_metrics})

                            print(f"✅ Execução {modelo_str} finalizada e salva.\n")

                        except Exception as e:
                            print(f"❌ ERRO CRÍTICO no modelo {mp_name} (combo: {combo}, seed: {seed}):")
                            traceback.print_exc()
                            print(f"   ⚠️ Pulando este modelo e continuando com os próximos...\n")
                            continue                            
                            
                        # =========================================================
                        # SALVAMENTO COM APPEND + MERGE
                        # =========================================================
                        new_df = pd.DataFrame(self.results_log)

                        if os.path.exists(master_csv_path) and not new_df.empty:
                            try:
                                existing_df = pd.read_csv(master_csv_path)

                                # 🔥 Deduplicação dinâmica
                                # Chave de identidade = colunas fixas + TODAS as colunas
                                # de hiperparâmetros (n_estimators, max_depth, hidden_layer_sizes, etc.)
                                # Excluímos métricas e metadados do critério de dedup.
                                colunas_identidade = [
                                    "seed", "dataset", "model_preset",
                                    "training.threshold_mode", "model.sequence_length",
                                ]

                                metricas_e_meta = {
                                    "val_score", "val_f2", "global_threshold", "best_percentile", "prevalence", "lift_pr",
                                    "pr_auc", "roc_auc", "f1_score", "precision", "recall",
                                    "tp", "fp", "tn", "fn", "elapsed_sec", "mem_peak_mb",
                                    "source_file", "split_strategy", "supervised",
                                }

                                for col in new_df.columns:
                                    if col not in colunas_identidade and col not in metricas_e_meta:
                                        colunas_identidade.append(col)

                                available_subset = [
                                    col for col in colunas_identidade
                                    if col in existing_df.columns and col in new_df.columns
                                ]

                                combined_df = pd.concat([existing_df, new_df], ignore_index=True)

                                # 🔥 CORREÇÃO: converte listas/tuplas em strings antes do drop_duplicates,
                                # pois listas não são hashable e quebram o drop_duplicates.
                                for col in combined_df.columns:
                                    if combined_df[col].apply(
                                        lambda x: isinstance(x, (list, tuple))
                                    ).any():
                                        combined_df[col] = combined_df[col].apply(
                                            lambda x: str(x)
                                            if isinstance(x, (list, tuple))
                                            else x
                                        )

                                if available_subset:
                                    combined_df = combined_df.drop_duplicates(
                                        subset=available_subset, keep="last"
                                    )

                                combined_df = combined_df.drop_duplicates()
                                combined_df.to_csv(master_csv_path, index=False)
                                print(f"💾 Resultados mesclados com sucesso (Total: {len(combined_df)} linhas).")

                            except Exception as e:
                                print(f"⚠️ Erro ao mesclar CSV existente: {e}. Salvando apenas os novos resultados.")
                                new_df.to_csv(master_csv_path, index=False)
                        else:
                            new_df.to_csv(master_csv_path, index=False)    
                            

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--exp_config", type=str, required=True, help="Caminho para o arquivo YAML único.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    runner = ExperimentRunner(args.exp_config)
    runner.run()

################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\eda_features.py
################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
EDA de atributos — read-only sobre os parquets + metadados já produzidos.
NÃO treina, NÃO retreina, NÃO toca no teste (usa apenas treino para as descritivas).

Uso:
  # 1) descobrir caches disponíveis
  python scripts/eda_features.py --discover

  # 2) rodar num cenário específico
  python scripts/eda_features.py \
      --cache_dir data/GLOBAL_CACHE_DETECTOR/Synthetic_Chronological/seed_42/chronological \
      --out results_eda/synthetic_chrono_42

  # 3) rodar em lote (vários cenários em sequência)
  python scripts/eda_features.py --batch \
      --targets Synthetic_Chronological:42:chronological \
                Synthetic_Random:42:random \
                CERT:42:chronological \
                LANL:42:chronological
"""

import os
import sys
import pickle
import argparse
import warnings
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns

from scipy.stats import ks_2samp
from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score

warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")

BASE = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))

if BASE not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, BASE)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Descoberta de caches
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def discover(cache_roots=None):
    if cache_roots is None:
        data_root = os.path.join(BASE, "data")
        cache_roots = [
            os.path.join("data", name)
            for name in sorted(os.listdir(data_root))
            if name.startswith("GLOBAL_CACHE")
            and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(data_root, name))
        ] if os.path.isdir(data_root) else []
    for cache_name in cache_roots:
        root = os.path.join(BASE, cache_name)
        if not os.path.exists(root):
            print(f"\n[não existe] {cache_name}")
            continue
        print(f"\n=== {cache_name} ===")
        for ds in sorted(os.listdir(root)):
            ds_path = os.path.join(root, ds)
            if not os.path.isdir(ds_path):
                continue
            for seed_dir in sorted(os.listdir(ds_path)):
                if not seed_dir.startswith("seed_"):
                    continue
                seed_path = os.path.join(ds_path, seed_dir)
                for split in sorted(os.listdir(seed_path)):
                    split_path = os.path.join(seed_path, split)
                    if not os.path.isdir(split_path):
                        continue
                    has_old = os.path.exists(os.path.join(split_path, "t_train.parquet"))
                    has_new = os.path.exists(os.path.join(split_path, "train.parquet"))
                    fmt = "OLD" if has_old else ("NEW" if has_new else "??")
                    print(f"  {ds:45s} {seed_dir:12s} {split:15s} [{fmt}]")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Carregamento com detecção automática de formato
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_cache(cache_dir):
    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(cache_dir, "t_train.parquet")):
        fmt = "OLD"
        f_train = "t_train.parquet"
        f_test  = "t_test.parquet"
        f_meta  = "processor_meta.pkl"
    elif os.path.exists(os.path.join(cache_dir, "train.parquet")):
        fmt = "NEW"
        f_train = "train.parquet"
        f_test  = "test.parquet"
        f_meta  = "n1_meta.pkl"
    else:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Nenhum cache reconhecido em: {cache_dir}")

    train = pd.read_parquet(os.path.join(cache_dir, f_train))
    test  = pd.read_parquet(os.path.join(cache_dir, f_test)) if os.path.exists(
        os.path.join(cache_dir, f_test)) else None

    with open(os.path.join(cache_dir, f_meta), "rb") as f:
        meta = pickle.load(f)

    # Compatibilidade entre formatos
    features = meta.get("valid_features") or meta.get("feature_columns") or []

    # Fallback defensivo: extrai colunas numéricas do parquet se os metadados estiverem vazios
    if not features:
        target_col = "Is_Anomaly"
        excluded = {"UserID", "Time", target_col, "AnomalyType", "session_id", "datetime", "user", "Username"}
        features = [c for c in train.columns if c not in excluded and pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(train[c])]
        if not features:
            raise ValueError("Nenhuma feature numérica detectada no parquet — abortando.")
        print(f"   ⚠️  'valid_features' vazio no meta; recuperadas {len(features)} features do parquet.")

    return fmt, train, test, meta, features


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Blocos de análise
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _descriptive_by_class(train, features, out):
    rows = []
    for f in features:
        for lab, name in [(0, "normal"), (1, "anomalia")]:
            s = train.loc[train["Is_Anomaly"] == lab, f]
            if len(s) == 0:
                continue
            rows.append(dict(
                feature=f, classe=name, n=len(s),
                mean=float(s.mean()), std=float(s.std()),
                median=float(s.median()), min=float(s.min()), max=float(s.max()),
                pct_zero=float((s == 0).mean() * 100),
                pct_nan=float(s.isna().mean() * 100),
            ))
    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(out, "descriptive_by_class.csv"), index=False)
    return df


def _univariate_auc_ks(train, features, out):
    y = train["Is_Anomaly"].values
    rows = []
    for f in features:
        x = train[f].values
        if np.isnan(x).all() or len(np.unique(x[~np.isnan(x)])) < 2:
            rows.append(dict(feature=f, auc=0.5, ks_stat=0.0, ks_p=1.0, note="constante/NaN"))
            continue
        try:
            auc = roc_auc_score(y, np.nan_to_num(x))
            auc = max(auc, 1 - auc)  # direção-agnóstica
        except ValueError:
            auc = 0.5
        try:
            ks, p = ks_2samp(x[y == 0], x[y == 1])
        except Exception:
            ks, p = 0.0, 1.0
        rows.append(dict(feature=f, auc=float(auc), ks_stat=float(ks), ks_p=float(p), note=""))
    df = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("auc", ascending=False)
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(out, "univariate_auc.csv"), index=False)
    return df


def _correlation_and_vif(train, features, out):
    X = train[features].replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0)

    corr = X.corr(method="spearman")
    corr.to_csv(os.path.join(out, "correlation_spearman.csv"))

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 10))
    sns.heatmap(corr, cmap="coolwarm", center=0, square=True, cbar_kws={"shrink": 0.7})
    plt.title("Correlação de Spearman — treino (features)")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(os.path.join(out, "correlation_heatmap.png"), dpi=180)
    plt.close()

    # VIF (opcional — requer statsmodels)
    try:
        from statsmodels.stats.outliers_influence import variance_inflation_factor
        Xv = X.loc[:, X.std() > 0]
        vif_rows = []
        for i, f in enumerate(Xv.columns):
            try:
                vif_rows.append(dict(feature=f, vif=float(variance_inflation_factor(Xv.values, i))))
            except Exception:
                vif_rows.append(dict(feature=f, vif=np.nan))
        pd.DataFrame(vif_rows).sort_values("vif", ascending=False).to_csv(
            os.path.join(out, "vif.csv"), index=False)
    except ImportError:
        print("    ⚠️  statsmodels ausente — VIF pulado. `pip install statsmodels` para incluir.")


def _mutual_information(train, features, out):
    from sklearn.feature_selection import mutual_info_classif
    X = train[features].replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0)
    y = train["Is_Anomaly"].values
    mi = mutual_info_classif(X, y, random_state=42, discrete_features=False)
    df = pd.DataFrame({"feature": features, "mutual_info": mi}).sort_values(
        "mutual_info", ascending=False)
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(out, "mutual_information.csv"), index=False)
    return df


def _per_attack_profile(train, features, out):
    if "AnomalyType" not in train.columns:
        return None
    df = train.groupby("AnomalyType")[features].mean()
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(out, "profile_by_attack.csv"))
    return df


def _per_user_auc_and_per_attack(train, features, out):
    # Identifica coluna de identificação de usuário
    user_col = None
    for cand in ["UserID", "user", "Username", "user_id"]:
        if cand in train.columns:
            user_col = cand
            break

    # (a) AUC normalizada por usuário
    if user_col is not None:
        train_norm = train.copy()
        for f in features:
            train_norm[f] = train.groupby(user_col)[f].transform(
                lambda x: (x - x.mean()) / (x.std() + 1e-6)
            )

        y = train_norm["Is_Anomaly"].values
        rows_user = []
        for f in features:
            x = np.nan_to_num(train_norm[f].values)
            try:
                auc = roc_auc_score(y, x)
                auc = max(auc, 1 - auc)
            except ValueError:
                auc = 0.5
            rows_user.append(dict(feature=f, auc_per_user=auc))

        pd.DataFrame(rows_user).sort_values("auc_per_user", ascending=False).to_csv(
            os.path.join(out, "univariate_auc_per_user.csv"), index=False
        )
    else:
        print("    ⚠️  Coluna de identificador de usuário não encontrada. Pulando AUC per-user.")

    # (b) AUC por família de ataque (event-level)
    if "AnomalyType" in train.columns:
        rows_atk = []
        for atk in train["AnomalyType"].dropna().unique():
            if str(atk).lower() == "normal":
                continue
            sub = train[train["AnomalyType"].isin([atk, "normal"])]
            if len(sub) == 0 or len(sub["AnomalyType"].unique()) < 2:
                continue
            y_atk = (sub["AnomalyType"] == atk).astype(int).values
            for f in features:
                x = np.nan_to_num(sub[f].values)
                try:
                    auc = roc_auc_score(y_atk, x)
                    auc = max(auc, 1 - auc)
                except ValueError:
                    auc = 0.5
                rows_atk.append(dict(attack=atk, feature=f, auc=auc))

        if rows_atk:
            pd.DataFrame(rows_atk).to_csv(
                os.path.join(out, "univariate_auc_per_attack.csv"), index=False
            )
    else:
        print("    ⚠️  Coluna 'AnomalyType' ausente. Pulando AUC per-attack.")


def _plot_auc_comparison(out):
    global_path = os.path.join(out, "univariate_auc.csv")
    per_user_path = os.path.join(out, "univariate_auc_per_user.csv")
    if not (os.path.exists(global_path) and os.path.exists(per_user_path)):
        return

    g = pd.read_csv(global_path)[["feature", "auc"]].rename(columns={"auc": "auc_global"})
    u = pd.read_csv(per_user_path)[["feature", "auc_per_user"]]
    merged = g.merge(u, on="feature").sort_values("auc_per_user", ascending=False)
    merged["delta"] = merged["auc_per_user"] - merged["auc_global"]

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, max(6, 0.35 * len(merged))))
    y = np.arange(len(merged))
    ax.barh(y - 0.2, merged["auc_global"], height=0.4, label="AUC global", color="#4C72B0")
    ax.barh(y + 0.2, merged["auc_per_user"], height=0.4, label="AUC per-user", color="#DD8452")
    ax.set_yticks(y)
    ax.set_yticklabels(merged["feature"])
    ax.axvline(0.5, ls="--", color="gray", label="acaso (0.5)")
    ax.set_xlim(0.45, 1.0)
    ax.set_xlabel("AUC (direção-agnóstica)")
    ax.set_title("Poder discriminativo: AUC global vs. AUC normalizada por usuário")
    ax.legend(loc="lower right")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(os.path.join(out, "auc_global_vs_per_user.png"), dpi=180)
    plt.close()


def _plots(top_features, train, univ, out):
    # Violino das top-10 por AUC global
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 5, figsize=(22, 8))
    for ax, f in zip(axes.ravel(), top_features[:10]):
        sns.violinplot(data=train, x="Is_Anomaly", y=f, ax=ax, cut=0, inner="quartile")
        ax.set_xlabel("")
        ax.set_title(f, fontsize=10)
    plt.suptitle("Top-10 atributos por AUC univariada global — distribuição por classe (treino)", fontsize=13)
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(os.path.join(out, "violin_top10.png"), dpi=180)
    plt.close()

    # Barras de AUC
    plt.figure(figsize=(9, max(6, 0.3 * len(univ))))
    sns.barplot(data=univ, y="feature", x="auc", orient="h", palette="viridis")
    plt.axvline(0.5, ls="--", color="gray", label="acaso (0.5)")
    plt.title("AUC univariada por atributo (poder discriminativo isolado)")
    plt.xlim(0.45, 1.0)
    plt.legend()
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(os.path.join(out, "univariate_auc.png"), dpi=180)
    plt.close()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pipeline principal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def run_eda(cache_dir, out_dir):
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"\n📂 cache: {cache_dir}")
    fmt, train, test, meta, features = _load_cache(cache_dir)
    print(f"   formato  : {fmt}")
    print(f"   treino   : {train.shape}")
    print(f"   teste    : {test.shape if test is not None else 'n/a'}")
    print(f"   features : {len(features)}")

    if "Is_Anomaly" not in train.columns:
        raise ValueError("Coluna 'Is_Anomaly' ausente no treino.")

    print("   → descritivas por classe...")
    _descriptive_by_class(train, features, out_dir)

    print("   → AUC univariada + KS...")
    univ = _univariate_auc_ks(train, features, out_dir)

    print("   → correlação + VIF...")
    _correlation_and_vif(train, features, out_dir)

    print("   → informação mútua...")
    _mutual_information(train, features, out_dir)

    print("   → perfil por família de ataque...")
    _per_attack_profile(train, features, out_dir)

    print("   → AUC per-user + per-attack...")
    _per_user_auc_and_per_attack(train, features, out_dir)

    print("   → gráficos...")
    top = univ[~univ["note"].eq("constante/NaN")].head(10)["feature"].tolist()
    if top:
        _plots(top, train, univ, out_dir)

    # Gráfico comparativo AUC global vs. per-user
    _plot_auc_comparison(out_dir)

    print(f"✅ EDA concluída em: {out_dir}")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--discover", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--cache_dir", type=str, default=None,
                        help="Caminho relativo a BASE, ex: data/GLOBAL_CACHE_DETECTOR/Synthetic_Chronological/seed_42/chronological")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=str, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--batch", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--targets", nargs="*", default=[],
                        help="Formato: <ds>:<seed>:<split>")
    parser.add_argument("--cache_root", type=str, default=None)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.discover:
        discover([args.cache_root] if args.cache_root else None)
        return

    if args.batch and args.targets:
        for t in args.targets:
            ds, seed, split = t.split(":")
            cache_root = args.cache_root or "data/GLOBAL_CACHE_DETECTOR"
            cache_rel = f"{cache_root}/{ds}/seed_{seed}/{split}"
            out = f"results_eda/{ds}__seed{seed}__{split}"
            run_eda(os.path.join(BASE, cache_rel), os.path.join(BASE, out))
        return

    if not args.cache_dir or not args.out:
        parser.error("Use --discover, ou --cache_dir + --out, ou --batch --targets ...")

    run_eda(os.path.join(BASE, args.cache_dir), os.path.join(BASE, args.out))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\evaluate_performance.py
################################################################################

# scripts/evaluate_performance.py
#
# Avaliação de desempenho para detecção comportamental UEBA.
# Compatível retroativamente com JSON anterior e estendido na Fase 6 com:
# - Average Precision (NaN se sem positivos)
# - Precision@K / Recall@K para K in {50, 100, 200}
# - MCC, Balanced Accuracy, F2-Score
# - Taxa operacional de alertas/dia e alertas/usuário

import os
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import (
    classification_report,
    precision_recall_curve,
    auc,
    roc_auc_score,
    confusion_matrix,
    average_precision_score,
    matthews_corrcoef,
    balanced_accuracy_score,
)
from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import sliding_window_view


def _compute_precision_recall_at_k(y_true: np.ndarray, scores: np.ndarray, k: int):
    n = len(scores)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, 0.0
    k_eff = min(k, n)
    top_indices = np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:k_eff]
    tp_k = int(np.sum(y_true[top_indices] == 1))
    prec_k = float(tp_k / k_eff) if k_eff > 0 else 0.0
    n_pos = int(np.sum(y_true == 1))
    rec_k = float(tp_k / n_pos) if n_pos > 0 else 0.0
    return prec_k, rec_k


def run_external_evaluation(config, test_df=None, scores=None, indices=None, verbose=False, y_true=None, users=None, y_pred=None):
    output_dir = config.get("output_dir", "./output")
    target_col = config.get("data", {}).get("target_col", "Is_Anomaly")
    sequence_length = config.get("model", {}).get("sequence_length", 10)
    thresh_mode = config.get("training", {}).get("threshold_mode", "per_user")

    # 1. Carrega scores e índices se não fornecidos em memória
    if scores is None or indices is None:
        scores_file = os.path.join(output_dir, "scores.npy") if os.path.exists(os.path.join(output_dir, "scores.npy")) else os.path.join(output_dir, "test_scores.npy")
        indices_file = os.path.join(output_dir, "indices.npy") if os.path.exists(os.path.join(output_dir, "indices.npy")) else os.path.join(output_dir, "test_indices.npy")
        scores = np.load(scores_file)
        indices = np.load(indices_file)

    # 2. Alinhamento Estrito de Rótulos (sempre com sort=False)
    if y_true is not None:
        y_test = np.asarray(y_true).astype(int)
    elif os.path.exists(os.path.join(output_dir, "y_true.npy")):
        y_test = np.load(os.path.join(output_dir, "y_true.npy")).astype(int)
    elif test_df is not None and not test_df.empty:
        y_test_list = []
        for user_id, group in test_df.groupby("UserID", sort=False):
            if len(group) < sequence_length:
                continue
            windows_labels = sliding_window_view(group[target_col].values, window_shape=sequence_length)
            y_test_list.append(np.max(windows_labels, axis=1))

        y_test = np.concatenate(y_test_list) if y_test_list else np.array([])
        y_test = np.nan_to_num(y_test, nan=0).astype(int)
    else:
        y_test = np.array([])

    if len(y_test) != len(scores):
        min_len = min(len(y_test), len(scores))
        y_test, scores = y_test[:min_len], scores[:min_len]

    # 3. Predições e Limiares
    if y_pred is not None:
        y_pred = np.asarray(y_pred).astype(int)
        if len(y_pred) != len(scores):
            y_pred = y_pred[:len(scores)]
    else:
        user_thresholds = {}
        thresholds_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "user_thresholds.json")
        if os.path.exists(thresholds_path):
            with open(thresholds_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                user_thresholds = json.load(f)

        global_fallback = user_thresholds.get("__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__", 0.5)
        if users is not None:
            test_users = [str(u) for u in users]
        else:
            user_mapping = test_df["UserID"].to_dict() if test_df is not None and "UserID" in test_df.columns else {}
            test_users = [str(user_mapping.get(idx, "unknown")) for idx in indices]

        sample_thresholds = np.array([
            user_thresholds.get(u, global_fallback) if thresh_mode == "per_user" and u != "__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__" else global_fallback 
            for u in test_users
        ])
        y_pred = (scores > sample_thresholds).astype(int)

    # 4. Cálculo das Métricas Canônicas e Novas Métricas (Fase 6)
    n_pos = int(np.sum(y_test == 1))
    
    # Average Precision (NaN se sem positivos)
    if n_pos > 0:
        avg_precision = float(average_precision_score(y_test, scores))
    else:
        avg_precision = None

    precision_curve, recall_curve, _ = precision_recall_curve(y_test, scores) if len(np.unique(y_test)) > 1 else ([], [], [])
    pr_auc = float(auc(recall_curve, precision_curve)) if len(precision_curve) > 0 else 0.0
    try:
        roc_auc = float(roc_auc_score(y_test, scores)) if len(np.unique(y_test)) > 1 else 0.5
    except ValueError:
        roc_auc = 0.5 

    tn, fp, fn, tp = confusion_matrix(y_test, y_pred, labels=[0, 1]).ravel() if len(y_test) > 0 else (0, 0, 0, 0)
    report_dict = classification_report(y_test, y_pred, output_dict=True, zero_division=0)
    class_1 = report_dict.get("1", report_dict.get("1.0", report_dict.get("True", {})))

    prec = float(class_1.get("precision", 0.0))
    rec = float(class_1.get("recall", 0.0))
    f1 = float(class_1.get("f1-score", 0.0))

    # F2-Score
    f2 = float((1.0 + 4.0) * (prec * rec) / ((4.0 * prec) + rec)) if (4.0 * prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0

    # MCC e Balanced Accuracy
    if len(np.unique(y_test)) > 1 and len(np.unique(y_pred)) > 1:
        mcc = float(matthews_corrcoef(y_test, y_pred))
    else:
        mcc = 0.0
    balanced_acc = float(balanced_accuracy_score(y_test, y_pred)) if len(np.unique(y_test)) > 1 else 0.5

    # Precision@K e Recall@K para K in {50, 100, 200}
    p50, r50 = _compute_precision_recall_at_k(y_test, scores, 50)
    p100, r100 = _compute_precision_recall_at_k(y_test, scores, 100)
    p200, r200 = _compute_precision_recall_at_k(y_test, scores, 200)

    # Taxas operacionais de alertas
    total_alerts = int(np.sum(y_pred))
    n_users = len(np.unique(users)) if users is not None and len(users) else (test_df["UserID"].nunique() if test_df is not None and "UserID" in test_df.columns else 1)
    alerts_per_user = float(total_alerts / max(n_users, 1))

    time_span_days = 1.0
    if test_df is not None and "Time" in test_df.columns and len(test_df) > 1:
        span_sec = float(test_df["Time"].max() - test_df["Time"].min())
        time_span_days = max(span_sec / 86400.0, 1.0 / 24.0)
    alerts_per_day = float(total_alerts / time_span_days)

    prevalence = float(np.mean(y_test)) if len(y_test) else 0.0
    lift_pr = float(pr_auc / prevalence) if prevalence > 0 else 0.0

    # Dicionário compatível com versões legadas
    metrics_dict = {
        "pr_auc": pr_auc,
        "prevalence": prevalence,
        "lift_pr": lift_pr,
        "roc_auc": roc_auc,
        "f1_score": f1,
        "f2_score": f2,
        "precision": prec,
        "recall": rec,
        "tp": int(tp), "fp": int(fp), "tn": int(tn), "fn": int(fn),
        # Novas métricas Fase 6
        "average_precision": avg_precision,
        "mcc": mcc,
        "balanced_accuracy": balanced_acc,
        "precision_at_50": p50,
        "recall_at_50": r50,
        "precision_at_100": p100,
        "recall_at_100": r100,
        "precision_at_200": p200,
        "recall_at_200": r200,
        "alerts_per_day": alerts_per_day,
        "alerts_per_user": alerts_per_user,
        "total_alerts": total_alerts,
    }

    with open(os.path.join(output_dir, "classification_report.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(metrics_dict, f, indent=4)

    if verbose:
        total_events = len(test_df) if test_df is not None else len(scores)
        anom_events = int(test_df[target_col].sum()) if test_df is not None and target_col in test_df.columns else n_pos
        prev_events = (anom_events / total_events * 100) if total_events > 0 else 0.0

        total_windows = len(y_test)
        anom_windows = int(np.sum(y_test))
        prev_windows = (anom_windows / total_windows * 100) if total_windows > 0 else 0.0

        print("\n" + "="*60)
        print("📊 RESUMO DA AUDITORIA DE DETECÇÃO (DOUBLE CHECK)")
        print("="*60)
        print(f" 🔹 Eventos Totais (Brutos)      : {total_events}")
        print(f" 🔹 Eventos Anômalos (Brutos)    : {anom_events} (Prevalência: {prev_events:.2f}%)")
        print("-" * 60)
        print(f" 🔹 Janelas Avaliadas (Seq={sequence_length})   : {total_windows}")
        print(f" 🔹 Janelas Anômalas (Gabarito)  : {anom_windows} (Prevalência: {prev_windows:.2f}%)")
        print(f" 🔹 Alertas Disparados           : {total_alerts} ({alerts_per_day:.1f}/dia, {alerts_per_user:.1f}/user)")
        print("-" * 60)
        print(f" ✅ Verdadeiros Positivos (TP)   : {int(tp)}")
        print(f" ⚠️  Falsos Positivos (FP)       : {int(fp)}")
        print(f" ❌ Falsos Negativos (FN)       : {int(fn)}")
        print(f" 🛡️  Verdadeiros Negativos (TN)   : {int(tn)}")
        print("-" * 60)
        print(f" 🎯 Precision                   : {prec:.4f} | Recall: {rec:.4f}")
        print(f" 🎯 F1-Score: {f1:.4f} | F2-Score: {f2:.4f} | MCC: {mcc:.4f}")
        print(f" 🎯 PR-AUC: {pr_auc:.4f} | Lift: {lift_pr:.2f}x | Avg Precision: {avg_precision}")
        print(f" 🎯 Precision@50: {p50:.4f} | Recall@50: {r50:.4f}")
        print(f" 🎯 Precision@100: {p100:.4f} | Recall@100: {r100:.4f}")
        print(f" 🎯 ROC-AUC                     : {roc_auc:.4f}")
        print("="*60 + "\n")
        
    return metrics_dict


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\extract_anomalies.py
################################################################################

import argparse
import json
import yaml
import glob
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import os
from sklearn.metrics import precision_recall_curve, auc
from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import sliding_window_view

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Auditoria estrita alinhada ao classification_report com leitura inteligente de YAML (Suporta Debug).")
    parser.add_argument("--output_dir", type=str, required=True, help="Pasta da run (ex: results/LAST_RUN)")
    parser.add_argument("--test_csv", type=str, required=True, help="Caminho do test.jsonl")
    parser.add_argument("--labels_csv", type=str, default=None, help="Caminho do labels.csv (se o gabarito estiver separado)")
    parser.add_argument("--seq_len", type=int, default=None, help="Tamanho da janela (força um valor, ignorando o YAML se preenchido)")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. INTELIGÊNCIA DE CONFIGURAÇÃO (ANTES DE CARREGAR OS DADOS)
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    run_config = {}
    seq_len = args.seq_len
    
    # Procura o YAML na pasta de saída ou na pasta pai (útil para /eval_mixed)
    yaml_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(args.output_dir, "*.yaml"))
    if not yaml_files:
        parent_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.normpath(args.output_dir))
        yaml_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(parent_dir, "*.yaml"))
        
    if yaml_files:
        config_encontrado = yaml_files[0]
        print(f"🔗 Lendo parâmetros automaticamente de: {config_encontrado}")
        try:
            with open(config_encontrado, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                run_config = yaml.safe_load(f)
                if seq_len is None:
                    seq_len = run_config.get("model", {}).get("sequence_length")
                    if seq_len is not None:
                        print(f"⚙️ sequence_length recuperado do YAML: {seq_len}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Erro ao ler o YAML: {e}")

    # Descobre se rodou em modo debug
    sampling_mode = run_config.get("sampling", {}).get("mode", "full")
    test_size = run_config.get("sampling", {}).get("test_size", 1000)
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. CARREGAMENTO INTELIGENTE DOS DADOS
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    print(f"📂 Carregando dados de teste: {args.test_csv}")
    
    # Lê apenas o necessário se for modo debug (como o DatasetAdapter faz)
    if sampling_mode == "debug" and test_size > 0:
        print(f"🐛 Modo DEBUG detectado no YAML. Carregando apenas as primeiras {test_size} linhas.")
        if args.test_csv.endswith(".jsonl"):
            test_df = pd.read_json(args.test_csv, lines=True, nrows=test_size)
        else:
            test_df = pd.read_csv(args.test_csv, nrows=test_size)
    else:
        if args.test_csv.endswith(".jsonl"):
            test_df = pd.read_json(args.test_csv, lines=True)
        else:
            test_df = pd.read_csv(args.test_csv)

    test_df.columns = [str(c).strip() for c in test_df.columns]
    col_map = {
        'userid': 'UserID', 'user': 'UserID', 'username': 'UserID',
        'timestamp': 'Time', 'time': 'Time', 'datetime': 'Time',
        'is_anomaly': 'Is_Anomaly', 'target': 'Is_Anomaly', 'label': 'Is_Anomaly'
    }
    
    cols_low = {str(c).lower(): c for c in test_df.columns}
    rename_map = {cols_low[k]: v for k, v in col_map.items() if k in cols_low}
    if rename_map:
        test_df = test_df.rename(columns=rename_map)

    # Injeção de gabarito externo (se necessário)
    if args.labels_csv and os.path.exists(args.labels_csv):
        print(f"🔗 Injetando gabarito externo: {args.labels_csv}")
        labels_df = pd.read_csv(args.labels_csv)
        labels_df.columns = [str(c).strip() for c in labels_df.columns]
        labels_low = {str(c).lower(): c for c in labels_df.columns}
        labels_df = labels_df.rename(columns={labels_low.get(k, k): v for k, v in col_map.items()})
        
        test_df = pd.merge(test_df, labels_df[['UserID', 'Time', 'Is_Anomaly']], on=['UserID', 'Time'], how='left')
        test_df['Is_Anomaly'] = test_df['Is_Anomaly'].fillna(0).astype(int)

    if test_df.columns.duplicated().any():
        test_df = test_df.loc[:, ~test_df.columns.duplicated()]

    # Ordenação (ocorre APÓS o corte do debug, garantindo o mesmo comportamento do pipeline)
    if "UserID" in test_df.columns and "Time" in test_df.columns:
        test_df = test_df.sort_values(["UserID", "Time"]).reset_index(drop=True)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. CARREGAMENTO DOS ARTEFATOS (CORRIGIDO PARA OS NOVOS NOMES)
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    scores_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "scores.npy")
    indices_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "indices.npy")
    thresholds_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "user_thresholds.json")

    if not (os.path.exists(scores_path) and os.path.exists(indices_path)):
        print(f"❌ Erro: Arquivos .npy não encontrados em {args.output_dir}.")
        return

    scores = np.load(scores_path)
    indices = np.load(indices_path)
    
    with open(thresholds_path, "r") as f:
        thresholds = json.load(f)
    global_fallback = thresholds.get("__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__", 0.5)

    target_col = "Is_Anomaly" if "Is_Anomaly" in test_df.columns else "target"
    if target_col not in test_df.columns:
        print(f"❌ Erro Crítico: Coluna de gabarito '{target_col}' não encontrada!")
        return

    # Fallback de dedução matemática da janela (caso o YAML falhe)
    if seq_len is None:
        if len(indices) > 0:
            first_user = test_df.loc[indices[0], "UserID"]
            user_first_idx = test_df[test_df["UserID"] == first_user].index[0]
            seq_len = indices[0] - user_first_idx + 1
            print(f"🔍 YAML ausente. Janela deduzida matematicamente pelos índices: {seq_len}")
        else:
            seq_len = 10
            print("⚠️ Aviso: Usando fallback padrão (sequence_length = 10).")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. REPRODUÇÃO DA LÓGICA DE JANELAMENTO E AVALIAÇÃO
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    y_true_list = []
    for user_id, group in test_df.groupby("UserID"):
        if len(group) < seq_len:
            continue
        windows_labels = sliding_window_view(group[target_col].values, window_shape=seq_len)
        max_labels = np.max(windows_labels, axis=1)
        y_true_list.append(max_labels)

    if len(y_true_list) > 0:
        y_true = np.concatenate(y_true_list)
    else:
        y_true = np.array([])

    # SOLUÇÃO ROBUSTA: Reconstrução exata via índices em caso de desalinhamento
    if len(y_true) != len(scores):
        print(f"⚠️ Desalinhamento detectado (Rótulos: {len(y_true)} vs Scores: {len(scores)}).")
        print("🔧 Reconstruindo rótulos via mapeamento exato de índices...")
        y_true_reconstruido = []
        for idx in indices:
            start_idx = max(0, idx - seq_len + 1)
            window = test_df.loc[start_idx:idx, target_col].values
            y_true_reconstruido.append(np.max(window))
        y_true = np.array(y_true_reconstruido)
        print("✅ Alinhamento corrigido com sucesso!")

    # Aplicação dos limites
    user_mapping = test_df["UserID"].to_dict()
    test_users = [str(user_mapping.get(idx, "unknown")) for idx in indices]

    sample_thresholds = np.array([
        thresholds[u] if u in thresholds and u != "__GLOBAL_FALLBACK__" else global_fallback 
        for u in test_users
    ])
    
    y_pred = (scores > sample_thresholds).astype(int)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. AUDITORIA STATS
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    df_evaluated = test_df.loc[indices].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    df_evaluated["anomaly_score"] = scores
    df_evaluated["threshold_applied"] = sample_thresholds
    df_evaluated["predito_anomalo"] = y_pred

    status_list = []
    for real, pred in zip(y_true, y_pred):
        if real == 1 and pred == 1:
            status_list.append("Verdadeiro Positivo (TP)")
        elif real == 0 and pred == 1:
            status_list.append("Falso Positivo (FP)")
        elif real == 1 and pred == 0:
            status_list.append("Falso Negativo (FN)")
        else:
            status_list.append("Verdadeiro Negativo (TN)")
            
    df_evaluated["status_deteccao"] = status_list

    df_alertas = df_evaluated[df_evaluated["predito_anomalo"] == 1].copy()
    out_csv = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "anomaly_list.csv")
    df_alertas.to_csv(out_csv, index=False)
    
    tp = np.sum((y_pred == 1) & (y_true == 1))
    fp = np.sum((y_pred == 1) & (y_true == 0))
    fn = np.sum((y_pred == 0) & (y_true == 1))
    tn = np.sum((y_pred == 0) & (y_true == 0))

    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0
    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0
    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0.0

    precision_curve, recall_curve, _ = precision_recall_curve(y_true, scores)
    pr_auc = auc(recall_curve, precision_curve)

    print("\n" + "="*60)
    print("📊 RESUMO DA AUDITORIA DE DETECÇÃO (DOUBLE CHECK)")
    print("="*60)
    print(f" 🔹 Janelas Avaliadas            : {len(y_true)}")
    print(f" 🔹 Anomalias Reais no Gabarito  : {int(np.sum(y_true))}")
    print(f" 🔹 Alertas Disparados           : {len(df_alertas)}")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f" ✅ Verdadeiros Positivos (TP) : {tp}")
    print(f" ⚠️  Falsos Positivos (FP)     : {fp}")
    print(f" ❌ Falsos Negativos (FN)     : {fn}")
    print(f" 🛡️  Verdadeiros Negativos (TN) : {tn}")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f" 🎯 Precision (Precisão)      : {precision:.4f}")
    print(f" 🎯 Recall (Sensibilidade)    : {recall:.4f}")
    print(f" 🎯 F1-Score                  : {f1:.4f}")
    print(f" 🎯 PR-AUC                    : {pr_auc:.4f}")
    print("="*60)
    print(f"💾 Lista exportada com sucesso: {out_csv}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


################################################################################
# ARQUIVO: scripts\gini.py
################################################################################

import sys
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier
import os

# Adiciona o caminho do projeto ao sys.path (se ainda não estiver)
project_path = "data/output_thesis_marathon/synthetic/test/test.jsonl"  # ajuste se for outro
if project_path not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_path)

# Importa as classes do seu detector
from core.data.dataset_adapter import DatasetAdapter
from core.features.feature_processor import FeatureProcessor

# ----------------------------------------------------------
# 1. CARREGAR OS DADOS BRUTOS USANDO O DATASET ADAPTER
# ----------------------------------------------------------
# Cria uma configuração mínima para o adapter
config = {
    "data": {
        "train_path": "data/output_thesis_marathon/synthetic/train/train.jsonl",
        "val_path": "data/output_thesis_marathon/synthetic/val/val.jsonl",
        "test_path": "data/output_thesis_marathon/synthetic/test/test.jsonl",
        "target_col": "Is_Anomaly",
        "time_nan_strategy": "nan"
    },
    "sampling": {"mode": "full"},
    "features": {"enabled": True}
}

adapter = DatasetAdapter(config)
_, _, test_df = adapter.load()  # só precisamos do test_df

print(f"✅ Dados brutos carregados: {len(test_df)} eventos")

# ----------------------------------------------------------
# 2. PROCESSAR AS FEATURES (gera as 14 colunas)
# ----------------------------------------------------------
# Usa uma instância do FeatureProcessor com o mesmo config
processor = FeatureProcessor(config)

# O fit() precisa ser chamado sobre os dados de treino para aprender
# as distribuições históricas (LogHost_enc e ProcessName_enc)
# Vamos carregar o treino rapidamente para isso
train_df, _, _ = adapter.load()  # recarrega tudo; você pode otimizar depois
processor.fit(train_df)

# Aplica a transformação nos dados de teste
test_df_processed = processor.transform(test_df)

# Agora sim, as colunas de features existem
print("🔍 Colunas disponíveis após o FeatureProcessor:")
print(test_df_processed.columns.tolist())

# ----------------------------------------------------------
# 3. EXTRAIR AS 14 FEATURES E O ALVO
# ----------------------------------------------------------
features = [
    'hour_sin', 'hour_cos', 'hour_dev', 'out_of_hours',
    'event_z', 'fail_rate', 'rdp_rate', 'golden_ticket_score',
    'rare_process', 'rare_process_rate', 'admin_rate',
    'unique_hosts', 'LogHost_enc', 'ProcessName_enc'
]

target = 'Is_Anomaly'

# Verifica se todas as colunas existem
missing = [f for f in features if f not in test_df_processed.columns]
if missing:
    print(f"⚠️ Colunas faltando: {missing}")
    # Se alguma coluna estiver faltando, você pode preencher com 0
    for f in missing:
        test_df_processed[f] = 0

X = test_df_processed[features]
y = test_df_processed[target]

print(f"✅ Dados prontos: {len(X)} amostras, {X.shape[1]} features")

# ----------------------------------------------------------
# 4. MATRIZ DE CORRELAÇÃO (PEARSON)
# ----------------------------------------------------------
print("\n📊 Calculando Matriz de Correlação...")
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 10))
corr_matrix = X.corr(method='pearson')
sns.heatmap(corr_matrix, annot=True, cmap='coolwarm', fmt=".2f", square=True)
plt.title("Matriz de Correlação de Pearson - Atributos Comportamentais")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig("pearson_correlation.png", dpi=300)
print("✅ Gráfico 'pearson_correlation.png' salvo com sucesso!")
plt.show()

# ----------------------------------------------------------
# 5. IMPORTÂNCIA (GINI) COM RANDOM FOREST
# ----------------------------------------------------------
print("\n🌲 Treinando Random Forest...")
rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=100, random_state=42, n_jobs=-1)
rf.fit(X, y)

importances = rf.feature_importances_
indices = np.argsort(importances)[::-1]

plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
plt.title("Importância dos Atributos (Impureza de Gini)")
plt.bar(range(X.shape[1]), importances[indices], align="center", color='steelblue')
plt.xticks(range(X.shape[1]), [features[i] for i in indices], rotation=45, ha='right')
plt.ylabel("Escore de Importância Média")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig("gini_importance.png", dpi=300)
print("✅ Gráfico 'gini_importance.png' salvo com sucesso!")
plt.show()

# Exibe o ranking
print("\n📊 Ranking de Importância (Gini):")
for i in indices:
    print(f"  {features[i]}: {importances[i]:.4f}")

# ARQUIVO: scripts\inferir_usual.py
